[1] Benchmarking Multilingual Document Understanding in Vision-Language Models cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Matteo Gabburo, Daniela Baiamonte, Stefano Simonazzi, Silvia Gardini, Sean Cesare Lazzeri

TL;DR: 论文提出多语言文档理解基准 MDU，包含 13 类任务、5 种语言、23146 个样例，覆盖从文本/Markdown 抽取到摘要、分类、命名实体识别及布局敏感抽取等能力。作者在统一协议下评估了 45 个开源权重视觉语言模型和 1 个商业系统，发现基准远未饱和，且语言差距与任务能力差异不能仅靠模型规模扩大解决。

Details

Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型的文档理解评测多集中于英文文档和较窄任务，难以反映模型跨语言、任务、样式和布局的真实泛化能力，因此需要构建更全面的多语言文档理解基准。

Result: MDU 上最佳平均分为 69.0，45 个开源系统中仅 8 个达到 65；最佳两个结果来自 27B 模型而非最大模型。最强与最弱语言间差距不随模型规模扩大而缩小，且在控制语言领域构成后仍存在；各任务分数在控制整体模型质量后大多不相关，转录能力与语义抽取存在权衡。

Insight: 创新点在于构建多语言、多任务、布局敏感的文档理解基准，并系统评测大量不同规模 VLM，揭示规模扩展无法自动消除语言不平衡和任务能力不均衡。客观看，其价值主要在于诊断性评测设计和对“规模即全能”假设的反证，而非提出新模型。}

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform strongly on multimodal tasks, Document Understanding (DU) among them, but the benchmarks that measure them mostly cover English documents and narrow problems. How well current VLMs generalize across languages, tasks, styles and layouts therefore remains unclear. We introduce MDU, a multilingual DU benchmark of 23146 examples spanning 13 tasks and five languages: English, French, German, Italian, and Spanish. The tasks isolate complementary capabilities, from surface-level text and Markdown extraction through summarization, classification and named-entity recognition to layout-sensitive passage and layout extraction. We evaluate 45 state-of-the-art open-weight VLMs from 1.7B to 235B parameters, plus one commercial system under the same protocol. The benchmark is far from saturated: the best average is 69.0, only eight of the 45 evaluated systems reach 65, and the two best results come from 27B models rather than from the largest systems we evaluate. Performance is uneven in ways that scale does not fix. The gap between the strongest and weakest language does not close as models grow, and it survives controlling for the domain composition of each language. Capabilities also do not improve together: once we partial out overall model quality, per-task scores are largely uncorrelated, and transcription ability in particular trades off against semantic extraction.

[2] An LLM Is All You Need: Data-Efficient Adaptation to Full-Duplex, Word-Level Spoken Dialogue cs.CL | cs.SD | eess.ASPDF

Tairan Chen, Richard Yucheng He, Yihang Liu, Baodong Cao, Chen Xu

TL;DR: 论文提出一种数据高效的全双工口语对话适配方法，将冻结的通用多模态LLM改造成词级全双工系统，采用80ms帧时钟的thinker-talker级联，仅训练轻量适配器和少量新token嵌入。thinker逐帧输出一个文本token，并将停顿、让出话轮、开始说话等表示为同词表token，由有限状态机约束合法性；talker仅读取离散token，二者可独立训练。通过四阶段课程学习，约30 GPU小时完成转换，在Full-Duplex-Bench和InteractionBench上与大规模语音训练的开源全双工系统竞争。

Details

Motivation: 传统全双工口语对话依赖数十万至数百万小时语音训练，成本高，且会重写提供知识的语言模型，话轮转换往往隐式或粗粒度而非词级。论文希望用少量数据将冻结的通用多模态LLM适配为词级全双工系统，降低训练成本并保留LLM知识。

Result: 在Full-Duplex-Bench和InteractionBench上，该系统与通过大规模语音训练构建的开源全双工系统具有竞争力；在用户仅停顿时最不容易抢话、backchannel最多，并在口语任务准确率、有据性和长对话连贯性上领先。摘要未给出具体数值。

Insight: 宣称创新包括冻结LLM仅训练97M参数适配器、用同词表token表示话轮控制并由FSM保证合法、四阶段课程和thinker-talker独立训练。客观看，将对话控制离散化为词级token并与冻结LLM结合，是以较小数据和算力实现细粒度全双工交互的可借鉴思路。

Abstract: Full-duplex spoken dialogue, in which a system listens while it speaks and can backchannel, yield the floor, and be interrupted, is usually obtained by training on hundreds of thousands to millions of hours of speech. This is costly, rewrites the language model that supplies the system’s knowledge, and still leaves turn-taking either implicit or decided coarsely rather than word by word. We show that a frozen, general-purpose multimodal LLM can instead be adapted into a word-level full-duplex system with little data. Our system is a thinker-talker cascade on an 80 ms frame clock. The thinker keeps the LLM frozen and trains only lightweight adapters and a few new token embeddings (97M parameters); at every frame it emits one token of agent text, with pauses, yields, and onsets represented as tokens of the same vocabulary and kept legal by a finite-state machine. A four-stage curriculum, each stage anchored to the model’s own behavior under the previous format, performs the conversion in about 30 GPU-hours. The talker reads only the thinker’s discrete tokens, so the two halves are trained independently. On Full-Duplex-Bench and InteractionBench, the system is competitive with open-source full-duplex systems built through large-scale speech training: among them, it is the least likely to take the floor when the user merely pauses, backchannels most often, and leads in spoken-task accuracy, groundedness, and coherence over long conversations.

[3] LLMs and the Homogenization of Teaching cs.CLPDF

Mei Tan, Hyunji Nam, Hari Subramonyam, Dorottya Demszky

TL;DR: 论文研究大语言模型在教育内容生成中的同质化问题，比较前沿与开放权重模型在数学题目和作文反馈两类任务上与人类作者内容的多样性差异，并从词汇、语义和领域教学形式等维度评估直接提示、多样性提示和后训练的效果。结果表明，模型输出显著比人类内容更同质，且现有提升多样性的方法多停留在表层特征，未能真正拓宽教学形式与思维空间。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注LLM在开放式文本生成中缺乏多样性，尤其在教育场景中可能压缩学生被邀请进行的思考方式和学科活动，因此需要系统评估教育生成任务的同质化程度及其成因。

Result: 在数学题目和作文反馈任务上，直接提示的模型输出相比人类内容在词汇相似度Self-BLEU上高78%、语义嵌入相似度上高51%，且教学形式范围更窄；多样性提示和后训练虽有改善，但主要增加表面变化，未显著拓宽教学 repertoire。

Insight: 创新点在于从教育领域出发，用词汇、语义和领域教学形式多维指标比较模型与人类作者分布，并揭示模型会继承并放大人类教学中最规范化的惯例；客观看，其价值在于指出教育LLM多样性不足不仅是生成表层问题，更涉及教学数据与教师专业判断。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are known to lack diversity in open-ended text generation, a limitation that is especially consequential in education, where homogeneity can narrow the kinds of thinking and disciplinary work students are invited to do. We study diversity in two educational generation tasks: math items and essay feedback. We compare frontier and open-weight models with human-authored reference distributions using lexical, semantic, and domain-grounded measures. For math, we examine the mathematical activities students are asked to perform; for feedback, we examine the revision work requested and the rhetorical strategies used to communicate it. We evaluate direct prompting, two diversity prompting methods, and post-training. Across both tasks, directly prompted model outputs are markedly less diverse than human-authored content–78% higher on lexical (Self-BLEU) and 51% higher on semantic (embedding) similarity–and represent a narrower range of instructional forms. Diversity prompting and post-training increase variation largely through surface features without broadening the instructional repertoire. Models inherit and amplify the most normative routines of human instruction. Overcoming mode collapse in education will require richer instructional data and investment in teachers’ creative capacity and professional judgment.

[4] Do Large Language Models Show Racial and Sex Bias in Pain Management? A Counterfactual Audit of GPT, Claude, Gemini, Llama, DeepSeek and Qwen cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.CYPDF

Richard J. Young, Alice M. Matthews

TL;DR: 论文对17个LLM的24种配置及4个开放权重模型进行反事实审计，考察急性和Q-Pain疼痛场景中种族/性别是否影响疼痛管理与阿片处方。结果显示校正后未发现稳定偏见，疼痛严重度差异基本在0-10量表1分内等价于零；模型间和配置间差异远大于患者身份差异。

Details

Motivation: 少数族裔患者疼痛治疗不足，作为医疗AI的LLM可能复制偏见；既往审计多只测少数闭源模型、显式标注种族且少测模型自身响应不稳定性。

Result: Study1无差异经校正存活；Study2的1386个比较无经FDR存活，疼痛严重度差异均在1分内（Q-Pain的237/238在0.25分内）；阿片决策精度不足不能排除约20个百分点差异，故为未检出而非证明无差异；少数存活差异反而偏向黑人患者，且模型/配置差异远大于身份差异（参考患者高阿片剂量比例6%-93%）。

Insight: 创新在于多名字线索、同病例同种子配对、多模型多配置与推理/精度设置、报告噪声下限并按部署配置审计；客观启示是临床AI公平性审计应重视模型与配置方差和测量精度，而非仅看群体平均差异。

Abstract: Racial and ethnic minority patients receive less pain treatment than non-Hispanic White patients, and large language models (LLMs) used as medical AI could reproduce that bias. Prior audits tested a few proprietary models, stated race explicitly and rarely measured the models’ own response instability. In exploratory, non-preregistered analyses, Study 1 had four open-weight LLMs assess six acute-pain vignettes in every combination of four races and two sexes, with identity stated or cued only by a name (two names per group), comparing each identity with a White male reference on the same case and sampling seed. In Study 2, 24 configurations of 17 models, including OpenAI’s GPT-4.1 mini and gpt-oss, Anthropic’s Claude Haiku 4.5, Google’s Gemini 2.5 Flash, Meta’s Llama, DeepSeek and Qwen, with reasoning on and off and at two precisions, answered the same vignettes and the 50 clinician-written Q-Pain cases. In this single-prompt, JSON-constrained task, no difference survived correction in Study 1, none of 1,386 comparisons survived the false discovery rate in Study 2, and every pain-severity difference was equivalent to zero within one point on a 0-10 scale (within a quarter point for 237 of 238 Q-Pain comparisons). Opioid decisions were bounded less precisely (Study 1 could not exclude differences of about 20 percentage points), so they show no detected difference, not proof of none. The few differences surviving correction within a model favoured Black patients. Models differed far more from one another and across their own settings than between patients (the reference patient received the high opioid dose in 6% to 93% of responses), although serving software, provider and prompt format also differed. Fairness audits of clinical AI should use several names per group, pair comparisons on the seed, report the noise floor and test each deployed configuration.

[5] CoLFA: Collaboration-Guided Lightweight Failure Attribution for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems cs.CLPDF

Yufei Luo, Yuchen Ling, Chunrong Fang

TL;DR: 论文提出CoLFA，一种面向LLM多智能体系统的协作引导轻量级故障归因方法。该方法利用智能体交接和内容传递等协作线索，组织需要检查的步骤与内容呈现，从而在长日志中定位导致失败的关键步骤和负责智能体。实验表明其在多个设置下同时实现更高定位准确率和更低token消耗。

Details

Motivation: 多智能体系统故障归因需要从交错步骤和杂乱内容的长记录中追踪错误形成，现有方法难以兼顾步骤定位准确率与token成本。论文希望利用协作过程留下的执行连接和内容传递记录作为归因证据，减少无关和重复信息处理。

Result: 在Who&When和LongRCA的五个设置及三个骨干模型上，CoLFA取得最高精确步骤定位准确率和最低token消耗；相比各设置中的单独基线，准确率最高提升37.50%，token消耗最高降低97.6%。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点是通过协作线索组织归因输入，并设计Handoff-Guided Step Expansion与Cross-Referenced Content Organization，使模型聚焦错误形成及每个决策的作用。客观来看，其创新主要在于将多智能体协作结构作为轻量级归因证据，而非简单检索全部日志，从而同时优化定位精度和计算开销。

Abstract: Failure attribution in multi-agent systems requires tracing error formation to identify the decisive error step and the responsible agent. However, agents’ division of work and information exchange lead to Interleaved Steps and Cluttered Content, requiring models to link executions and check details in lengthy records. This makes it difficult to balance localization accuracy and token cost. We find that multi-agent collaboration also leaves records of how executions connect and content passes between them as results are formed, providing evidence for tracing error formation. Based on this observation, we propose CoLFA (Collaboration-Guided Lightweight Failure Attribution), which uses collaboration cues to organize the attribution input according to how results are formed, determining which steps are examined and how their content is presented. Through Handoff-Guided Step Expansion and Cross-Referenced Content Organization, CoLFA helps the model focus on examining error formation and the role of each decision while reducing the processing of irrelevant and repeated records. Across five settings spanning Who&When and LongRCA and three backbone models, CoLFA achieves the highest exact step-localization accuracy with the lowest token consumption. Compared with individual baselines in each setting, CoLFA improves accuracy by up to 37.50% and reduces token consumption by up to 97.6%.

[6] A Time-Frequency-Condition Multimodal Fusion Foundation Model for Universal Rotating-Machinery Fault Diagnosis under Multi-Element Heterogeneous Scenarios cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Xinyu Zou, Jian Ma, Liang Ma, Chen Lu, Tao Peng

TL;DR: 论文提出 TFC-MMFD，一种面向多元素异构场景下旋转机械故障诊断的时频-工况多模态融合基础模型。该方法将统计指标、时频图像、振动序列以及机器与工况文本描述统一分词到类语言空间，并借助 LLaMA 主干、跨模态双向注意力和 Q-Former 得到故障模式查询，再与自然语言故障模式锚点匹配完成诊断。模型还引入回归对抗式工况解耦和工况条件掩码预训练，以提升对变化工况的鲁棒性。

Details

Motivation: 传统故障诊断通常采用一机一模型范式，新机器或新工况需重新训练，扩展性差；迁移学习和域泛化虽能减少标注负担，但主要追求域不变共性，可能丢弃对诊断关键的机器特有特征。

Result: 在覆盖多种轴承类型、转速和负载的十个公开轴承数据集、四个渐进任务上，TFC-MMFD 达到 99.40% 准确率和 99.16% macro-F1；在多数插值未见工况下无适配仍保持超过 80% 准确率；在仅每类五个标注样本的未见机器上，准确率从 20.26% 提升到 95.63%。其比八个域泛化基线中最强方法高 10.57 个百分点，并以不到一半的微调数据与近期轴承基础模型相比具有优势。

Insight: 创新点在于将多物理来源信息统一为类语言序列，用基础模型式架构融合时频、统计、序列和文本模态，并以自然语言故障模式锚点匹配替代固定类别索引；同时结合工况解耦与条件掩码预训练增强跨工况泛化。客观看，其可借鉴之处是面向异构故障诊断的多模态统一建模和条件感知预训练范式。

Abstract: Intelligent fault diagnosis models are typically developed for a single machine under a specific set of operating conditions, such that each new machine or condition requires retraining from scratch. This one-machine-one-model paradigm scales poorly. Although transfer learning and domain generalization can reduce the labeling burden, they primarily seek domain-invariant commonality, potentially discarding machine-specific characteristics that are critical for diagnosis. This paper proposes TFC-MMFD, a time-frequency-condition multimodal fusion foundation model for rotating-machinery fault diagnosis under multi-element heterogeneous scenarios. Statistical indicators, time-frequency images, vibration sequences, and textual machine and condition descriptions are tokenized into a unified language-like space, allowing inputs of different physical origin to be represented as a single sequence. A LLaMA backbone, together with cross-modal bidirectional attention and a Q-Former, then distills this sequence into fault-mode queries. Diagnosis is performed by matching these queries against natural-language fault-mode anchors rather than fixed class indices, while regression-adversarial condition disentanglement and condition-conditioned masked pre-training improve robustness to changing conditions. Across four progressive tasks on ten public bearing datasets spanning diverse bearing types, speeds, and loads, TFC-MMFD attains 99.40% accuracy and 99.16% macro-F1, maintains over 80% accuracy on most interpolated unseen conditions without adaptation, and improves from 20.26% to 95.63% accuracy on an unseen machine with only five labeled samples per class. It outperforms the strongest of eight domain-generalization baselines by 10.57 percentage points and compares favorably with a recent bearing foundation model while using less than half of its fine-tuning data.

[7] A Closer Look at Language Model Introspection: Probing Information and Reporting cs.CLPDF

Dustin Rubin, Vincenzo Timmel, Xufeng He, Aritra Saha, JooMan Kim

TL;DR: 论文重新审视语言模型内省能力：通过线性探针从模型激活中解码被扰动句子的位置，并与模型自身零样本报告结果对比，发现位置信息在激活中高度可解码，甚至随机初始化网络中也存在，而模型报告能力可能远弱于探针。作者据此指出，模型能报告扰动位置并不意味着其具备更广泛、忠实的内部计算访问能力。

Details

Motivation: 现有研究常以模型能否识别自身激活被扰动的位置来评估内省能力，但若该信息本身已容易被简单线性分类器恢复，则成功报告可能低估任务难度，不能代表模型能忠实解释推理或评估答案可靠性。

Result: 在OLMo 3 32B中，第0层注入4倍典型激活范数、第24层探针读取时，监督探针在8.4B训练token后达98.3%准确率，而模型零样本答案仅为随机水平11.1%；在发布的基础检查点下，二者分别达99.1%和89.6%。此外，7B和32B模型在随机初始化时位置信息已可解码。

Insight: 创新点在于将“信息是否可从激活中解码”与“模型是否能报告该信息”分离，用线性探针作为基线重新界定内省任务难度，并跨预训练阶段和随机初始化分析信息可及性。客观看，其价值在于提醒社区：报告成功不等同于深层内省或对内部计算的广泛访问。

Abstract: One way to study language-model introspection is to test whether models can identify where their activations have been perturbed. We train a simple linear classifier to identify the perturbed sentence from the model’s activations, then compare its predictions with the model’s own answers across pretraining and injection strengths. This tests whether the model is being asked to report information that is already easy to recover. If so, successful localization may be less demanding than it first appears and may not extend to the broader uses that motivate introspection research, such as faithfully explaining a model’s reasoning or helping researchers assess the reliability of its answers. With an injection four times the typical activation norm at layer 0 and probe readout at layer 24 in OLMo~3 32B, the supervised probe reaches 98.3% accuracy after 8.4 billion training tokens, while the model’s zero-shot answer remains at chance (11.1%). At the released base checkpoint, these accuracies reach 99.1% and 89.6%, respectively. Across 7B and 32B, location is already decodable in randomly initialized networks, showing that the architecture can support these traces without pretraining. In these experiments, location information lies close enough to the surface for a simple linear probe to recover it, so successful reporting need not imply broader access to the model’s internal computation.

[8] Knowledge in the Harness, Reliability in the Weights: Teacher-Free Co-Evolution of an LLM Agent cs.CL | cs.AI | cs.LGPDF

Yujia Zhang, Xinqi Ren, Shixiong Zhou, Jia Meng

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需更强教师模型的LLM智能体自进化框架，让小开源模型（Gemma 4 12B）仅凭自身部署日志和参考结果，同时弥合“策略知识缺失”和“执行不一致”两个差距。方法将领域策略写为可按需调用的技能规则，并经代码检查、反例自审、配对重测、噪声校准验证门筛选后固化；再用GRPO在多轮工具调用对话上训练LoRA适配器，使“知识在规则（harness）、可靠性在权重（weights）”协同进化。

Details

Motivation: 小型开源模型适合私有客服部署，但落后于前沿模型：既抓不住书面策略在具体情境下的要求，又在已知知识上表现不一致。论文希望在无强教师模型条件下，仅用部署日志及其参考结果修复这两类缺陷。

Result: 在τ²-bench的零售与航空域、未经官方任务训练或筛选，两轮后较起点提升12.5/19.5个pass^1点和18.4/22.0个pass^4点；并超过同模型全策略置入系统提示的参考智能体11.8/8.0（pass^1）和22.8/16.0（pass^4）点，缩小了与三个前沿模型的大部分差距。2×2消融显示规则与训练权重互补。

Insight: 宣称创新在于无需教师模型的自进化闭环，以及将策略知识外化为可审计技能规则并配多级验证门。客观看，其价值在于把“知识”与“可靠性”解耦：规则救回从未解决的任务并承载RL上下文无法内化的知识，权重则让已解任务稳定，这种分工与验证机制值得借鉴。

Abstract: Small open models suit private customer-service deployments but trail frontier models in two ways: they miss what the written policy requires in a given situation, and they act inconsistently on what they know. We study whether such a model can close both gaps from its own deployment logs and their reference outcomes, without a stronger teacher model. Our starting point is a Gemma 4 12B agent that reads its domain policy from on-demand skills rather than from its system prompt. In each round, the deployed system serves a fresh batch of tasks; the base model diagnoses failed conversations and writes rules into the skills, and a rule survives only if it passes code checks, counterexample self-review, a paired retest, and a noise-calibrated validation gate. Reinforcement learning (GRPO) then trains a LoRA adapter on complete multi-turn, tool-using conversations. [9] Janus-MOPD: Tracing the Student Learning Dynamics in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation cs.CLPDF

TL;DR: 论文关注多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）中学生难以同时获得多领域能力的问题，提出Janus-MOPD框架，从学生内部几何表示的学习动态出发进行追踪。作者发现学生在时间维度呈现“扩张—巩固—优化”（ECO）三阶段动态，在空间维度存在域内与跨域的子空间锁定，并据此给出统一的学习动态形式化、几何追踪方法和系统性发现。

Abstract: Multi-domain capabilities are critical for practically applicable Large Reasoning Models (LRMs). Therefore, Multi-teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD) has become a crucial part of the post-training process, integrating the capabilities of multiple domain-specific teacher models into a single student model. However, the student usually fails to acquire multi-domain capabilities simultaneously due to undesirable domain heterogeneity. Existing works have address this problem in view of temporal uneven efficiency and spatial hard allocation in external multi-teacher supervision. While in broader multi-domain training, varying degrees of performance degradation is still largely unexplained. In this paper, we shift the focus on how the student learns and integrates multi-domain capabilities internally, and then propose \textbf{\textsc{Janus-MOPD}}, a general learning dynamics framework of geometric representations for LRMs. Specifically, our main focus is on the internal performance of multi-domain capabilities on both temporal and spatial dimensions within the student. Temporally, our study shows that the student exhibits a Expansion-Consolidation-Optimization (ECO) three-phase learning dynamics, together with inherent limitations in learning efficiency and scaling. Spatially, we summarize two types of subspace locking within and across domains by tracing the representation space of the student, while explaining its clear learning directions but inherent cross-domain conflict. Therefore, \textsc{Janus-MOPD} yields a unified learning dynamics formulation, a geometric tracing methodology, and systematic findings of how the student learns and integrates multi-domain capabilities. Overall, we provide a \textsc{Janus} perspective to internally trace the continual evolution of the multi-domain capabilities within the model itself.

Details

Motivation: 现有监督微调在推理轨迹远长于最终答案时会导致答案学习信号被稀释；而 GRPO 等强化学习方法存在奖励设计成本高、性能平台期和奖励黑客等问题。

Abstract: In the legal domain, LLMs can improve reliability and interpretability by generating explicit reasoning traces. However, training LLMs to produce high-quality reasoning traces remains challenging. Existing supervised fine-tuning methods struggle when reasoning traces are significantly longer than final answers, as the learning signal for the answer becomes diluted. Meanwhile, reinforcement learning methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization also have several limitations, including costly reward design, performance plateaus, and reward hacking. We therefore propose a two-stage training framework. In Stage I, JurisCoT-SFT employs a length-normalized dual objective to balance learning signals between reasoning traces and final answers. In Stage II, Lifecycle-Aware Backtrackable Policy Optimization dynamically evaluates and retires auxiliary reward signals based on their impact on final evaluation objectives, enabling efficient reward utilization. Our framework substantially improves legal reasoning performance on relevant tasks selected from multiple legal benchmarks, including ChJGBench, LawBench, and LegalBench. Experimental results demonstrate consistent improvements in both reasoning trace quality and final answer accuracy, while our models remain competitive with larger models.

Abstract: LLM-based recommendation agents can search catalogs, inspect products, ask users, and revise rejected recommendations. However, these behaviors are often governed by hand-designed workflows, while existing training methods optimize isolated components such as query rewriting or list ranking rather than the interaction policy as a whole. Abstract: Retrieval-augment generation is a prevalent strategy to mitigate hallucinations of LLMs. The attributable RAG (RAGQ) generates quotes for its answers. However, existing RAGQs exhibit significant degradation when dealing with questions that require multi-hop reasoning and multi-modal understanding, suffering from over-citation, implicit entity identification failure, and poor generalization. In this paper, we propose a novel RAGQ framework, namely QDRAG. QDRAG breaks down the input question into atomic subquestions to identify the implicit entities. Details

Motivation: 基准分数常被当作模型能力的代理，论文反向追问性能能否被可信地节流到指定分数，以及失败模式是否像自然弱模型，从而检验分数控制与真实能力退化之间的差异。

Abstract: A benchmark score is commonly read as a proxy for model capability. We study the inverse problem: can a model be driven to a target score, and do its failures resemble those of a naturally weaker model? We operationalize believable performance reduction along three axes: target calibration, item-difficulty structure, and error-distribution similarity to a real weak reference. We introduce CapLadder, an IRT-calibrated suite of 1,985 items spanning mathematics, code, multiple-choice reasoning, and tool use. A simple weak-teacher LoRA dial reaches unseen target levels on reasoning with a 1.9-point held-out calibration error, whereas prompting, temperature, and weight noise cannot combine comparable range and precision. Yet on multiple choice, the dial converges to an answer-token attractor: up to 97% of its newly introduced errors land on one letter, with the attractor changing from D in Qwen to A in Llama. Logit interventions identify a dominant near-constant shift: subtracting the estimated shift on held-out items recovers 52 to 59% of lost accuracy and removes the dominant attractor, while option permutation confirms that the bias tracks token position rather than option content. With high inter-annotator agreement ($κ=0.94$), human-coded error-category distributions nonetheless match the weak reference on reasoning (JS = 0.00), showing that local plausibility and aggregate detectability differ. Structured tool calls exhibit more natural degradation in the Qwen family (JS = 0.09), while a globally calibrated dial transfers poorly across benchmarks (14.2-point error on two usable benchmarks). Precise score control, therefore, does not imply a portable or believable reduction in skill.

Details

Motivation: 现有基准多关注总体准确率，难以全面刻画 System One 模型的能力广度与决策可靠性，因此需要更系统、细粒度且贴近交互场景的评估方法。

Abstract: System One models promise fast, general-purpose decision-making without lengthy token generation. However, understanding their capability spectrum and decision reliability requires more than aggregate performance on current benchmarks. [16] ShadowBench: Exposing Lexical Anchoring and the Illusion of Forgetting in Large Language Models cs.CL | cs.LGPDF

Sujan Maharjan, Lu Liu

TL;DR: 论文提出ShadowBench，通过Dual-Trait Association任务评估LLM在缺少显式实体名时的潜在实体关联能力，发现知识检索存在词法锚定和普遍的“Shadow Gap”，移除名称线索后性能下降超20%。进一步将该基准用于机器遗忘评估并提出LELR指标，揭示当前遗忘算法仅实现表面词法擦除，形成“遗忘幻觉”，并非可靠的参数级知识删除。

Details

Motivation: 当前LLM事实检索评估几乎都依赖显式实体名，可能高估模型真实知识能力；同时机器遗忘可能只是过滤输出而非删除内部知识，需要量化无名称线索下的知识检索与残留泄漏。

Result: 所有规模模型包括GPT-5.4和Claude-Sonnet-4.6均出现Shadow Gap，移除词法锚点导致性能下降超过20%。机器遗忘算法如Gradient Difference、NPO仅实现表面擦除；热门实体在内部推理中重构“遗忘”实体的LELR超过74%，小众实体虽LELR为0%，但通过隐式属性关联仍保持60-90%的shadow accuracy。

Insight: 创新点在于提出抗捷径的ShadowBench和DTA任务来量化潜在实体关联，并定义LELR揭示“遗忘幻觉”。客观看，论文强调词法锚定是LLM知识访问的关键瓶颈，也说明遗忘评估应关注内部推理与隐式属性关联，而不能只看直接查询输出。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as primary interfaces for factual retrieval, yet current evaluation paradigms rely almost exclusively on explicit entity names. In this work, we demonstrate that LLM knowledge is fundamentally lexically anchored: models possess extensive factual information but find it significantly difficult to retrieve without explicit name tokens. To quantify this limitation, we introduce ShadowBench, a rigorously hardened, shortcut-resistant benchmark designed to evaluate Latent Entity Association via a novel Dual-Trait Association (DTA) task. Our evaluation reveals a pervasive “Shadow Gap” across all model scales$\unicode{x2013}$up to the frontier models GPT-5.4 and Claude-Sonnet-4.6$\unicode{x2013}$where removing lexical anchors causes performance drops of over 20%. Furthermore, we demonstrate that this lexical dependency exposes a critical vulnerability in AI safety. Applying ShadowBench to Machine Unlearning, we find that state-of-the-art algorithms (e.g., Gradient Difference, NPO) achieve only superficial lexical erasure. While unlearned models fail on direct queries, our novel Latent Entity Leakage Rate (LELR) metric reveals that for popular entities, models explicitly reconstruct the “forgotten” entity in their internal reasoning traces in over 74% of cases, utilizing residual shadow knowledge to solve associative tasks. Conversely, for niche entities, models retain high shadow accuracy (60-90%) via implicit attribute correlation despite 0% LELR. Ultimately, ShadowBench indicates that current unlearning paradigms create an “Illusion of Forgetting,” demonstrating that existing methods act as superficial output filters and are not yet reliable for achieving true parametric erasure.

[17] Privileged Information Does Not Guarantee Privileged Guidance: Auditing On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Models cs.CLPDF

Haoyi Qiu, Akshat Agarwal

TL;DR: 论文审计了在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中“特权信息是否真正转化为有效指导”的问题：以完整解题过程作为教师模型可见、学生模型不可见的特权信息，在数学任务和三个规模 Qwen3 上分来源、编码、传输、表达四阶段评估。结果显示教师虽能用解题过程解决难题并纠正学生错误，但其下一词元目标并不稳定偏向正确轨迹，学生学到的纠正也未必能在无解题过程时表达。最大收益来自思考模式教师监督非思考模式学生，问题-only 教师常可匹配或超过带解题过程教师，作者称其为“推理套利”。

Details

Motivation: OPSD 通常假设：教师模型获得解题过程等特权信息后，其监督能让学生获得相应推理能力；但学生表现提升是否真正反映特权信息仍不清楚。论文希望审计特权信息从教师到学生的各环节是否有效传递。

Result: 在数学任务和三个 Qwen3 规模上，教师带解题过程可解决难题并从学生错误中恢复，但不能可靠识别错误，且 token 目标不偏向正确轨迹；重复训练更多是提高学生能力范围内成功频率，而非解锁新能力。最大增益出现在思考模式教师监督非思考模式学生时，问题-only 教师常匹配或超过带解题过程教师；未报告具体数值或 SOTA。

Insight: 创新点在于提出来源、编码、传输、表达四阶段审计框架，系统检验特权信息是否真正转化为监督信号和学生行为；并发现收益更多跟随教师思考模式而非特权解题过程，即“推理套利”。客观看，其价值在于强调需用受控对比衡量特权信息相对同等训练的增量，而非仅看准确率提升。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on next-token targets from a teacher, a frozen copy of the student that additionally receives privileged information, such as a worked solution, hidden from the student. Whether student gains reflect that information remains unclear. Taking the worked solution as privileged information, we audit four stages across three Qwen3 scales on mathematics: Source, whether the teacher can use the solution; Encoding, whether the resulting token targets are useful; Transmission, whether the student learns from the targets; and Expression, whether the student applies the learned behavior without the solution. With the solution, the teacher solves hard problems and recovers from student errors, yet the teacher does not reliably recognize errors, and its token targets do not favor correct trajectories. Repeated training makes successes within the student’s reach more frequent rather than unlocking new ones, and corrections the student learns to predict are not reliably expressed in free generation. To isolate the solution’s contribution, we vary its availability separately from teacher and student thinking modes. The largest gains occur when a teacher in thinking mode supervises a student trained and evaluated without an explicit thinking trace, and problem-only teachers often match or exceed privileged-solution teachers: the gain tracks the teacher’s thinking mode rather than the solution, a pattern we call reasoning arbitrage. Accuracy gains alone therefore do not establish transfer of reasoning from privileged solutions: bottlenecks lie after Source, across Encoding, Transmission, and Expression. Future evaluations must measure what the solution adds to otherwise identical training.

[18] Show Your Work in Swahili: Reinforcement Learning for Native-Language Mathematical Reasoning cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Jessica Lundin, Katherine Rosenfeld, David Adelani, Geoffrey Mosoti Nyakiongora, Halfan Ngowo

TL;DR: 论文提出 Fikiria，一个面向斯瓦希里语数学推理的两阶段 SFT+GRPO 训练方案。其奖励函数先经过格式门控，再结合答案准确率与斯瓦希里语推理相对语言内参考思维链的 ChrF++ 相似度，以同时约束最终答案和推理过程的语言表面保真度。作者用 GPT-4.1 翻译 GSM8K 并经 SSA-COMET 过滤构建 2.3k 斯瓦希里语数学题数据，在 AfriMGSM 斯瓦希里语上取得 79.2% 的结果。

Details

Motivation: 关注低资源语言数学推理：不仅要求模型给出正确答案，还希望其推理过程以目标语言（斯瓦希里语）表达，并贴近语言内参考思维链。

Result: Fikiria-AfriqueQwen-14B 在 AfriMGSM Swahili 上达到 79.2%；在相同固定提示和严格答案格式评分下，GPT-4o 为 68.0%，部分因其常不符合答案格式。IrokoBench 报告的 GPT-4o 78.8% 使用最优提示，数值接近但不可直接比较；小规模人工评估中 GPT-4o 输出整体更受偏好。

Insight: 创新点在于将格式门控、答案准确率和目标语言推理轨迹的 ChrF++ 相似度组合为 GRPO 奖励，从而显式优化“用斯瓦希里语展示推理过程”的表面保真度。客观看，这为低资源语言推理提供了可操作的奖励设计，但 ChrF++ 只衡量表面相似而非单步推理有效性，且人工评估未占优，说明语言保真与推理质量仍有差距。

Abstract: We present Fikiria, a two-stage SFT+GRPO recipe for Swahili mathematical reasoning. Its reward multiplies a format gate by a weighted sum of answer accuracy and the ChrF\texttt{++} similarity of the Swahili reasoning to an in-language reference chain of thought, so that training targets the surface fidelity of the reasoning trace and not only the final answer. We train on a 2.3k-problem Swahili dataset created by translating GSM8K with GPT-4.1 and filtering with SSA-COMET. On AfriMGSM Swahili, Fikiria-AfriqueQwen-14B reaches 79.2%. Under the same fixed prompt and strict answer-format scoring, GPT-4o reaches 68.0%, partly because it often fails the required answer format; IrokoBench reports 78.8% for GPT-4o using a best-per-model prompt, a figure numerically close to ours but not directly comparable. In a small human evaluation with co-author annotators, GPT-4o’s outputs were preferred overall. ChrF\texttt{++} measures surface fidelity, not the validity of individual reasoning steps.

[19] Indic-Task Gains and Regressions after Reinforcement Learning cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Sutanshu Saksena Raj, Daksh Maan, Avinash Benki, Parijat Chatterjee, Pasunuti Prasanjith

TL;DR: 论文从一个覆盖英语和八种印度语言的30B-A3B混合专家监督微调模型出发，用GRPO在四个环境中进行强化学习，并在34个基准上统一比较RL前后的表现。结果显示数学能力大幅提升、多数印度语言基准也有提升，但部分印度语言问答显著退化且模型更倾向输出英文；第二次加入印度语言环境及语言/重复奖励后，篇章问答和指令遵循改善且保持数学表现，但推理和编程退步，书写系统合规仍未解决。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注强化学习对多语言模型印度语言任务是普遍增益还是伴随回归，并强调即使其他基准分数提升，印度语言篇章问答和书写系统合规也需要被直接评估。

Result: MATH提升36.07分，多数Indic基准提升；但XQuAD-Indic F1下降19.32分，英文答案比例从8.7%升至91.7%，IndicQA下降8.64分，而English XQuAD提升11.71 F1。第二次RL改善了篇章问答和指令遵循并保持MATH，但在推理和编程基准上退步，且语言奖励未确保使用请求的书写系统。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于用一次统一评测揭示RL后印度语言任务“增益与回归并存”，并指出语言奖励不等于书写系统合规，相关能力需直接评测。客观看，其价值主要是系统性的多基准诊断和回归发现，但多个训练选择同时改变，无法隔离各奖励项的具体作用。

Abstract: We run reinforcement learning (RL) with GRPO across four environments, starting from a supervised fine-tuned 30B-A3B mixture-of-experts model covering English and eight Indic languages. We compare the model before and after RL across 34 benchmarks in one evaluation campaign. MATH improves by 36.07 points, and most Indic benchmarks also improve. Yet XQuAD-Indic F1 falls 19.32 points as English answers rise from 8.7% to 91.7%; IndicQA falls 8.64, while English XQuAD gains 11.71 F1. A second RL run from the same supervised initialization adds an Indic environment together with language and repetition rewards. Compared with the released model, it improves both passage-answering and instruction-following benchmarks, along with preserving MATH performance. But it loses ground on reasoning and coding benchmarks. Several training choices changed together, so we cannot isolate the effect of any one of the reward terms. Script compliance remains unresolved. Adding a language reward did not ensure use of the requested script. Passage answering and script compliance therefore need direct evaluation, even when other benchmark scores improve.

[20] Label-Efficient Document-Grounded Factuality Detection via Cross-Paraphrastic Invariance Learning cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Chao Chen, Shanshan Lin, Dongsheng Hong, Sibo Ju, Wenjie Yang

TL;DR: 论文提出CPIL，一种两阶段孪生框架，通过跨复述不变性学习将少量标注数据转化为关系监督，用于文档 grounded 事实性检测。它利用同一实例的不同保标签复述视图构造正对，并利用同源文档的异标签样本构造负对，再结合分位数感知半困难负采样训练编码器和分类头。推理时无需复述生成、样本对构造或孪生比较，仅需一次编码器-分类器前向。在11任务LLM-AggreFact基准上，仅用2-3%标注池即取得81.04/81.60 macro-F1，优于对比的紧凑检测器和两个LLM参考。

Details

Motivation: LLM会生成源文档不支持的流畅声明，因此需要准确高效的文档 grounded 事实性检测。现有LLM评估器需重复推理、计算成本高；紧凑检测器推理高效但依赖大量标注或合成语料。论文旨在低标注成本下实现高效且准确的事实性检测。

Result: 在11-task LLM-AggreFact基准上，CPIL-2%和CPIL-3%分别达到81.04和81.60 macro-F1，仅使用FactCG发布标注池的2-3%，超过所有对比紧凑检测器和两个vanilla LLM参考；消融和敏感性分析支持任务感知样本对构造、动态负采样和多枢轴复述的贡献。

Insight: 创新点在于将少量标注转化为关系监督：用同一文档-声明实例的不同保标签复述视图构造正对，用同源文档的异标签样本构造负对；引入分位数感知半困难负采样平衡信息量与覆盖度；两阶段训练后推理轻量。客观看，标签高效、无需额外事实标签且推理仅一次前向是其可借鉴之处。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent claims that are unsupported by a given source document, motivating accurate and efficient document-grounded factuality detection. Existing LLM-based evaluators often require repeated inference and incur high computational cost, whereas compact factuality detectors are efficient at inference time but commonly rely on large labeled or synthetically generated corpora. We propose CPIL (Cross-Paraphrastic Invariance Learning), a two-stage Siamese framework that converts a small labeled set into relational supervision without acquiring additional factuality labels. CPIL constructs positive pairs from distinct label-preserving paraphrastic views of the same document-claim instance and negative pairs from opposite-label examples associated with the same source document. It further introduces quantile-aware semi-hard negative mining, which derives a boundary from the positive-pair similarity distribution and balances informative negatives with broader coverage. In the two-stage training procedure, Stage 1 learns an encoder from these task-aware pairs, and Stage 2 jointly fine-tunes the encoder and a linear classification head for binary factuality prediction. At inference time, CPIL requires no paraphrase generation, pair construction, or Siamese comparison and uses only one encoder-classifier forward pass. On the 11-task LLM-AggreFact benchmark, CPIL-2% and CPIL-3% achieve macro-F1 scores of 81.04 and 81.60 while using only 2-3% of the FactCG-released labeled pool. Both settings outperform all compared compact detectors and two vanilla LLM references. Ablations and sensitivity analyses support the contributions of task-aware pair construction, dynamic negative mining, and multi-pivot paraphrasing.

[21] daVinci-Prover: Agent-Native Mid-Training for Formal Theorem Proving cs.CLPDF

Wenhong Zhu, Xuefeng Li, Jiashuo Zhang, Yu Chen, Tiantian Mi

TL;DR: 论文提出 daVinci-Prover，面向形式定理证明的 Agent 原生中训练方法。作者构建了约 337 亿 token 的中训练语料，覆盖分解、搜索、自验证和修订，并结合验证器驱动的智能体轨迹；同时提出 daVinci-Prover harness，使模型可在隔离工作区中构造证明并与 Lean 验证器交互，进而收集约 3.6 万条高质量轨迹用于监督微调。

Details

Motivation: 形式定理证明模型缺乏透明且有效的数据配方来系统提升能力；论文认为有效的智能体证明器需要强数学技能先验，以及支持长程交互式证明构造的智能体轨迹。

Result: 该中训练配方在 MiniF2F、FATE-M 和 ProofNet 上相较匹配的 Qwen3-8B 仅 SFT 基线平均提升 13.7 个点；应用于 Qwen3.5 系列时也在多个定理证明基准上表现强劲，并通过记忆压缩使 MiniF2F 接近饱和。

Insight: 创新点在于将 Agent 原生中训练语料、验证器驱动的智能体轨迹和可交互 Lean 验证的 harness 结合，形成较透明的数据配方；客观看，其价值在于用分解、搜索、自验证、修订等技能先验与长程证明轨迹共同提升交互式定理证明智能体。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) are rapidly transforming formal theorem proving. Yet further progress remains constrained by the lack of a transparent and effective data recipe for systematically improving theorem-proving models. In this work, we argue that effective agentic provers require strong mathematical skill priors to ground theorem proving and agentic trajectories to enable long-horizon, interactive proof construction. To this end, we synthesize an agent-native 33.7B-token mid-training corpus covering decomposition, search, self-verification, and revision, together with verifier-grounded agentic trajectories. We further introduce the daVinci-Prover harness, which enables models to construct proofs and interact with a Lean verifier in an isolated workspace, and use it to collect approximately 36K high-quality agent trajectories for supervised fine-tuning. Our mid-training recipe yields an average improvement of 13.7 points over the matched Qwen3-8B SFT-only baseline across MiniF2F, FATE-M, and ProofNet. When applied to the Qwen3.5 series, our approach also yields strong performance across theorem-proving benchmarks and achieves near-saturating MiniF2F performance with memory compaction. The results highlight agent-native mid-training as an effective foundation for developing stronger interactive theorem-proving agents.

[22] EvenVLM: A Text and Multimodal Dataset for the Even Language with Vision-Language Fine-Tuning cs.CLPDF

Daniil Makovetskii, Ivan Bondarenko

TL;DR: 论文提出 EvenVLM，首个面向濒危埃文语（Even/Lamut）的文本与多模态 VQA 数据集，并从词典/短语本抽取 50,521 条 Even-俄语条目，合成 107,543 条训练样本。针对黏着形态导致的 tokenization 低效，提出三步 tokenizer 扩展算法，并用 LoRA 微调 Qwen3-VL-4B 完成文本任务和视觉问答。

Details

Motivation: 埃文语使用人数极少、资源匮乏，现有 LLM 缺乏其数据与有效分词，文本和视觉语言任务支持不足；论文旨在构建数据集、评测基准和训练流程，推动低资源语言研究。

Result: 最佳配置在文本基准达 11.4% exact match、13.6 weighted score，句子翻译 chrF 0.1283；VQA 准确率 65%，显著高于基座模型和仅文本微调的 0%。Tokenizer 扩展使平均每词 token 数降低 27%（4.99→3.64）。

Insight: 创新点包括首个 Even 文本/VQA 多模态数据集、面向黏着语形态的三步 tokenizer 扩展，以及基于 LoRA 的文本与 VQA 分离微调流程；客观看，其价值主要在于低资源濒危语言的数据构建与适配方法，可迁移到其他低资源语言。

Abstract: We present EvenVLM, the first text and multimodal (VQA) dataset for Even (Lamut), an endangered Northern Tungusic language spoken by approximately 6,800 people in Siberia. Our dataset comprises 50,521 structured Even-Russian entries extracted from dictionary and phrasebook sources via GPT-4o Mini-based parsing, from which we synthetically generate 107,543 training examples spanning translation, grammar, reading comprehension, and semantic classification. To address tokenization inefficiency on Even’s agglutinative morphology, we propose a three-step tokenizer expansion algorithm that adds Even-specific graphemes (eng, oe, o-umlaut), whitelisted vocabulary, and BPE merges, reducing average tokens per word by 27% (4.99 to 3.64). Using LoRA fine-tuning of Qwen3-VL-4B, we adapt the model for text tasks (0.53% trainable parameters, rank 32) and separately for visual question answering (rank 64, frozen vision encoder). Our best configuration achieves 11.4% exact match and a 13.6 weighted score on text benchmarks, 0.1283 chrF for sentence translation, and 65% accuracy on VQA (up from 0% for the base model and text-only fine-tuning). We release our datasets, evaluation benchmarks, and training pipeline to support further research on Even and other low-resource languages.

[23] MuSeToM: Multi-Sensory Evaluation of Belief Revision with Textual Cues Across Six Senses cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Chunkit Chan, Kawai Chung, Sze Heng Douglas Kwok, Yuxuan Liu, Yauwai Yim

TL;DR: 论文提出MuSeToM，一个经人类验证的文本基准，用于评估大语言模型在心智理论推理中能否根据六类感官线索判断他人何时应修正信念并推断更新后的信念状态。该基准包含2,415个改编自BigToM和OpenToM的实例，并系统评测了最先进LLM在直接提示和思维链提示下的表现。

Details

Motivation: 现有对LLM心智理论的评估主要集中于静态信念归因和错误信念推理，忽略了社会推理中的关键问题：模型能否判断感知证据是否足以支持他人的信念修正。

Result: 论文在MuSeToM上评估了最先进LLM，发现模型常能检测到信念可能变化，却难以识别更新后的信念，并经常对感知显著但认知上不充分的线索产生过度更新；摘要未给出具体量化分数或SOTA结论。

Insight: 创新点在于构建人类验证的多感官文本基准，将心智理论评估从静态信念归因推进到认知信念修正判断，并揭示LLM“检测到变化”与“正确更新信念”之间的显著不一致及过度更新问题。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer mental states in others, is pivotal for human social cognition. Existing evaluations of ToM in large language models (LLMs) mainly focus on static belief attribution and false-belief reasoning, leaving a critical aspect of social reasoning underexplored: can LLMs decide whether perceptual evidence is sufficient to justify another person’s belief revision? To address this gap, we present MuSeToM, a human-validated, text-based benchmark for evaluating epistemic belief revision in ToM reasoning. MuSeToM contains 2,415 instances adapted from BigToM and OpenToM, with textual cues spanning six sensory categories, and tests whether models can determine when a protagonist should revise a belief and infer the resulting updated belief state. Using MuSeToM, we systematically evaluate state-of-the-art LLMs under direct and chain-of-thought prompting, revealing a pronounced dissonance: while models often detect that a belief may change, they struggle to identify the updated belief and frequently over-update on perceptually salient but epistemically insufficient cues.

[24] Outcome-Mass Alignment: Controlling Length-Dependent Trajectory Influence in Multi-Turn LLM Agent Reinforcement Learning cs.CLPDF

Dawei Zhang, Yubin Huangfu, Jessica Fan, Boyu Wei, Likang Wu

TL;DR: 论文研究多轮 LLM 智能体强化学习中，将长轨迹展开为多个响应行和 token 后引发的“外部记账”问题：同一 episode 结果可能被重复计数，不同轨迹获得的目标质量也可能不均衡。作者提出 Outcome-Mass Alignment（OMA），分别用 Outcome Alignment 按唯一源轨迹统计结果、用 Mass Alignment 控制轨迹内聚合后的总目标质量。方法不改变基础优化器的信用分配、重要性比和裁剪机制。

Details

Motivation: 多轮智能体训练中，轨迹展开会导致长度相关的隐式加权：失败轨迹往往展开更多，从而可能被重复计入 outcome 统计或获得不等目标质量，影响策略优化。论文旨在显式修正这种轨迹级记账偏差，而不改动 GRPO 等优化器核心机制。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 的 GRPO 匹配比较中，OMA 在两个模型规模上提升 matched-horizon endpoint success 7.0–9.4 个百分点；在面向成功的 WebShop-SOL 上，将 success nAUC 从 0.399 提升到 0.511。普通 WebShop 中展开与结果耦合弱或反向，效果混合；消融及与 GiGPO、GSPO 的比较支持组件可分性和跨优化器兼容性。

Insight: 核心创新是把轨迹展开带来的 outcome 统计重复和目标质量不均衡显式建模并校正：按唯一轨迹统计 outcome，并在轨迹内聚合后对齐总目标质量。其价值在于不重设计基础优化器即可修正长度依赖的隐式加权，且指出轨迹展开总会定义一种隐式加权规则，实际效果取决于其与结果和优化器更新的耦合方式。

Abstract: To keep long interaction contexts tractable, stepwise group-relative training for multi-turn LLM agents materializes each source trajectory into multiple response rows and valid tokens. This expansion creates an outer-accounting problem distinct from within-trajectory credit assignment: row-based outcome statistics can count one episode outcome multiple times, while token-level reduction can assign unequal aggregate objective mass across trajectories. We term these effects outcome-statistics multiplicity and objective-mass imbalance. We introduce Outcome-Mass Alignment (OMA), a two-component method that makes both accounting choices explicit. Outcome Alignment computes outcome statistics over unique source trajectories, while Mass Alignment controls aggregate objective mass after within-trajectory aggregation of optimizer-native token objectives. Neither redesigns the base optimizer’s credit assignment, importance ratios, or clipping. Across matched ALFWorld GRPO comparisons at two model scales, where failed trajectories typically expand much more than successful ones, OMA improves matched-horizon endpoint success by 7.0-9.4 percentage points. Ordinary WebShop exhibits weak or reversed expansion-outcome coupling and correspondingly mixed effects, whereas in a success-oriented WebShop setting, WebShop-SOL, OMA improves success nAUC from 0.399 to 0.511. Component ablations and GiGPO and GSPO comparisons further support component separability and cross-optimizer compatibility. These results show that trajectory expansion always defines an implicit weighting rule, but its practical effect depends on how expansion couples to outcomes and optimizer-native updates.

[25] Lara Mini Base: Language Diversity as a Resource, Not a Trade-off cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Translated, Maria Pia Adinolfi, Isabelle Andrieu, Grazia Fiorella Anfuso, Melania Annunziata

TL;DR: 论文提出 Lara Mini Base，一个从零训练的多语言基础模型，使用 6T token 覆盖英语和44种其他语言（含欧盟24种官方语言）。模型采用 7.2B 总参数、0.6B 激活参数的 MoE 架构，在最多2B激活参数的开放基础模型中取得英语、多语言、数学和代码基准的最高平均分，并支持128k上下文。

Details

Motivation: 现有通用语言模型主要依赖英语，开放模型中非英语预训练数据占比最多仅12%；面向欧洲语言的模型虽多语言数据更多，但同尺寸性能低于通用模型。论文关注小模型中多语言是否因容量竞争而牺牲性能的问题。

Result: 在最多2B激活参数的开放基础模型中，Lara Mini Base 在标准英语、多语言、数学和代码基准上的平均分最高，超过使用4至6倍token训练的模型；其优势在多语言基准和数学基准上最明显。模型支持128k上下文，单GPU单用户无量化生成速度至少比平均分更高的模型快1.8倍。

Insight: 论文宣称的创新点是从零构建高比例多语言数据训练的小型MoE基础模型，使44种其他语言总权重随训练超过英语，并通过MoE降低语言间容量竞争。客观看，其可借鉴之处在于将语言多样性视为资源，用稀疏激活在低推理成本下兼顾多语言、英语、数学和代码能力，并发布多语言评测翻译T-HellaSwag。

Abstract: General-purpose language models are trained mostly on English: over open models we review, at most, non-English pretraining data makes up only a 12% share. Models built for European languages give them far more, but score well below general-purpose models of the same size. A common concern is that, in small models, languages compete for capacity. We present Lara Mini base, a model trained from scratch on 6T tokens in English and 44 other languages, which together cover all 24 official languages of the European Union. As training progresses, the 44 other languages together come to outweigh English. To give these languages enough capacity at low cost, Lara Mini base uses a mixture of experts architecture with 7.2B total and 0.6B active parameters. Among open base models with up to 2B active parameters, it achieves the highest average score across standard English, multilingual, math, and code benchmarks, ahead of models trained on 4 to 6 times as many tokens. Its margin is largest on multilingual benchmarks in the languages it supports and on math benchmarks. It supports contexts of up to 128k tokens. We find that our MoE architecture reduces capacity competition between languages. For a single user on one GPU, without quantization, it generates text at least 1.8 times faster than any model with a higher average score. We release the weights of Lara Mini base under the CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 license. In addition, we will release T-HellaSwag, a higher-quality machine translation of the commonsense reasoning benchmark HellaSwag into 110 language variants.

[26] Parameter-Efficient Conciseness Adaptation: Differentiable Length Control for Concise Clinical Reasoning in Smaller Language Models cs.CL | cs.AIPDF

Tanzeel Abbas, Ahmad Kamran Malik, Nafees Qamar, Sana Liaqat

TL;DR: 论文提出PECA（参数高效简洁性适配），面向小型语言模型在临床推理中的冗长输出问题，在QLoRA监督微调目标中加入可微的简洁性约束。由于token数是离散不可微的，PECA在生成超过预算后提高结束符概率，从而在普通训练中实现长度控制。在1.7B模型和医学推理数据上，它显著缩短回答并提升诊断选择题准确率。

Details

Motivation: 小型语言模型适合低成本临床部署，但微调后在医学任务上回答过于冗长，影响临床决策支持和床旁使用。现有长度控制方法依赖偏好优化、奖励模型或专有教师模型，削弱了SLM的可及性优势。

Result: 在1.7B参数模型上基于medical-o1-reasoning-SFT微调后，PECA将平均回复长度降低72.8%，同时将多项选择诊断准确率从40.8%提升到44.0%；调节简洁性强度可得到可解释的准确率-长度权衡前沿。

Insight: 创新点在于将简洁性项直接融入QLoRA监督微调，并用超过token预算后提高EOS概率作为可微代理，避免偏好数据、辅助模型和专有教师。客观看，其价值在于低资源、单卡可训练的长度控制机制及准确率-长度前沿分析。

Abstract: Smaller language models (SLMs) make it feasible to deploy clinical natural language processing where large proprietary models are too costly, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning allows them to be specialised on commodity hardware. One obstacle persists after training: on medical tasks these models answer at far greater length than the clinical question requires. In clinical decision support and point-of-care use, where time is short, such verbosity is hard to act on even when the underlying reasoning is sound. Existing length-control methods depend on preference optimisation, reward models, or proprietary teachers that undercut the accessibility making SLMs attractive. We introduce Parameter-Efficient Conciseness Adaptation (PECA), which adds a single differentiable conciseness term to the QLoRA supervised fine-tuning objective. Because the token count is discrete and non-differentiable, PECA instead raises the end-of-sequence probability once generation exceeds a token budget, a smooth signal optimised during ordinary training, with no preference data, no auxiliary model, and a single commodity GPU. On a 1.7-billion-parameter model fine-tuned on the medical-o1-reasoning-SFT corpus, PECA reduces mean response length by 72.8% while improving multiple-choice diagnostic accuracy from 40.8% to 44.0%. A sweep over the conciseness strength traces an interpretable accuracy-to-length frontier for balancing brevity and diagnostic completeness.

[27] Learning When Diffusion Language Models Should Regenerate cs.CLPDF

Minhae Oh, Jeonghye Kim, Jinwoo Koo, Yeongoon Kim, Jimin Seo

TL;DR: 论文研究扩散语言模型在生成文本后如何有效利用重掩码进行纠错，指出仅靠token置信度难以暴露隐藏状态中的错误信息，且固定上下文下单独重掩码孤立错误往往会复现错误。为此提出DiVR验证器，基于模型自身生成轨迹的最终正确性训练，预测部分生成序列能否得到正确答案，并决定何时重生成一个块以及保留哪个候选，而生成器本身保持不变。

Details

Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽可通过重掩码回看并修正已生成文本，但有效纠错需要知道何时修正、修正什么；现有方法对错误定位和孤立错误修正不足。

Result: 在五个推理基准和LLaDA-8B-Base上，DiVR将微调生成器的平均准确率从35.3%提升到43.4%，成本为贪心解码的1.9-3.5倍，优于使用3.8-7.4倍成本的搜索基线；还可兼容SFT/RL后训练并泛化到Dream等生成器。

Insight: 创新点在于从Sudoku诊断出发利用隐藏状态中的错误信息而非仅依赖token置信度，并训练一个预测轨迹最终正确性的验证器来做块级重生成与候选保留决策，形成不改变生成器的即插即用纠错机制。

Abstract: Remasking allows diffusion language models to revisit generated text, but effective correction requires knowing when and what to revise. Our diagnosis on Sudoku reveals two gaps. Hidden states contain error information that token confidence does not reliably expose, and remasking an isolated error usually reproduces it when the surrounding context remains fixed. These findings motivate DiVR , a verifier that predicts whether a partially generated sequence will reach a correct answer. Trained on the final correctness of the model’s own trajectories, DiVR decides when to regenerate a block and which alternative to retain, while the generator itself stays unchanged. On five reasoning benchmarks with LLaDA-8B-Base, DiVR raises the average accuracy of the fine-tuned generator from 35.3% to 43.4% at $1.9$-$3.5\times$ the cost of greedy decoding, above search baselines that use $3.8$-$7.4\times$. It also complements post-training such as supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, and generalizes to other generators, including Dream.

Details

Motivation: 工业界常引入架构创新但很少公开哪些想法成功、哪些被放弃及原因；学术界受算力和基线不一致限制，评估多停留在小规模单点比较，难以判断改进能否扩展到更大规模。

Abstract: Model architecture shapes the scaling behavior, training efficiency, and inference cost of LLMs, making it a central focus of both industry and academia. In industry, teams such as those behind DeepSeek, Qwen, and Kimi routinely introduce architectural innovations, yet public reports rarely document which ideas succeeded, which were abandoned, and why. In academia, limited compute and inconsistent baselines often restrict evaluations to small-scale, single-point comparisons, making it difficult to establish whether improvements hold at larger scales or translate into broader impact. ArchSpace addresses both gaps as a public platform for Recording, Reviewing, and Referencing Architecture Researc}: each proposal is recorded with its experiments and reasoning, reviewed by the community, and referenced by later work. To evaluate ideas before costly large-scale training, ArchSpace leverages scaling laws to predict model loss at larger scales from smaller experiments, a practice widely adopted in industry. We provide initial references that make the platform usable: Qwen3.5 and OLMo3 baselines, fitted scaling laws, and a reusable training recipe. Ideas validated through this review can in turn become new baselines, and ArchSpace thereby aims to broaden the range of ideas that shape the next generation of models.

[29] FreeDeshadow: Shadow Removal for User-Specified Objects With Multimodal Prompts cs.CVPDF

Details

Motivation: 现有阴影去除方法多为全局去除或依赖精确阴影掩码，难以在实际应用中仅去除用户指定物体投射的阴影，因此需要研究对象级、可交互控制的阴影去除。

Result: 在自建PCSRD+数据集（154,571组、1024×1024、六类提示）上实验表明，FreeDeshadow能在视觉和文本提示下准确去除目标物体阴影并有效保留非目标区域，且在阴影去除性能和可控性上优于现有方法。

Insight: 创新点在于定义对象感知可控阴影去除任务，支持点、线、物体掩码及属性/空间关系/状态文本等多模态提示；通过对象感知生成与阴影感知局部精化建立物体与其空间分离投射阴影的对应关系，并将修改约束在目标阴影区域。

Abstract: Existing shadow removal methods either perform global shadow removal or rely on precise shadow masks to guide restoration. Such paradigms limit their ability to selectively remove the shadow cast by a user-specified object in practical applications. To this end, we introduce an object-aware controllable shadow removal task, which allows users to specify a target object through a simple prompt and removes only its cast shadow while preserving other content. However, object-shadow asymmetry makes it challenging to establish accurate correspondence between the specified object and its spatially separated cast shadow and to confine restoration to the target shadow region. To address these challenges, we propose FreeDeshadow, a unified controllable shadow removal framework that supports both visual and textual prompts. It consists of an object-aware generator and a shadow-aware local refiner. The former interprets the user prompt and produces a high-quality shadow-free proposal, while the latter establishes the correspondence between the object and its cast shadow and constrains the generated changes to the target shadow region. To support training and evaluation, we further construct PCSRD+, a large-scale dataset containing 154,571 quadruples at 1024$\times$1024 resolution and covering six types of user prompts. The visual prompts include a dot, a line, and an object mask, while the textual prompts describe the target object using attributes, spatial relations, or states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FreeDeshadow accurately removes the shadow of the target object under both visual and textual prompts while effectively preserving non-target regions. It also outperforms existing methods in shadow removal performance and controllability. Project Page: https://wzlwlm13.github.io/FreeDeshadow

[30] What Entropy-Based View Selection Cannot See: An Audit of Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language Models cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Sivasuthan Sukumar, Uthayasanker Thayasivam

TL;DR: 论文审计了视觉语言模型（如CLIP）测试时适应中基于熵的视图选择。作者在固定其他流程后发现，精度提升几乎全部来自视图选择而非梯度更新；当模型预测错误但至少有一个视图正确时，熵对正确视图的排序接近随机，仅恢复理想选择器约1/20的精度。作者还发现裁剪保留原图比例这一被忽视的信号，用其排序视图在多数基准上优于熵和最强评分函数。

Details

Motivation: CLIP等视觉语言模型在分布偏移下精度下降，测试时增强通过随机裁剪/翻转生成多视图并聚合预测，但需要判断哪些视图可信；主流方法依赖预测熵等模型自身响应，却很少审计这些信号是否真正选出有用视图。

Result: 在15个基准数据集上，按裁剪保留比例排序视图在14/15上优于熵、11/15上优于所测试的最强评分函数；同时熵在错误预测场景下对正确视图的排序仅接近随机，恢复精度约为理想选择器的1/20，且跨两个图像编码器成立。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于将TTA增益主要归因于视图选择，并揭示熵只度量置信度而不度量视图支持哪一类别；同时从增强流程中挖掘“裁剪保留原图比例”这一无需额外模型信号的简单排序线索，并能用预方法测量预测哪些数据集会受益。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP can classify images into categories they were never explicitly trained to recognize, but accuracy degrades when test images differ in style or source from their training data. A common remedy generates randomly cropped and flipped copies of each test image, called views, and combines their predictions. Since some views miss the object, methods must identify which views to trust. The dominant approach uses prediction entropy to measure confidence and retains the most certain views. Other methods use signals such as embedding typicality. Despite repeated refinements of these scoring functions, the information they rely on, the model’s own response to each view, remains largely unexamined. We investigate this choice while holding other pipeline stages fixed. First, we show that view selection, rather than the gradient update, accounts for almost all accuracy gains. We then examine whether entropy identifies useful views when predictions are wrong, despite at least one view predicting the correct class. In these cases, entropy ranks correct views above incorrect ones no better than chance, recovering only about one-twentieth of the accuracy recovered by an oracle over the same views. This finding holds across two image encoders and under a control ruling out an obvious statistical artifact. An exact analysis explains this limitation: entropy measures confidence but not which class a view favors. We identify an unused signal already available in the augmentation pipeline: the fraction of the original image retained by each crop. Ranking views by this quantity improves accuracy on 14 of 15 benchmark datasets over entropy and on 11 of 15 over the strongest scoring function tested. A pre-method measurement also predicts which datasets benefit.

Result: 在 PhysicalAI-AV 和 nuScenes 数据集上，BirdDreams 比先前驾驶世界模型更忠实地跟随输入自车轨迹，并在无自车动作条件下生成更合理的自车运动。人类评估表明，在生成逼真的周围车辆行为方面优于仅透视基线。

Insight: 创新点包括：联合生成透视视频和 BEV 视频，使场景布局成为预测的一部分；两个视频扩散 transformer 通过联合注意力相互指导；BEV 提供几何接口用于自车控制（通过平移旋转 BEV 最新帧沿输入轨迹生成软条件）；推理时仅需单张相机图像，无需高精地图或车辆状态输入。客观分析认为，结合 BEV 减少遮挡并提供几何控制接口，同时保留透视视频细节，是核心创新。

Abstract: Driving world models aim to simulate how a scene unfolds under different ego actions. This requires plausible motion of surrounding vehicles as well as precise ego-trajectory control. Learning vehicle motion from perspective video alone is challenging because motion is entangled with appearance and vehicles can be occluded. Prior work therefore conditions video generation on layouts that specify vehicle motion, providing control over surrounding vehicles but requiring their future trajectories as inputs. We introduce BirdDreams, an autoregressive driving world model that jointly generates perspective video and a schematic bird’s-eye-view (BEV) video, making scene layout part of the predicted future. The BEV, rendered from scene annotations for training, depicts roads and vehicles as simple shapes at a fixed metric scale, providing a top-down view of their positions and motion with reduced occlusion between vehicles. Perspective video, by contrast, retains scene details that the BEV omits, such as traffic signals and object appearance. Joint attention between two video diffusion transformers, one per stream, lets each stream inform the other during generation. The predicted BEV also provides a geometric interface for ego control: we translate and rotate its latest frame along an input ego trajectory and use the resulting frames as a soft condition that shows how the scene would appear to move if only the ego vehicle moved. At inference, BirdDreams generates both videos from a single camera image without requiring an HD map or vehicle-state inputs. On PhysicalAI-AV and nuScenes, BirdDreams follows input ego trajectories more faithfully than prior driving world models and generates more plausible ego motion without ego-action conditioning. Human evaluation shows that BirdDreams outperforms a perspective-only baseline in generating realistic surrounding-vehicle behavior.

Abstract: Cataloguing a Roman Imperial coin involves reading its legends and identifying its imagery in order to match it to an entry in the standard reference catalogue. We instead formulate coin description as image-to-text generation and adapt an open-weight vision-language model (VLM), Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, to four tasks: transcribing the obverse and reverse legends, and describing the obverse and reverse imagery. To obtain training data, we convert approximately 30,000 auction records into structured image-text pairs. We separate the two sides of each coin, extract and normalize legends and descriptions, and standardize obverse descriptions against a bust-type taxonomy. We then train a separate low-rank adapter for each task on a single 20GB GPU, and evaluate each task with metrics that we define to capture the key components of numismatic descriptions. Result: 在宇宙学、分子动力学和等离子体物理数据上的评估表明，神经编解码器相比传统G-PCC基线仅实现边际比特率降低，存在显著性能差距，未能达到CV基准上的优势水平。

Insight: 创新点在于指出科学粒子与CV点云在分形维数上的关键差异——CV点云局部平面而粒子非平面，从而限制了八叉树结构的有效性；并系统梳理了科学数据结构对当前神经方法的限制及未来学习可介入的方向。

Abstract: As particle-based simulations grow in scale, efficient compression of scientific data becomes critical. Neural point cloud compression (PCC) methods excel on computer vision (CV) benchmarks, but do they transfer to scientific particle data? We highlight that scientific particles differ from CV point clouds in fractal dimension: CV point clouds are locally planar, but particles are not. This key difference limits the effectiveness of the octree data structure that underpins most PCC methods. Neural codecs achieve only marginal bitrate reductions over the traditional G-PCC baseline on scientific data, revealing a significant performance gap. Supported by an evaluation spanning domains of cosmology, molecular dynamics, and plasma physics, we outline where the structure of scientific data limits current neural approaches and where learning can help in the future.

Abstract: Open-weight Vision-Language Models (VLMs) score 85-94% on spatial benchmarks (BLINK Spatial, What’s Up CLEVR) yet collapse to chance on simple geometric questions when object, scene, and texture priors are stripped away. We introduce and open-source Sophon, a procedurally generated diagnostic of paired colored point-cloud stimuli across three coordinate systems (1D linear, 2D Cartesian, 2D polar) and four question types (binary, categorical, comparative, quantitative). We use linear probes and low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to trace the open-weight failures. Probes of the vision encoder (ViT) and language decoder (LLM) recover Sophon spatial information at 83-85% and 80-86% accuracy, respectively, and the language tower’s reasoning circuit answers text-only controls (point clouds as sets of coordinates) at +40 pp over visual stimuli. Language tower per-layer attention attribution localizes visual integration to mid-stack layers. At readout, models elevate the correct answer-set tokens but select among them randomly. We localize the mechanism by tuning individual and grouped components across every architecture’s stack, including the vision tower (ViT workspace and reader), the projector, and the language tower (LLM workspace and reader). Abstract: We present OrthoPairGTL, a geometric training framework for cross-domain deepfake detection, built on a partially frozen DINOv3 backbone ($\sim$300M parameters), and make three contributions: (1) We re-evaluate six recent transfer-learning detectors (Forensics Adapter, Effort, three GenD variants and FakeSTormer) using the authors’ released checkpoints under a single preprocessing pipeline across 17 cross-domain datasets (2018-2025), so that the reported gaps reflect method rather than per-method pipeline tuning. Motivation: 论文质疑“表达性路由和特征融合优于简单平均”的普遍报告，旨在揭示跨域deepfake检测评估中的泄漏与粒度问题，并给出更可靠的融合评估和尾部鲁棒性分析。

Result: 理论上，score-space路由的最好情况仅为读取测试标签的oracle，且实验中无真实router超过校准全局平均；特征融合的+2.6pp增益在leaky folds下12/12显著，在honest family-held-out folds下0/12显著而消失。全局校准融合InvB在worst-family AUC、AURC和低假阳性召回上稳定优于最佳单专家，专家增加使全专家误判视频从15.2%降至2.3%。

Abstract: Cross-domain video deepfake detection increasingly uses frozen foundation-model experts, combined by calibrated score averaging, a per-video router picking one expert, or a learned head over concatenated features. Expressive routing and feature fusion are often reported to beat plain averaging. Across a 22-dataset frozen-expert testbed, we find those gains are largely evaluation artifacts. A corner theorem locates the gap routing aims to close: because AUC depends only on score ordering, the best any score-space router can do is an oracle reading the test label to pick each video’s expert, a ceiling no realisable, label-blind router reaches. The theorem does not order real routers against a calibrated global average; the case against routing is empirical, and no router we test beats that average. Feature fusion reads raw features, falls outside the theorem, and is audited empirically. Reported gains trace to leakage: holding out one dataset leaks siblings sharing identities and reals (over-crediting routing), while holding out one manipulation keeps that dataset’s reals in training (over-crediting feature fusion). A $2\times 2$ over fold and evaluation granularity pins the $+2.6$pp feature-fusion “win” to the fold alone: 12/12 significant under leaky folds vs. 0/12 under honest family-held-out folds, where both gains vanish. Fusion still pays off in the tail of hard videos and families: as experts are added, videos every expert misclassifies drop from 15.2% to 2.3%, and global calibrated fusion (InvB) beats the best single expert on worst-family AUC, selective risk (AURC), and low-false-positive recall, stably across protocols. Practitioners should calibrate and combine globally, hold out whole dataset families, and measure tail performance. For score-space routing under ranking metrics, honest evaluation and expert diversity matter more than combiner complexity.

Abstract: AI-generated content (AIGC) has substantially narrowed the visual gap between natural and synthetic images, while existing detectors often rely on generator-specific artifacts and exhibit limited generalization to unseen models. We investigate this by systematically identifying robust physical features and incorporating them into a vision-language representation without external textual prompts at inference. Abstract: Reasoning segmentation requires identifying the target referred to by a query among visually similar objects, yet trajectory-level rewards cannot directly indicate which tokens provide relevant visual evidence. An external model generates Segment Hints offline from training images, queries, and ground-truth target annotations, organizing target identity, discriminative attributes, confusable objects, and anchoring paths. A hint-conditioned exponential moving average teacher scores the student’s own trajectories, and HISD computes the teacher-to-student evidence ratio over a token support set to adjust only the magnitudes of positive group-relative advantages, leaving nonpositive advantages unchanged. Abstract: AI-manipulated video detectors are typically evaluated on clean, curated datasets. To measure detection under these conditions, we introduce Reality Perception Gap (RPG), a benchmark of 10,000 videos from frontier generators and partially synthetic edits, processed with cross-platform repost compression and paired with real footage that carries camera-side optical artifacts. Motivation: 日常场景中反复、便捷地测量水合状态仍然受限，论文希望验证面部视频数字生物标志物能否在真实世界中追踪水合状态转变方向，从而支持生活化水合监测。

Result: 主队列104人、208段视频中，评分从禁食前均值36.6升至补水后70.6，变化+33.9（95% CI [+30.7,+37.1]，p<0.001，Cohen’s d=2.03），98.1%参与者上升；体重亚组补水后增加+0.74 kg，血清渗透压亚组下降-5.1 mOsm/kg，水与电解质补液短期评分变化无显著差异（p=0.247）。

Insight: 创新点在于用面部视频构建可追踪水合状态变化方向的数字生物标志物评分，并在真实世界禁食模型中结合体重和血清渗透压进行多层验证；其价值更多在于生活化趋势监测而非临床诊断。

Abstract: Hydration influences physiology and daily function, yet repeated practical measurement in everyday settings remains limited. We present initial real-world evidence that a facial-video lifestyle digital biomarker score can track the direction of hydration state transitions. Using Ramadan fasting as a model of prolonged fluid abstinence, we assessed whether the score tracks the transition between the fasting (pre-drink) state and the rehydrated (post-drink) state across three validation layers. In the primary cohort (104 participants, 208 videos), the score increased from a mean of 36.6 in the fasting (pre-drink) state to 70.6 in the rehydrated (post-drink) state (change = +33.9, 95% CI [+30.7, +37.1], p < 0.001, Cohen’s d = 2.03), with 102 of 104 participants (98.1%) showing an upward shift. In a nested body-weight subgroup (20 participants), body weight increased by +0.74 kg after fluid intake (p < 0.001), confirming fluid uptake and providing supportive physical anchoring. In a nested serum osmolality subgroup (10 participants), osmolality decreased by -5.1 mOsm/kg (p < 0.001), with all post-drink values within the normal clinical range, providing exploratory biological support. An exploratory secondary comparison of water versus electrolyte rehydration showed no statistically significant difference in short-term score change (p = 0.247). This study does not establish a clinical diagnostic test for dehydration. Rather, these findings support the score as a lifestyle digital biomarker for tracking the direction of hydration state change in real-world settings.

Details

Motivation: 解决视觉语言模型在存在无关干扰图像时准确率下降的问题；现有方法多聚焦选择相关图像或限制图像间信息交换，但论文发现即使正确选择目标图像，干扰仍会影响推理，因此需要理解并干预其内部机制。

Result: 在五个加入干扰图的感知基准上，使用五个干扰图时，该方法相比 Qwen2.5-VL 基线在循环准确率上平均提升16个百分点，同时保持单图性能。

Insight: 创新点在于系统揭示“正确选图仍受干扰”的现象，并将干扰机制定位到中层干扰图到文本 token 的连接通路；提出答案一致性正则化，无需直接约束注意力即可减少问题 token 对干扰图的中层注意力，体现了从机制分析到训练目标的闭环设计。

Abstract: The visual world is cluttered: robust vision-language reasoning requires ignoring irrelevant information. Yet vision-language models (VLMs) can suffer substantial accuracy losses when answering questions about a target image with distractors present. Existing mitigations focus on selecting relevant images or limiting information exchange between images. Through a systematic analysis of three open 7-8B VLMs on five perception benchmarks adapted to include distractor images, we show that distractors reduce accuracy even when models correctly identify the relevant image, suggesting that selection alone is not enough. To understand the mechanism behind this interference, we combine attention analysis with targeted knockouts in Qwen2.5-VL and identify middle-layer distractor-to-text connections as a pathway through which irrelevant visual information affects reasoning. We introduce answer consistency regularization, which encourages consistent answer distributions with and without distractors. Without directly constraining attention, this objective reduces middle-layer attention from question tokens to distractor images, consistent with the pathway our analysis implicates. [43] Saying No by Asking Yes: Affirmative Decomposition and Evidence Reflection for Frozen CLIP cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: 论文针对冻结视觉-语言模型难以理解否定描述的问题，提出 Negation-Aware Evidence Scoring（NAES）：利用 CLIP 对肯定描述更强的匹配能力，将否定描述拆解为肯定查询，并结合否定对象存在性证据进行重打分。配套轻量文本分解器 NegSplit 提取否定片段与剩余肯定上下文，从而在无需微调模型的情况下提升否定感知的检索与多选排序。

Details

Motivation: 现有 CLIP 类模型常忽略 caption 中的否定词，例如“没有烤面包机的厨房”反而更偏向包含烤面包机的图像；但模型对肯定描述的匹配能力明显更强，论文希望利用这种不对称性解决否定理解问题。

Result: 在 LAION-2B CLIP 上，NAES 在 COCO-MCQ 和 VOC2007-MCQ 分别达到 82.4% 和 93.0%，比 KTN 高 2.9 和 0.9 个百分点；在 OpenAI CLIP 上比 PeakPatch 高 4.4 和 26.0 个百分点。方法还提升 COCO、MSR-VTT 上的否定描述检索，并可扩展到 SigLIP、SigLIP 2 和更大 CLIP 编码器。

Insight: 创新点在于不微调冻结 CLIP，而是显式分离“肯定内容匹配”和“被否定对象存在性证据”，用轻量 NegSplit 按 caption 结构组合证据，并在无肯定剩余时围绕参考进行证据反射。客观看，该方法巧妙利用了模型能力不对称，训练成本低且跨模型泛化较好；但最佳多选结果依赖竞争描述做中心化与证据对齐，检索提升则无需该条件。

Abstract: Frozen vision-language models often match concepts mentioned in a caption without respecting their negation: a query “a kitchen with no toaster” favors an image containing a toaster. Yet these models are substantially stronger at matching affirmative descriptions. We exploit this asymmetry with Negation-Aware Evidence Scoring (NAES), which reformulates negated descriptions into affirmative queries and combines their evidence by caption structure. A lightweight text decomposer, NegSplit, identifies this structure by extracting negated spans and the remaining affirmative context. For captions with an affirmative remainder, NAES matches that content and applies a presence-based correction for the denial. When no affirmative content remains, it queries the denied object’s presence and reflects the evidence around a reference to score the negated description. With LAION-2B CLIP, NAES achieves 82.4% accuracy on COCO-MCQ and 93.0% on VOC2007-MCQ, surpassing KTN by 2.9 and 0.9 percentage points; with OpenAI CLIP, it exceeds PeakPatch accuracy by 4.4 and 26.0 points, respectively. NAES also improves negated-description retrieval on COCO and MSR-VTT, with gains extending to SigLIP, SigLIP 2, and larger CLIP encoders. The strongest multiple-choice results use competing descriptions to center and align evidence; retrieval improvements persist without this conditioning. These findings show that explicitly separating affirmative matching from denied-object evidence can substantially improve negation-aware ranking.

Details

Motivation: 现有视觉 token 剪枝主要关注最终答案是否正确，忽略了剪枝对响应概率分布的改变，这种改变可能导致置信度决策分歧和采样失败。论文希望在大幅压缩视觉 token 的同时保持全模型响应分布保真度。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-VL-7B 上，10% 保留率时相对 FastV 将采样导致的负翻转减少 30.3%；30% 保留率在 MME 上保留 98.74% 的基线性能，并在四个任务的贪心正确控制样本上验证。

Abstract: Visual token pruning reduces the inference cost of multimodal large language models, but answer accuracy only partly reflects its effect. Pruning can change the probabilities of alternative response tokens even when greedy answers remain correct and unchanged. We compare full and pruned response distributions under shared reference prefixes. Average predictive drift grows with compression on fixed correctly answered samples. Separate analyses associate larger drift with more confidence-decision disagreements and sampling failures. We propose DiFiT (Distribution-Fidelity-guided Token pruning) to learn discrete selection from reference answers and full-token response distributions. Dual-View Distillation matches probabilities across the vocabulary, while answer supervision supports task quality. A learned scorer and differentiable Top-$K$ learn which visual evidence to retain. Insight: 创新点在于：以统一主干、统一预处理和显式成本核算的方式开展受控公平比较，系统性地审视 agentic 视频推理；并证明“简单即足够”——极简主动探索基线已具竞争力，且明确界定了 agentic 探索的适用边界，即当稠密处理会把与答案相关的事件采样得过于稀疏时，尤其是长视频与时间集中证据场景，选择性获取证据才更具优势。

Abstract: Agentic video-reasoning frameworks iteratively explore a video, choosing what to observe based on the query and evidence gathered so far. Existing methods are typically evaluated with different backbones, preprocessing pipelines, and computational budgets, making it difficult to determine which components actually drive performance, how much they cost, and whether agentic inference is preferable to native video reasoning in the first place. We conduct a controlled evaluation of a diverse set of recent agentic video-reasoning systems across two strong contemporary backbones, with explicit token and monetary-cost accounting. Across Gemini 3 Flash and Gemma 4, most existing agentic frameworks fail to consistently improve over native video inference and often increase computational cost. We investigate two primary questions: (1) How much agentic framework complexity is necessary for effective video reasoning? To answer the first question, we introduce a deliberately minimal Active Exploration Baseline and show that it remains competitive with substantially more elaborate agents. Turning to the second question, our results indicate that the advantage of agentic exploration grows when dense processing would otherwise represent answer-relevant events too sparsely, particularly for long videos and temporally concentrated evidence. Abstract: Pretrained video world models are compared and chosen as components for robot learning largely by future-prediction scores. We separate the three with a matched control. On 27,000 future-retrieval trials from household egocentric video, a model must pick the true continuation of a clip among eleven candidates from the same video (chance 0.091), ranked zero-shot by its own training criterion. Persistence alone recovers the encoder ordering expected from pretraining, a sign that the probe tracks real signal. Insight: 创新点在于用自然语言技能描述嵌入的加权组合和分数参数化路径实现评分，而非匹配固定等级概率；PRA-cond 通过文本项名称生成查询支持未见评估项零样本迁移，并具备一定可解释性。客观看，其核心价值是将评分标准与模型预测解耦到语义嵌入空间，提升跨 rubric 迁移能力。

Abstract: Expert review of surgical videos is essential for surgical training but labor-intensive, motivating AI-assisted skill assessment to provide feedback at scale. Although surgical assessment rubrics may use different language to describe similar skills, existing automated methods commonly predict predefined skill levels or numerical scores tied to fixed rubrics, limiting their ability to transfer across semantically equivalent assessment criteria. We propose Predictive Rubric Alignment (PRA), a joint embedding prediction approach that scores surgical videos using natural-language descriptions of skill levels. Rather than matching level probabilities, PRA predicts a rater-weighted combination of skill-level description embeddings. Adjacent description features define a score-parameterized path, and the predicted embedding is projected onto this path to read the score. We introduce two variants: \textit{PRA-item}, which uses learned queries for predefined assessment items, and \textit{PRA-cond}, which generates queries from textual item names to enable assessment of unseen items without retraining. On AIxSuture, a dataset of expert-rated suturing videos, PRA outperforms published baselines in skill classification. We further evaluate zero-shot transfer to the smaller surgical skill assessment benchmark JIGSAWS. Trained on only two AIxSuture rubric items, PRA-cond assesses unseen JIGSAWS items without further training, yielding positive score correlations and greater Grad-CAM overlap with instruments and suture than a description-matching baseline. These results support predictive rubric alignment as a promising direction for flexible and explainable automated surgical skill assessment.

Abstract: In this report, we introduce the Reciprocal World Model (RWM) and present Duo v1, a full-duplex world model that continuously perceives audiovisual inputs while responding to users in real time. The RWM treats intelligence and physical reality as equally fundamental to what constitutes a world. Existing world models largely focus on simulating the latter, leaving reasoning and decision-making as external controls rather than intrinsic parts of the world itself. In RWM, we identify two equally fundamental capabilities: Intent, which provides the cognition underlying world interactions, and Realization, which generates coherent audio-visual behavior. To realize this formulation, Duo v1 introduces three key technical designs: (1) Leveraging autoregressive modeling for continuous interaction reasoning and participation decisions, rather than restricting it to instruction understanding for diffusion models. (2) Constructing chunk-specific conditions and synchronize tokens across Intent and Realization on a unified timeline. Module-specific attention masks enforce causal dependencies both within and across chunks, preventing access to future inputs. (3) Mitigating distribution shift in both modules through dual rollout distillation, training on autoregressively generated Intent tokens and synthesized audiovisual histories from Realization. Both modules continuously perceive real-time audio, video, and text, enabling full-duplex interaction with spontaneous conversations and interruptions. Beyond conversation, users can share real objects, reshape virtual scenes, guide assistants, or play audiovisual games without mouse or keyboard controls. The release of Duo v1 makes our first step, with the next version coming soon. We look forward to broader exploration of this emerging field.

Abstract: Predictive video encoders provide dense visual representations, but adapting them to 3D reconstruction requires accurate depth, camera poses, and cross-view geometry. [52] Reframing Video Inverse Tone Mapping as Blind Posterior Inference via Latent Luminance Supervision cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Details

Motivation: 大多数视频仅有SDR内容，尤其生成视频没有HDR母版，而SDR到HDR转换需反演未记录的色调映射，截断导致HDR恢复成为盲逆问题。现有生成式先验方法补全结构合理但亮度重建存在系统性偏差，偏差方向和大小受未知色调算子影响。
