Insight: 创新点在于将视频ITM表述为未知算子下的盲后验推断，并用视频扩散潜变量的固定仿射函数直接读取对数亮度而无需解码；仅在截断导致亮度不受约束的区域使用不对称损失选择后验期望分位数；同时通过显式色调算子族合成训练SDR数据，使模型对不可观测的算子进行边缘化。

Abstract: High-dynamic-range (HDR) displays are now common, but most video content, such as archival footage, broadcast material, and the output of generative video models, exists only in standard dynamic range (SDR). The need is sharpest for generated video, which has no HDR master at all and is graded alongside live-action and visual-effects material finished in HDR. Converting SDR to HDR means undoing a tone-mapping operation that was never recorded, and clipping destroys the radiance wherever it acts, so HDR recovery is a blind inverse problem. Methods built on generative video priors fill clipped regions with plausible structure, but their reconstructions are systematically biased: in every public method we test, saturated content leans too bright or too dark, and the direction of the lean moves with the tone operator behind the input. We call this failure the luminance error, measure both its magnitude and its sign, and make it the focus of this work. Reframing ITM as posterior inference under an unknown operator locates the failure in two places and yields two corrections. A fixed affine functional of the video-diffusion latent reads log-luminance without decoding; on that coordinate an asymmetric loss selects an expectile of the posterior, applied only where clipping leaves luminance unconstrained. We also synthesize training SDR through an explicit family of tone operators, so the model marginalizes over what the observation cannot show. Trained this way, our luminance error stays flatter across operators and smaller on average than any public method, and the full system achieves the best perceptual quality on all three benchmarks.

Abstract: Recent advances in flow-matching Transformers have greatly improved Text-to-Image (T2I) and Text-to-Video (T2V) generation, but unconstrained generation risks producing illegal or unethical content. Safety alignment must therefore suppress unsafe content while preserving legitimate generation. A concept’s safety depends on context: a knife used for cooking is safe, whereas its use in violence is harmful. Yet most concept erasure methods indiscriminately suppress static, isolated representations, overlooking evolving semantic interactions. Consequently, safe scenes containing potentially sensitive terms may be unintentionally suppressed, compromising generation quality. To address this, we propose Dynamic Graph Semantic Recomposition (DGSR), which dynamically constructs and updates a semantic interaction graph from evolving text visual states during sampling. Context aware graph reasoning captures semantic dependencies, while paired safe/unsafe supervision distinguishes harmful from legitimate interactions, enabling precise identification of and selective intervention on high risk semantic relationships while preserving benign semantics. Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a mainstream representation for real-time novel view synthesis and 3D reconstruction. However, existing 3DGS rendering pipelines are poorly aligned with modern GPU architectures: depth-sorted alpha compositing introduces strong per-pixel sequential dependencies, limiting parallelism and resulting in scalar FMA-dominated execution that underutilizes high-throughput matrix units such as Tensor Cores. This mismatch becomes more severe for multimodal and semantically enriched 3DGS, where high-dimensional features must be accumulated from millions of Gaussians, leading to rapidly increasing computation and memory costs. We propose MatrixSplat, a matrix-parallel rendering framework for feature-scalable 3DGS on modern GPUs. We first identify the sequential dependency of alpha compositing as a key obstacle to efficient high-dimensional feature rendering, and adopt an order-independent weighted accumulation formulation that transforms feature rendering into matrix-oriented computation. Our framework features three key designs: (i) a tile-based splatting pipeline that maps feature accumulation to warp-level matrix-multiply-accumulate execution on Tensor Cores; (ii) a warp-granularity Gaussian packing strategy that mitigates visibility irregularity and improves feature reuse; and (iii) a lazy channel-batched feature-fetching pipeline that reduces shared-memory pressure, maintains high warp occupancy, and overlaps memory access with matrix execution for high-dimensional features. On Mip-NeRF 360, LERF, and 3D-OVS, MatrixSplat achieves up to 3.8$\times$ speedup over 3DGS and 2$\times$ over TC-GS while maintaining rendering quality and semantic accuracy, and further reduces render-kernel time by up to 36.7$\times$ for high-dimensional features. Integrated into existing fast renderers, it provides an additional 1.69-2.84$\times$ speedup.

TL;DR: 论文针对多模态大语言模型视觉定位中的激活引导，指出现有全局均值引导向量会引发跨任务干扰，提出无需训练的 MAST 框架：按任务分组并多中心聚类，选取每个簇的代表 medoid，推理时将查询路由到多个代表簇并聚合低秩局部引导方向；进一步用任务感知探针重加权局部方向族得到 MAST-PR。

Details

Motivation: 现有方法用全局激活均值作为引导向量，但任务激活呈多中心几何，全局均值落在簇间空白区域，无法忠实于任一任务，导致跨任务干扰和性能不稳定；同时各任务局部引导信号低秩，不同查询任务需侧重不同局部基方向。

Result: 摘要未给出具体 benchmark 名称或与 SOTA 的对比；仅报告 MAST-PR 相比 MAST 平均提升 +8.09 分。

Insight: 创新点在于将激活引导从单一全局均值转为基于 medoid 锚定的多中心局部低秩子空间引导，并做输入自适应路由与聚合；MAST-PR 引入轻量任务感知探针重加权任务特化方向族。客观看，核心可借鉴的是利用任务激活的多中心/低秩结构实现免训练、可组合的激活干预。

Abstract: Existing activation steering methods for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) use the global activation mean as the steering vector, which tends to cause cross-task interference and unstable performance. We trace this failure to the multi-centered geometry of task activations. The global mean lands in the empty region between clusters and is faithful to no single task. Moreover, the steering signal in each task-pure local cluster is low-rank, and different query tasks benefit from emphasizing different families of local-basis directions under the shared routing protocol. To address this, we propose MAST (Medoid-Anchored Subspace Steering), a training-free, input-adaptive activation steering framework. MAST groups samples by task, performs multi-center clustering within each group, and selects one representative medoid per cluster. At inference time, each query is routed to multiple representative medoids. For each matched cluster, a low-rank local steering direction is extracted and aggregated across clusters to produce the final steering vector. Building on MAST, MAST-PR (Probe-Reweighted) uses a lightweight task-aware probe to reweight task-specialized local-direction families, yielding a further average improvement of +8.09 points over MAST.

[57] GingivitisSeg: A Public Dataset and Comparative Benchmark for Gingivitis Segmentation in Intraoral Images cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Zeynep Ozdemir, Zehra Hasgul, Irem Ulku, Omer Ozgur Tanriover, Fatma Karacaoglu

TL;DR: 论文提出了GingivitisSeg，一个包含961张口内图像及像素级牙龈炎标注的公开数据集与分割基准，并划分训练/验证/测试集。作者在统一设置下比较了七种语义分割模型-骨干网络组合，并进行损失函数与辅助监督消融实验。UPerNet-ResNet50取得最高mIoU 76.49%和牙龈炎IoU 54.91%，但与DeepLabV3+-ResNet50、SegFormer-MiT-B2差异在Holm校正后不显著。

Details

Motivation: 从口内照片自动分割牙龈炎可辅助计算机辅助牙周评估，但像素级分割受前景-背景类别严重不平衡、低对比边界以及光照和解剖结构变化影响，且缺乏公开基准。

Result: 在自建GingivitisSeg基准上，UPerNet-ResNet50达到最高mIoU 76.49%、牙龈炎IoU 54.91%；与DeepLabV3+-ResNet50和SegFormer-MiT-B2的差异经Holm校正后不显著。组合优化组件可提升UPerNet-ResNet50性能，且性能与牙龈炎区域大小相关，并在更强图像模糊下下降。

Insight: 创新点在于发布带像素级牙龈炎标注的公开数据集和标准化分割基准，并进行多模型对比、损失函数与辅助监督消融及区域大小和模糊鲁棒性分析，为该细分任务提供可复现评价基础。

Abstract: Gingivitis segmentation from intraoral photographs can support computer-assisted periodontal assessment, but pixel-level delineation remains challenging due to severe foreground-background class imbalance, low-contrast boundaries, and variations in illumination and anatomy. We introduce GingivitisSeg, a public benchmark comprising 961 intraoral images with pixel-level annotations of gingival inflammation and predefined training (672), validation (144), and test (145) partitions. Seven semantic segmentation model-backbone configurations were compared using standardized partitions, input resolution, and evaluation metrics. Controlled ablation experiments examined class-weighted cross-entropy loss, Dice loss, and auxiliary supervision. UPerNet with ResNet50 achieved the highest mean Intersection over Union (mIoU) of 76.49% and gingivitis IoU of 54.91%. However, differences from DeepLabV3+ with ResNet50 and SegFormer with MiT-B2 were not statistically significant after Holm correction. The evaluated optimization components improved performance when combined in the UPerNet-ResNet50 configuration. Further analyses showed that performance was associated with gingivitis region size and decreased under stronger image blur. The dataset, benchmark splits, and source code are available at https://github.com/zeynepozdemir/GingivitisSeg.

[58] OT-BLS: Optimal-Transport-based Backbone Layer Selection for Frozen DINOv3 in Cross-Domain Few-Shot Segmentation cs.CVPDF

Natsuo Okamoto, Kazuki Kobayashi, Yoshio Kato, Shuhei Tarashima

TL;DR: 论文提出 OT-BLS，一种面向冻结 DINOv3 的跨域小样本分割免训练骨干层选择准则。它利用查询与支持 patch-token 特征间的最优传输计划，并按候选查询掩码的前景/背景划分聚合传输质量来评估各层，从而为每个 support-query episode 自适应选择最佳 DINOv3 层用于掩码预测。

Details

Motivation: 免训练 CD-FSS 中最佳 DINOv3 层随 episode 变化，现有层选择准则未显式检验查询预测的前景/背景是否与支持标签一致，导致选择不够可靠。

Result: 在 8 个基准数据集上验证；在 16 个数据集/shot 设置中 12 个优于最后一层基线，平均 mIoU 从 55.72 提升至 57.62，比最佳现有层选择准则高 2.99 点，且速度快 3.7–14.6 倍。

Insight: 摘要宣称的创新是提出基于最优传输对应关系的层选择准则，显式衡量查询前景/背景预测与支持的一致性；客观看，其创新在于把 OT 匹配质量作为免训练层选择信号，兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: Cross-domain few-shot segmentation (CD-FSS) aims to segment a target class in images from a previously unseen domain using only a few annotated support examples. Training-free CD-FSS methods, which typically leverage vision foundation models such as DINOv3, achieve competitive performance without parameter updates. However, the optimal DINOv3 layer for mask prediction varies across support–query episodes. Although several layer-selection criteria have been proposed, they do not explicitly evaluate whether the predicted query foreground and background labels agree with the corresponding support labels. To address this limitation, we propose a novel layer-selection criterion, Optimal-Transport-Based Backbone Layer Selection (OT-BLS). Our key insight is that an effective layer-selection criterion can be derived from the optimal-transport correspondence between query and support patch-token features. Based on this insight, we first estimate the transport plan using a well-established optimal transport algorithm and then compute a layer-selection score by aggregating the transported mass according to the foreground–background partition defined by each candidate query mask. The resulting score reflects how well the predicted foreground and background of the query correspond to those of the support. Experiments on eight benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of OT-BLS. It outperforms the conventional last-layer baseline in 12 of 16 dataset/shot settings. Furthermore, OT-BLS improves the average mIoU from 55.72 to 57.62 and achieves a 2.99-point improvement over the best-performing existing layer-selection criterion while running 3.7–14.6$\times$ faster.

[59] A General Concept-based Decomposition for Vision-Language Embeddings cs.CVPDF

Simone Alberto Peirone, Ortal Senouf, Francesca Pistilli, Giuseppe Averta, Pascal Frossard

TL;DR: 论文提出GRACE，用于将视觉-语言模型（VLM）的视觉嵌入在特征空间层面分解为语义与通用概念，并设计保持样本间几何关系的训练目标以建模概念组合与关联。方法在多个图像和视频基准上验证，概念分解与人类感知对齐，跨任务和领域保持较忠实分解，同时以较小开销提升VLM可解释性并保留大部分原始性能。

Details

Motivation: 现有VLM虽通过多模态对齐捕捉组合结构，但其表示是否与人类自然描述视觉输入的方式一致仍不明确；已有概念分解方法多依赖实例级优化，较为局部，难以捕捉全局语义结构并支持跨输入、任务和领域的组合泛化。

Result: 在多个图像和视频基准上验证，GRACE的概念分解与人类感知对齐，并在不同任务和领域上实现较忠实的分解；摘要未给出具体数值，但宣称以最小额外开销提升可解释性，同时保留现有VLM的大部分原始性能。

Insight: 创新点在于从实例级概念归因转向特征空间层面的概念结构学习，并引入保持不同样本在概念空间中几何关系的训练目标，以更好理解概念如何组合和关联。客观来看，其价值在于用全局语义结构约束概念分解，有望提升跨域组合泛化、可解释性与通用性，且以较低开销适配现有VLM。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models are designed to capture the compositional structure of the world through multi-modal alignment; however, it remains unclear whether their representations truly align with the way a human would naturally describe the same visual input. Prior works attempt to decompose visual embeddings into their concept-level contributions, but their instance-level optimization is inherently local and does not capture the model’s global semantic structure, which is essential for compositional generalization across different inputs, tasks, and domains. We introduce GRACE, a method that learns this concept-based structure at the feature space level, producing semantic and general concept decompositions. To achieve this, we propose a novel training objective that preserves the geometric relations of different samples in the concept space, enabling a better understanding of how concepts compose and relate to each other. We validate GRACE on several image and video benchmarks, showing that its concept decomposition is aligned with human perception and achieves faithful decompositions across different tasks and domains. With minimal overhead, GRACE makes existing Vision-Language Models more interpretable, while preserving most of their original performance.

[60] Driving World Models Move the Camera, Not the Scene cs.CVPDF

Yuqiao Feng, Xiaoyu Ao

TL;DR: 论文指出现有计划条件驾驶世界模型主要让相机跟随计划移动，却几乎不改变场景，常用控制指标也无法检验场景是否真实响应计划。作者提出无需训练的ReTime-Compose，将计划作为几何传递：把记录速度下的帧传输到计划相机，再由冻结生成器补全未覆盖区域，从而提升场景对横向偏移和速度计划的响应。

Details

Motivation: 计划条件驾驶世界模型应用于比较候选驾驶计划，因此预测应像真实场景一样随计划变化；但现有控制指标只检查相机是否跟随命令，忽略了场景是否真实变化，导致模型可能学会移动相机而非改变场景。

Result: 论文发现现有模型对真实距离变化的复现比例最多约为真实视频的三分之一，轨迹分数无法区分执行与忽略速度命令。ReTime-Compose使冻结生成器在KITTI、nuScenes、Argoverse 2上对0.54米横向偏移接近真实场景响应，并在三个生成器中对减速计划、两个生成器中对加速计划优于重采样。

Insight: 创新点在于诊断出失败源于计划通道，且预测几何主要跟随上下文帧；一旦上下文帧携带计划几何，计划token作用有限。方法上提出无需训练、将计划编码为几何的帧传输与冻结生成器补全框架，绕过了对计划条件的重新训练。

Abstract: Plan-conditioned driving world models are used to compare candidate plans, so their forecast should change with the plan as the real scene would, yet widely used control metrics check only whether the camera follows the command. Recorded drives already contain real outcomes of plans that were never driven: retiming a log gives pose-matched images and LiDAR for other speeds, and a stereo rig records a laterally shifted view. Against these references, released world models move their camera but barely move the scene: their scene response, the share of the real change in distance that the forecast reproduces, reaches at most about a third of real video’s, and the trajectory score cannot tell an executed speed command from an ignored one. Interventions inside the generator trace the failure to the plan’s channel. The forecast geometry follows the context frames; once they carry the planned geometry, plan tokens add little. ReTime-Compose therefore delivers the plan as geometry, without training. It transports the frames of one logged-speed rollout to the planned cameras, and the frozen model completes only what no frame covers. Frozen generators then follow a 0.54 m lateral shift nearly as the real scene does on KITTI, nuScenes and Argoverse 2, and respond more than re-sampling to slow-down plans in three generators and to speed-up plans in two.

[61] Track4D: Representing Dense 3D Tracking for Video Diffusion Models cs.CVPDF

Yushi Lan, Zeren Jiang, Kelvin Li, Xingang Pan, Chuanxia Zheng

TL;DR: 论文研究如何复用预训练视频扩散模型（VDM）进行密集3D点跟踪，并系统比较两种将3D跟踪表示为视频信号的方案：RTV直接编码像素相对查询帧的3D偏移，可从生成视频恢复3D轨迹；NCMV编码时间不变的规范坐标，更接近自然视频但需配合现成2D跟踪器后处理。方法基于冻结Wan主干加LoRA，并以现成3D重建器的点图作为几何条件，在多个基准上实现有竞争力的零样本跟踪。

Details

Motivation: 密集3D点跟踪训练数据稀缺，希望利用海量视频预训练得到的VDM先验；难点在于3D跟踪表示成视频信号后可能偏离VDM训练时的自然视频分布。

Result: Track4D仅用单个动态3D跟踪数据集训练，在四个真实和合成基准上取得有竞争力的零样本跟踪结果；RTV比NCMV更准确，绕过视频VAE的回归读出并加入额外训练数据可进一步提升性能。

Insight: 论文系统评估RTV与NCMV两种视频化3D跟踪表示，证明VDM可生成密集对应关系；创新在于用LoRA适配冻结Wan、以点图作几何脚手架，并设计回归读出绕过VAE，为利用VDM先验做密集3D跟踪和4D重建提供分析框架。

Abstract: We study how to repurpose video diffusion models (VDMs), pretrained on millions of videos, for dense 3D point tracking, where training data is scarce. Doing so is non-trivial, as it requires representing 3D tracks as video-like signals that may lie outside the natural-video distribution on which the VDM was trained. We thus systematically assess the effectiveness of two such video representations. The first, Residual 3D Tracking Video (RTV), encodes per-pixel 3D offsets relative to a query frame and allows recovery of 3D trajectories directly from the generated video. The second, Normalized Coordinate Map Video (NCMV), labels each pixel with a canonical coordinate invariant through time. This is closer to a natural video and easier for the VDM to learn, but requires additional post-processing with an off-the-shelf 2D tracker to recover 3D tracks. RTV tracks more accurately, while NCMV shows that a VDM can generate dense correspondence as a video. We instantiate both representations with LoRA adaptation of a frozen Wan backbone, conditioned on point maps from an off-the-shelf 3D reconstructor as a geometric scaffold. Trained with a single dynamic 3D tracking dataset, Track4D achieves competitive zero-shot tracking on four real and synthetic benchmarks. A regression readout that bypasses the video VAE and uses additional training data brings further improvement, demonstrating the potential of this direction for dense 3D tracking. We hope our analysis will inspire future work that leverages VDM priors for dense 3D tracking and 4D reconstruction. Project page: https://nirvanalan.github.io/projects/track4d

Chenyang Li, Qifan Yu, Saihui Hou, Zhaofeng He

TL;DR: 论文针对短视频中隐式、碎片化有害内容难以检测的问题，构建了包含7,110个样本的细粒度有害短视频数据集DeepHarm-7K，并提出融合视觉、音频与跨模态推理的多模态检测框架DeepHarm-VL。该方法采用两轮推理策略，先识别表层证据，再推断潜在有害含义，从而同时检测显式和隐式有害短视频。

Details

Motivation: 现有研究多关注暴力、仇恨言论等显式有害类别，难以识别通过叙事语境、情绪框架或跨模态线索隐式传递误导价值观的短视频，且缺乏专门的隐式危害评测基准。

Result: 实验表明，DeepHarm-VL在多个开源视频语言模型上相较强基线和现有最先进方法均取得一致提升，验证了其在显式和隐式有害短视频检测上的有效性；摘要未给出具体数值指标。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于构建覆盖多场景、采用细粒度分类和多阶段质量控制的大规模隐式有害短视频数据集，并提出先表层证据后潜在危害推断的两轮跨模态推理检测框架。

Abstract: Short videos have become a dominant online medium, where harmful content is increasingly conveyed in implicit and fragmented forms. Prior work largely focuses on explicit categories, such as violence and hate speech, and often fails to detect videos that subtly promote misleading values through narrative context, emotional framing, or cross-modal cues, partly due to the lack of dedicated benchmarks for implicit harm. To bridge this gap, we construct DeepHarm-7K, a large-scale harmful short-video dataset comprising 7,110 samples annotated according to a fine-grained harmful-content taxonomy. The dataset systematically covers implicitly harmful videos across diverse real-world scenarios and is constructed through a rigorous multi-stage quality-control process. Building on this dataset, we propose DeepHarm-VL, a multimodal detection framework that integrates visual, audio, and cross-modal reasoning. The framework employs a two-round reasoning strategy to progressively identify surface-level evidence and infer latent harmful implications. Experimental results show consistent improvements over strong baselines and state-of-the-art methods across multiple open-source video-language models, demonstrating its effectiveness in detecting both explicit and implicit harmful short videos.

[63] AcrossPlace: Do Vision–Language Models Recognize Places Beyond Their Source Views? A Comprehensive Benchmark cs.CVPDF

Zhenyu Li, Tianyi Shang, Shuhan Dong, Ruotong Xu, Qingyu Zhu

TL;DR: 论文提出 AcrossPlace，一个带有来源溯源信息的语言-视觉地点识别基准，用于系统评估模型能否在不同于描述来源图像的观测中恢复地点身份。该基准基于 GSV-Cities 构建，覆盖 23 个城市、56,726 个地点、156,800 条描述和 113,404 张目标图像，支持文本到图像与图像到文本的双向检索，并设置 P0（已见城市内未见地点）和 P1（未见城市泛化）两种地理协议。论文通过配对观测、画廊控制、固定查询规模和标注审计等实验揭示现有检索分数会掩盖观测迁移与画廊竞争带来的性能下降。

Details

Motivation: 现有语言引导地点识别常用聚合检索分数评价，但该分数混合了观测迁移、候选画廊竞争和语言证据等因素，难以真实反映模型跨视觉观测和跨地理环境的地点识别能力。论文希望建立更系统、可复现的评估标准来分离并刻画这些因素。

Result: 在 P0 协议下，地点监督的 SigLIP2 文本到图像 Recall@1 从源图像的 19.69% 降到独立目标图像的 12.31%（三个训练种子）。在 P1 固定查询下，画廊从 500 个地点扩展到 8,425 个地点时 Recall@1 从 14.67% 降至 2.87%；240 条描述审计还发现自动接收结果与事实证据存在偏差。

Insight: 创新点在于构建带来源溯源的双向语言-视觉地点识别基准，将描述来源图像与检索目标观测分离，并引入统一的地点身份相关性准则以支持多有效匹配。其价值还体现在 P0/P1 地理协议及画廊控制、固定查询扩展和标注审计等系统化分析，客观揭示了观测迁移和候选规模对地点识别性能的显著影响。

Abstract: Language-guided place recognition requires recovering geographic identity across visual observations beyond the image from which a description originates. However, aggregate retrieval scores often entangle observation transfer, gallery competition, and linguistic evidence, obscuring the underlying recognition capability. We introduce AcrossPlace, a provenance-aware benchmark establishing a systematic evaluation standard for bidirectional language–vision place recognition. Built upon GSV-Cities, AcrossPlace comprises 56,726 places across 23 cities, 156,800 automatically generated, rule-filtered descriptions, and 113,404 target images. Each description retains its source-image provenance, while retrieval operates on disjoint target observations. Text-to-image (T2I) and image-to-text (I2T) retrieval share a unified place-identity relevance criterion supporting multiple valid matches. Two geographic protocols evaluate unseen places within represented cities (P0) and generalization to held-out cities (P1). Paired observation comparisons, gallery-controlled evaluations, fixed-query scaling, and blinded annotation auditing systematically characterize retrieval performance. Across three training seeds, place-supervised SigLIP2 T2I Recall@1 decreases from 19.69% on source images to 12.31% on independent targets under P0. For fixed P1 queries, expanding the gallery from 500 to 8,425 places reduces Recall@1 from 14.67% to 2.87%. A 240-description audit further reveals discrepancies between automated acceptance and factual evidence. These findings establish AcrossPlace as a reproducible evaluation standard for assessing transferable place identity across visual observations and geographic environments.

[64] CaseScope: A Multi-Field Benchmark for Case-Level Pathology Diagnosis with Multimodal LLMs cs.CVPDF

Wenchuan Zhang, Chengyang Zhang, Fengling Li, Ting Yin, Chen Shu

TL;DR: 论文提出 CaseScope，一个面向多模态大模型病例级病理诊断的多视野基准，包含来自住院医师培训图库的 CaseScope-Teaching（436 例）和来自 88 本出版图谱的 CaseScope-Atlas（800 例），覆盖 18 个器官系统、843 个实体和 6 类任务共 4,679 个问题。作者系统评测了 28 个开源和 4 个专有 MLLM，发现模型在多视野病理诊断上仍不稳定，错误常偏向经典相似病变，且开源模型对额外视野的利用有限。

Details

Motivation: 真实病理诊断需要病理医生在低倍镜下观察组织结构、放大选定视野观察细胞学，并整合多视野多尺度证据；然而现有 MLLM 病理基准多基于单图或全切片流程，少数多视野基准也仅来自单一公开队列的癌症切片嵌套区域，难以反映病例级多视野诊断能力。

Result: 在 Teaching 病例中，最佳开源和专有模型在十种选项轮换下全部正确的比例分别仅为 17.9% 和 61.7%，错误答案偏向经典相似病变；开源模型对额外视野利用较少，而 Teaching 中标本部位信息更重要，Atlas 中三视野以上时视野数与部位信息作用相近。开源模型验证形态学发现的中位准确率仅 62.0%，最佳 81.5%，图像带来中位 +3.4 分提升，专有模型达到 83.2–93.2%，且两个数据来源上的模型排名大体一致。

Insight: 论文的创新在于构建多视野、病例级、跨器官系统的病理诊断基准，并通过 WHO 本体论协调、病理医生复核以及双 LLM 标注一致性来提升数据质量；任务设计包含开放式诊断、十分类诊断、本体引导 hard negatives、循环评估及形态学发现验证与定位。客观来看，其价值主要在于揭示当前 MLLM 在多视野证据整合和诊断稳定性上的不足，并为病例级病理诊断评测提供了更贴近临床工作流的测试框架。

Abstract: Pathologists rarely diagnose from a single image: they scan a section at low power for architecture, zoom into selected fields for cytology, and integrate the evidence across views and scales. Pathology benchmarks for multimodal large language models (MLLMs), however, mostly ask about one image or require whole-slide pipelines, and the few that show several fields draw nested regions from cancer slides of one public cohort. We introduce CaseScope, a benchmark of diagnosis from several curated fields of one lesion: CaseScope-Teaching, 436 cases from a residency-training image library, reconstructed, de-identified, harmonised against a WHO-based ontology and re-examined by a pathologist wherever an automated pre-review raised a doubt, and CaseScope-Atlas, 800 cases from 88 published atlases (387 of them figure series of one disease), labelled from the captions by two LLMs that must agree. They cover 843 entities across 18 organ systems and pose 4,679 items in six tasks, from open-ended and ten-way diagnosis with ontology-guided hard negatives under circular evaluation to the verification and location of morphological findings. Across twenty-eight open and four proprietary MLLMs, diagnoses are unstable – the best open and proprietary models are correct under all ten option rotations on 17.9% and 61.7% of the teaching cases – and wrong answers favour the classic look-alike; open models make little use of the additional fields on teaching cases, where the one-line specimen site matters more, although on atlas cases with three or more fields the fields count about as much as the site; and most open models verify findings barely above chance (median 62.0% vs. 50%, best 81.5%), gaining a median +3.4 points from images; proprietary models reach 83.2-93.2%. Rankings broadly replicate between the sources. The evaluation code is available at https://github.com/Wenchuan-Zhang/CaseScope.

Abstract: Multilingual Text-Based Person Retrieval (TBPR) aims to retrieve individuals with texts in multiple languages. Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models bring multimodal understanding and world knowledge to end-to-end autonomous driving. To further improve the quality of predicted trajectories, existing studies introduce candidate refinement, residual denoising, and token correction. However, these methods typically focus on unified refinement of the entire trajectory, paying limited attention to the distinct physical quantities and their interactions. We further investigate physical specialization and coordination in trajectory refinement, centering on position and heading for simpler and more intuitive correction modeling. To this end, we propose CARE-Drive (Coupled Alternating Refinement for Ego-trajectories), which decomposes trajectory refinement into waypoint residual correction and position-conditioned heading generation. Through imitation learning and a future-token auxiliary task, the backbone provides predictive representations and a base trajectory. The position expert predicts bounded metric waypoint residuals, while the heading expert reconstructs vehicle orientation from adjacent angular increments conditioned on the corrected path. Abstract: Reconstructing video from degraded measurements requires priors that capture both frame appearance and temporal dynamics. Many recent approaches combine pretrained image diffusion priors with motion guidance, sidestepping the costs of training large video models. Temporal consistency is often enforced via photometric losses or warping-based constraints, which implicitly assume that motion explains frame-to-frame or latent-to-latent changes up to small deviations, an assumption that fails under occlusions, lighting changes, and non-rigid deformation. In this paper, we propose to learn the coupling between motion and frame appearance as a generative prior. We learn the distribution of motion-induced appearance residuals with a conditional diffusion model and use it to regularize reconstruction in video inverse problems. Our motion-appearance prior acts as a plug-in regularizer for diffusion-based video reconstruction, preserving the benefits of image diffusion models while remaining compatible with existing appearance regularization techniques. Experiments on dynamic video inverse problems show that reconstruction with a learnt motion-appearance coupling prior outperforms photometric-consistency and noise-warping baselines, and that the approach also transfers to cardiac MRI reconstruction.

Abstract: Scene graphs provide a compact, structured representation of environments by capturing objects and their spatial or semantic relationships in a form suitable for embodied agents. Existing 3D scene graph generation methods often rely on depth sensing at inference, either to associate object instances across views via point cloud overlap or to compute inter-object relations from geometric cues, limiting deployment to platforms with depth sensors. We propose IncR-3DSGG, which builds 3D scene graphs incrementally from posed\footnote{RGB images with camera extrinsics estimated by an off-the-shelf visual SLAM system.} RGB images, eliminating the use of depth sensors at inference. Our method leverages a Parametric Relation Predictor (PRP) that learns to estimate pairwise distances and relative angles between objects from 2D visual features and 2D geometric cues, using 3D geometric supervision available only during training. These learned pairwise measurements are fused across views through Pose Graph Optimization (PGO)\cite{grisetti2010tutorial} to recover metrically grounded 3D object positions and build a globally consistent 3D scene graph incrementally. On the real-world 3RScan\cite{wald2019rio} dataset, our method attains $52.2$ mean object recall and $30.8$ mean predicate recall under the 3DSSG\cite{wald2020learning} evaluation protocol, against $27.0$ and $17.6$ for MonoSSG\cite{wu2023incremental}, while using no depth or reconstructed geometry at inference. Abstract: Automatic evaluation is now central to text-to-image benchmarking, but the verifier that converts generated images into scores is often treated as an implementation detail. Motivation: 传统 PnP 多关注单一位姿估计，难以量化 6-DoF 相机位姿的不确定性，尤其当后验存在多模态或替代假设时。论文旨在用非参数粒子表示完整后验，并解决 SVI 直接用于 PnP 时漏检位姿假设和概率权重不准确的问题。

Abstract: We propose SteinPnP, a probabilistic Perspective- N-Point (PnP) solver that models the full 6-DoF camera pose posterior using a non-parametric set of pose particles. By lever- aging Stein Variational Inference (SVI), SteinPnP combines the expressivity of particle-based representations with the efficiency of optimization-based solvers. However, a naive application of SVI struggles in the PnP problem, often missing alternative pose hypotheses or assigning incorrect probability weights to them. To address these limitations, we propose practical remedies that discover new potential poses, identify distinct hypothesis clusters, and balance their relative probability weights to capture the true underlying distribution. Our experiments across synthetic and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that SteinPnP accurately captures complex multimodal uncertainties while maintaining competitive accuracy.

Abstract: In computer vision, vision-language and self-supervised encoders are two unique paradigms characterized by global language alignment and high-quality vision-only patch representations, respectively. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often use the former because of language alignment, despite the recent rise in vision-centric tasks resembling traditional dense vision tasks that would likely benefit from the high-quality patch representations, raising the question whether self-supervised models could aid MLLMs in vision-centric tasks. To investigate this, we define semantic (in)consistency of patch tokens from language-aligned encoders and identify two sets of tokens, semantically consistent (SCTs) and semantically inconsistent (SITs). Through systematic probing via the removal of SCTs or SITs from the input of modern MLLMs, we show higher reliance on SCTs than SITs, as removal of the former induces larger performance drops. This demonstrates that MLLMs benefit from semantic consistency of vision tokens. With this in mind, we devise a simple yet effective extension of the MLLM training objective that combines traditional language modeling with semantic guidance via minimization of semantic inconsistency. This strategy, named Semantically-Guided Training (SGT), combines the signals of both loss terms to push the vision encoder towards providing semantically consistent tokens, while preserving language alignment. Extensive evaluation shows that SGT outperforms all baselines and MLLMs with specialized vision encoders on vision-centric benchmarks while keeping strong scores on text-centric benchmarks, thus setting state of the art on average across all tasks. Our results highlight the importance of combining language alignment and semantic consistency through the lens of MLLMs rather than independently from them and pave the way towards the design of better MLLM vision encoders.

Abstract: Text-aware image super-resolution (TAISR) aims to recover high-resolution images from low-resolution inputs while preserving textual content. Existing methods treat this as a visual restoration problem, yet severely degraded text often cannot be read from local visual evidence alone. Humans instead read it by reasoning over image context, logical patterns, and prior knowledge. This raises a fundamental question: Does TAISR need reasoning? To answer this, we introduce two diagnostic tools. The first, ReasonText, is a human-curated benchmark that separates reasoning-required text from locally recoverable text. The second, Ground-Truth Text-Crop Accuracy (GTTCA), is a recognition-based metric that measures text restoration quality without text detection errors. Evaluating twelve recent super-resolution (SR) models, we find a consistent and substantial performance drop on reasoning-required text, revealing a systematic blind spot. Two observations point to a remedy. One is that text-conditioned SR models restore text substantially better when given ground-truth text. The other is that multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can read degraded text in low-resolution images through reasoning. We therefore propose Reasoning Transfer via Captioning (RTC), a training-free plug-in that uses an external MLLM to infer the degraded text and feeds the resulting caption to a text-conditioned SR model. RTC consistently improves all six text-conditioned SR models we evaluate, especially on reasoning-required text. Surprisingly, a real-world SR model with RTC can even outperform the TAISR model with RTC, suggesting that reasoning should be a core design axis for future TAISR.

[73] Real Pairs Are Not Enough: Physically Anchored Supervision for Volumetric CT Super-Resolution cs.CV | eess.IVPDF

TL;DR: 论文针对体数据CT超分辨率中合成LR-HR对缺乏真实采集效应、配准后的真实物理对又含跨扫描噪声和微结构差异，导致体素级回归过度平滑的问题，提出物理锚定隐式网络PIN与真实对双锚学习。方法将物理对监督解耦为HR锚和LR锚，并结合精确合成监督训练共享体数据隐式模型；推理仅需真实低分辨率CT。实验在真实CT上优于多组基线，显著改善感知质量、表面距离、骨体积分数误差和孔隙保持。

Details

Motivation: 现有体数据CT超分辨率依赖合成对或配准的真实物理对训练，但前者缺少真实采集效应，后者存在噪声、伪影和微结构不匹配，直接体素级回归甚至简单混合合成与真实数据都会过度平滑细孔结构。

Result: 在真实CT的30组外部基线配置中，PIN取得最低的全体积LPIPS；相较各指标最强的三个基线，平均对称表面距离降低42.3-49.5%，骨体积分数误差降低52.2-53.4%，并将参考孔隙被擦除的体积分数从37.3-72.9%降至10.4%。

Insight: 创新点在于用真实对双锚解耦物理监督：HR锚通过配对条件谱重放学习细结构，LR锚通过仿射校准低通投影约束预测，并与精确合成监督统一到一个共享体数据隐式模型中，避免强制真实LR重建逐体素匹配配准HR。客观看，其价值在于将不完美真实配对转化为互补约束，而非直接回归，从而兼顾真实采集特性与细结构保持。

Abstract: Volumetric CT super-resolution (SR) models are commonly trained on synthetic or physically acquired pairs of low-resolution (LR) and high-resolution (HR) volumes. However, synthetic pairs miss real acquisition effects, whereas registered physical pairs retain inter-scan noise, artifact, and microstructure differences—causing direct voxelwise regression, and even naive synthetic-real mixing, to oversmooth fine porous structures. We introduce the Physically-anchored Implicit Network (PIN) with Real-Pair Dual-Anchor learning, which decouples physical-pair supervision into two complementary roles. The HR anchor teaches fine structure from registered HR through pair-conditioned spectral replay, while the LR anchor constrains the prediction from acquired LR via affine-calibrated low-pass projection. Together with exact synthetic supervision in one shared volumetric implicit model, PIN learns from both physical scans without forcing the acquired-LR reconstruction to match registered HR voxel by voxel. Inference requires only acquired LR. Across 30 external baseline configurations on real CT, PIN achieves the lowest full-volume LPIPS, reduces average symmetric surface distance by 42.3-49.5% and bone-volume-fraction error by 52.2-53.4% relative to the three strongest baselines for each metric, and lowers the erased reference-pore volume fraction from 37.3-72.9% to 10.4%. Code, pretrained models, and evaluation scripts will be publicly available.

Details

Abstract: Mamba-based methods for video-based 3D human pose estimation can model long sequences with linear complexity, but their selective state updates usually lack direct exploitation of skeletal relations. Meanwhile, stacking modeling blocks at the full temporal resolution leads to high computational overhead, while compressing the temporal sequence makes it difficult to retain per-frame details. To address these issues, we propose SCR-Mamba, whose core consists of Skeleton-Conditioned State Modeling (SCSM) and Cross-Scale Incremental Refinement (CSIR). SCSM generates a skeleton context from bone connections and left-right symmetry relations and generates the selective parameters of the spatial and temporal branches conditioned on it, so that structural relations are involved in the writing, propagation, and reading of state information. CSIR constructs multi-scale motion representations through content-adaptive aggregation and progressively refines the retained per-frame features with the increments produced by coarse-scale modeling, integrating long-range motion context while reducing full-resolution computation. On Human3.6M with Stacked Hourglass (SH) 2D detections and 243-frame inputs, SCR-Mamba-L achieves 36.8 mm MPJPE (P1) with 7.72 M parameters and 15.10 G MACs, less than 1/7 of the computational cost of StructMamPose-L with similar accuracy; SCR-Mamba-X further reaches 36.6 mm P1 and 30.9 mm P-MPJPE (P2), achieving state-of-the-art performance. Abstract: Recent advances in medical large multimodal models (LMMs) have brought promising progress to clinical decision support, yet hallucination, the generation of confident but clinically unverifiable outputs, poses a critical threat to the reliability demanded in high-stakes medical settings. Existing hallucination detection methods, predominantly designed for unimodal settings, fail to capture the visual-linguistic dynamics inherent to medical imaging. We formulate the problem of hallucination detection in LMMs as disproportionate distribution of attention weights across the modalities during answer token generation. We quantify modality-specific attention concentration across layers and heads using the Gini coefficient and demonstrate statistically significant redistribution of attention mass between visual and textual tokens in hallucinated responses. We leverage these features to train a logistic regression-based classifier and evaluate it on two medical LMMs across three medical imaging modalities, demonstrating that our method achieves competitive or superior performance against contemporary hallucination detection baselines.

TL;DR: 论文提出 TraceKV，一种面向推理型视觉语言模型的免训练低秩 KV 缓存压缩框架。它针对推理过程中 KV 状态压缩导致的策略漂移问题，统一采用 on-policy 校准、敏感度感知 SVD 和全局秩分配来保持模型策略，从而在显著压缩 KV 缓存的同时尽量维持推理准确率。

Details

Motivation: 推理型 VLM 同时面临多模态长上下文和不断增长的推理轨迹带来的 KV 缓存开销。现有低秩 KV 压缩方法在压缩推理 KV 状态时会产生逐渐增大的预测策略漂移，导致推理失败。

Result: 在多个 VLM 和视觉推理基准上取得更优的准确率-内存权衡；结合量化可在 5.64 倍 KV 缓存压缩下实现近无损推理准确率，优化低秩注意力核还带来最高 1.83 倍端到端解码加速。

Insight: 创新点在于从策略保持角度统一低秩 KV 压缩流程，指出静态上下文与 on-policy 推理状态之间的校准不匹配、以及不同 KV 状态误差敏感度不均两个关键问题，并通过 on-policy 校准、敏感度感知 SVD 和全局秩分配降低策略 KL 散度。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language models (VLMs) impose substantial KV cache demands from both large multimodal contexts and growing reasoning traces. Low-rank KV compression has emerged as a promising paradigm that stores low-dimensional latents of KV states and can be combined with quantization. However, existing low-rank methods often induce growing predictive policy drift from the dense model when compressing reasoning KV states, resulting in reasoning failures. We identify two key causes of this degradation: calibration-state mismatch between static contexts and on-policy reasoning states, and unequal error sensitivity across KV states. To address these challenges, we propose TraceKV, a training-free low-rank KV compression framework that unifies on-policy calibration, sensitivity-aware SVD, and global rank allocation under a single policy-preserving principle, substantially reducing policy-KL divergence during reasoning. Experiments across multiple VLMs and visual reasoning benchmarks demonstrate superior accuracy–memory trade-offs, achieving near-lossless reasoning accuracy with 5.64$\times$ KV cache compression when combined with quantization. Optimized low-rank attention kernels further deliver up to 1.83$\times$ end-to-end decoding speedup. Code will be available at https://github.com/AK-DREAM/TraceKV.

Abstract: Deploying real-time object detection on power-constrained edge platforms is challenging as processing full-resolution frames is often computationally redundant. TL;DR: 论文提出 WaveletLoRA，一种面向个性化图像生成的频率感知内容-风格解耦框架。它先用多级离散小波变换将参考图分解为低频和高频子带，并为每个子带分配独立 LoRA 分支：低频分支建模全局外观以获取风格，高频分支捕捉结构细节以保留内容；同时引入稠密 Mixture-of-Experts 聚合模块和频域正则项减少风格泄漏、提升内容保真度。论文还构建了 WaveBench 及基于 VLM 的解耦评估协议。

Motivation: 现有单图内容-风格解耦方法主要在空间域操作，未显式利用内容与风格天然对应的不同频率特性，难以将主体身份与视觉风格灵活分离和重组。

Result: 摘要称大量实验表明 WaveletLoRA 持续优于现有方法，生成结果更符合人类偏好；并建立 WaveBench 和基于 VLM 的评估协议，从属性相似性与属性分离性衡量解耦效果。

Insight: 创新点在于用离散小波子带显式监督内容-风格解耦，将低频外观风格与高频结构内容分别建模到独立 LoRA 分支，并通过 MoE 聚合和频域正则增强解耦质量；同时提出配套 benchmark 与评估协议，具有较好的可借鉴性。

Abstract: Content-style decomposition from a single image is a fundamental challenge in personalized image generation, aiming to separate subject identity from visual style for flexible recomposition. Existing approaches primarily operate in the spatial domain, without explicitly exploiting the distinct frequency characteristics naturally associated with content and style. In this paper, we propose WaveletLoRA, a novel frequency-aware framework for explicit content-style decomposition through wavelet-domain supervision. Specifically, we first apply a multi-level discrete wavelet transform (DWT) to decompose the reference image into low-frequency and high-frequency subbands. We then assign a dedicated LoRA branch to each subband, where the low-frequency branch models global appearance for style acquisition, while the high-frequency branches capture structural details for content preservation. To further improve disentanglement quality, we introduce a dense Mixture-of-Experts aggregation module together with a frequency-domain regularization objective, which reduces style leakage and enhance content fidelity. In addition, we establish WaveBench and develop a VLM-based evaluation protocol that explicitly measures both attribute similarity and attribute separation for disentanglement assessment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that WaveletLoRA consistently outperforms existing methods and produces results that better align with human preference.

Abstract: Streaming video understanding aims to continuously process incoming visual observations, making the retention and retrieval of relevant information increasingly important as the video history grows. Although existing benchmarks include memory-related questions, question-answering accuracy alone does not reliably measure memory capabilities. Incorrect predictions may arise from failures in visual understanding rather than forgetting, whereas correct predictions may rely on linguistic priors or recent observations without requiring historical memory. To address these ambiguities, we introduce MemInStream, a benchmark dedicated to evaluating memory capabilities in streaming video understanding. We develop an evidence-controlled validation protocol that associates each question with temporally localized evidence intervals. The protocol validates evidence solvability and memory necessity by retaining questions that models can answer from the annotated evidence but cannot resolve using recent observations, post-evidence observations, or textual input alone. This helps disentangle memory limitations from understanding failures while reducing memory-independent shortcuts. MemInStream provides a systematic evaluation of memory capabilities through four major categories, covering Episodic Retrieval, State Tracking & Updating, Multi-Evidence Integration, and Contextual Memory across diverse video scenarios. Overall, \textsc{MemInStream} provides a comprehensive and reliable benchmark for systematically evaluating memory capabilities in streaming video understanding.

Abstract: We present a training-free system for the EBMV @ ECCV 2026 challenge “Event Stream Understanding with EventBench” (General Understanding track), which evaluates vision-language models on five event-camera understanding tasks. We use none of the challenge’s training data: our goal is to measure how far off-the-shelf, RGB-pretrained models can go on event data through representation and prompting alone. For four of the five categories (gesture recognition, detailed understanding, causal reasoning, and event OCR) we render event bins as red/ON, blue/OFF frames, select a category-specific subset of bins, stitch them into a single grid image, and query the same frozen Qwen3.5 9B vision-language model with a category-specific prompt for all four tasks. The fifth category, action recognition, feeds every bin directly into the organizers’ EventGPT-Plus-2B. That analysis shows that our bounding-box crop almost never tightened around content, so event OCR text reached the model at roughly one fifth of its horizontal resolution, and that detailed-understanding predictions are skewed toward the first two answer options.

[84] Prior-as-Skill: Remember Outside and Read Inside with On-Policy Self-Distillation for Self-Evolving VLM Agents cs.CV | cs.AIPDF

Yan Lin, Jingyu Sun, Yifei Qian, Zhengtao Yao, Yuxia Wang

TL;DR: 论文面向自进化视觉语言模型智能体，关注其将任务步骤顺序作为技能内化后，在部署时遇到关键帧缺失或步骤顺序异常时容易用记忆覆盖视觉证据的问题。作者提出 PriorSplit：不额外训练，仅在 OPSD 基础上把步骤先后顺序作为外部统计先验保存在模型外；推理时先判断两个被问步骤是否都在画面中，若在则由帧回答，否则由外部计数先验回答。方法在多个模型和 web、cooking、egocentric 任务上验证了帧缺失时的鲁棒性。

Details

Motivation: 现有 on-policy self-distillation 将步骤顺序写入帧阅读权重，导致模型在帧缺失时靠猜测、在训练遮挡帧时又可能让记忆覆盖所见，难以应对部署中帧缺失或顺序被重排。论文希望分离外部顺序先验与内部视觉读取，提升自进化 VLM 智能体的鲁棒性。

Result: 在合成技能任务上使用五个模型，并在已见 web、cooking、egocentric 任务的新 episode 的 step-tagged order 问题上评估；帧缺失时 PriorSplit 在 OPSD 同等训练成本下优于所有对比方法。Qwen3-VL-4B 在合成技能的不寻常顺序上也接近 OPSD，而依赖存储顺序的方法表现落后。

Insight: 创新点是把“顺序记忆”作为模型外部的可计数先验（prior-as-skill），模型内部只负责读取当前帧，并通过“两个步骤是否都在视野中”进行路由：可见则视觉回答，不可见则先验回答。客观看，这种记忆/感知解耦避免了 OPSD 内化顺序造成的记忆覆盖感知，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Self-evolving visual agents keep what they learn as skills: key frames with step notes on a task’s steps and their order. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is now a popular way to train such skills into a vision-language model that matches, on its own answers, a teacher that also reads the written order. At deployment, frames often go missing or show an unusual order, as when a person or a website reorders the steps. Existing internalization methods keep any order they store in the frame-reading weights: trained on full frames, the model guesses when they are missing, and trained with frames withheld, it stores the order, so memory can override what it sees. We propose PriorSplit, which fills in missing frames from an order kept outside the model, with no training beyond OPSD. It counts which of two neighboring steps comes first in training episodes, and the model checks if both asked steps are in view: if so, it answers from the frames, and otherwise the counts answer. Extensive experiments on synthetic skills with five models and on step-tagged order questions in new episodes of seen web, cooking and egocentric tasks demonstrate that, with frames missing, PriorSplit outperforms every compared method at OPSD’s training cost. With Qwen3-VL-4B on synthetic skills, it also stays close to OPSD on unusual orders, where methods that gain most by storing the order fall behind.

Abstract: Controllable real-world video generation models can condition on a rendered graybox proxy video, which fixes layout, motion and camera. Coding agents can build such videos by reconstructing a 4D scene in Blender, yet no benchmark checks both these videos and the long, multi-shot scenes behind them. We introduce VibePrevisBench, a benchmark for 4D scene reconstruction: an agent turns a brief into an executable 4D scene and renders its proxy video. Its 242 tasks span four input kinds, including scripts, photos, continuations, and reference videos. Each delivery is scored along two evaluation dimensions. Rule-based Scene Check re-runs the code and compares the exported 4D scene with hidden ground truth facts in nine dimensions. VLM-based Screen Check asks a VLM judge whether the facts that pass can be recognized in the proxy video. We analyze the evaluation results and trajectories of 11 frontier coding agents and identify three key observations. First, multimodal understanding separates the agents, especially in understanding space and camera motion. Second, all agents seem to already master basic camera work and staging, and fail at challenging requests. Third, agents inspect their own renders but fail to correct their errors. Agents thus need better visual understanding of space and camera, explicit checks for challenging requests, and item-by-item verification of renders against the brief.

However, Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), despite their impressive captioning capabilities, are prone to hallucination, producing descriptions that are inconsistent with the visual input. TL;DR: 论文提出 Lang-SVG，一种带语言先验的层级化图像矢量化方法。它将 SVG 基元组织为带语义标签和父子关系的显式树结构，利用粒度可控的开放词汇分割先验初始化对象—部件层级，并用多模态大语言模型为未标注节点分配语义标签。为降低冗余，方法引入深度自适应简化、树引导剪枝与合并，以及 L1-SAM-Prompting 恢复缺失区域。论文还提出衡量结构质量和语义部件对齐的新评估协议。

Details

Motivation: 现有图像矢量化方法主要优化像素级渲染保真度，生成的 SVG 往往缺乏与人类感知一致的对象—部件层级，语义可解释性和可编辑性不足，难以进行结构化操作。

Result: 实验表明 Lang-SVG 在渲染保真度和结构质量上均达到 state-of-the-art 水平；同时论文提出新的评估协议，用于衡量 SVG 的结构质量和语义部件对齐程度。

Abstract: Image vectorization reconstructs raster images as compact scalable vector graph- ics (SVG), providing a structured and editable code representation of images. However, most existing vectorization methods optimize for pixel-level render- ing fidelity, often producing SVGs that lack alignment with human-perceived object-part hierarchies, making them difficult to manipulate. To address this, we propose Lang-SVG, which introduces an explicit tree structure to organize SVG primitives with semantic labels and parent-child relationships. Lang-SVG incorporates granularity-controllable and open-vocabulary segmentation priors to initialize object-part hierarchies and leverages multimodal large language models (MLLMs) with SVG tree context to assign semantic labels to unlabeled nodes. To reduce redundancy, we introduce depth-adaptive simplification to reduce shape complexity and Tree-Guided Pruning and Merging to eliminate redundant SVG nodes. L1-SAM-Prompting further recovers missing regions to improve recon- struction fidelity and structural completeness. Beyond reconstruction fidelity, we introduce a new evaluation protocol that measures structural quality and semantic part alignment. Experiments demonstrate that Lang-SVG achieves state-of-the-art performance in both rendering fidelity and structural quality. Project website: https://xiliu8006.github.io/Lang-SVG/.

Abstract: More text-to-video models are available, but many remain limited to low resolutions. Training them for higher resolutions is costly, while existing training-free methods still struggle with fine detail, scene structure, and efficiency. We propose TRACE, a training-free method that synchronizes low- and high-resolution generation trajectories. During early denoising, TRACE aligns predicted scene content at corresponding noise levels. It computes high-resolution updates from low-resolution predictions and the current high-resolution state, without high-resolution model inference. The high-resolution trajectory retains fine-grained variation for generating new details. Local window attention then refines fine details in a shared high-resolution latent state. TRACE checks suspected duplicate subjects against the corresponding low-resolution layout and corrects selected regions and their solver history while preserving detail elsewhere. Abstract: Satellite constellations now image the Earth daily, capturing the lifecycle of events from deforestation to urban construction. Anticipating these events before completion enables timely intervention, yet existing systems cannot jointly identify where a change occurs, what it will become, and when it will finish across an open vocabulary. Building such a model requires diverse event data, but events are like needles in a haystack, making manual annotation at global scale infeasible. We address this problem by representing change as the vector difference between vision-language model embeddings at distinct time points. This arithmetic approach allows us to search for semantic transformations directly in the latent space, enabling (1) an automated data engine for global event discovery without manual annotation and (2) the largest global satellite event dataset to our knowledge, comprising 21,000+ locations across 64+ event types with ~2M images from PlanetScope and Sentinel-2 spanning six continents, to train (3) a novel global-scale event anticipation model. Abstract: Language moved from many task-specific models to one unified model: a single generative model with a single generative training task that handles all of them within the same backbone, and keeps improving with data, parameters and compute. We believe the bitter lesson applies just as much to computer vision, 3D and spatial understanding, in the end to visual intelligence: the effort should go into general architectures that solve all these tasks in a unified way, and into scaling them, rather than into task-specific designs. We test this on monocular geometry, a task that has belonged to specialised architectures for years. Iris-Z is a general image generator that produces appearance and geometry together. A single backbone denoises, reasons about and understands the image and its depth at the same time, and one set of weights generates images with their depth, estimates depth from photographs and paints images onto a given depth. At enough scale it surpasses state-of-the-art specialists on established depth benchmarks while keeping the generative abilities of the backbone it started from without specialized layers. We release two models for the community.

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic scenes and objects from monocular egocentric video is crucial for embodied AI, yet existing 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) approaches struggle with everyday interactions involving topological changes, such as cutting, assembly, opening, or closing. Since 4DGS methods rely on deforming a fixed representation, events that split, merge, or reveal surfaces cause severe visual artifacts. To address this challenge, we introduce TopoEgo, the first benchmark dedicated to topological changes in egocentric video (covering fission, fusion, open, and close), alongside Topo-4DGS, a topology-aware reconstruction method. Abstract: Our objective is to enhance VLMs ability to distinguish the roles of the left and right hands in egocentric actions. We introduce sub-action detection, a task that requires models to explicitly recognise the left- and right-hand roles and their manipulated objects within an ongoing high-level action. To address this challenge, we propose BiScopeNet, a framework that augments existing video-LLMs without modifying their vision encoders or language models. BiScopeNet incorporates two components: FocusAlign, a gated text-guided attention-based module that selectively aligns video features with textual context to emphasise action-relevant cues, and MetaLoss, a masked, object-weighted loss that prioritises visually grounded terms. Abstract: Potholes impose billions of dollars in annual vehicle damage, yet detectors report only where potholes are, not how severe they are or what to do. We present a five-stage modular framework for the offline assessment of already-detected potholes that combines geometric measurements with VLM reasoning: perspective transformation, zero-shot SAM segmentation, geometric feature extraction, Gemini 2.5 Pro severity classification with composite risk scoring, and natural-language warning generation. Severity agreement with a prediction-free three-annotator panel is weak (Cohen’s $κ= 0.139$, 95% CI [0.00, 0.28]; $n = 101$) and far below the annotators’ agreement with one another ($κ= 0.605$); against physically measured depth on an independent RGB-D dataset, it is also weak ($κ= 0.098$), although the predicted classes preserve the order of measured depths. TL;DR: 论文提出WorldDraft基准，用于评估在由代码/多模态编码智能体构建4D世界后，视频模型能否作为生成式渲染器可靠地渲染该世界。该基准包含432个严格配对测试用例、48个可执行4D世界族和6类动态挑战，并以3×3因子设计交叉控制草稿粒度与外观对齐程度。作者评测12个代表性模型，揭示世界保持与视觉实现之间的张力及源线索泄漏问题，并提出即插即用的WorldDraft-Primer视觉提示策略以提升冻结视频渲染器的表现。

Insight: 摘要宣称的创新点在于提出“代码建世界、视频做渲染”的解耦评测框架，以及通过配对因子设计区分4D动力学保持与外观实现能力。客观看，其价值主要在于系统暴露跨模态解耦脆弱性与源泄漏问题，并提供简单可插拔的视觉提示缓解方案，为程序化4D仿真与视频生成结合建立评测基础。

Abstract: Multimodal coding agents decouple 4D world construction from visual generation, repurposing video models from world generators toward generative renderers. This division of labor raises a fundamental question: if code builds the world, can video models just render it? A reliable generative renderer hinges on two complementary criteria: (i) world preservation, retaining prescribed 4D kinematics and camera paths; and (ii) visual realization, persistently binding text-specified target attributes over time. To evaluate this capability, we introduce WorldDraft, a benchmark of $432$ strictly paired test cases across $48$ executable 4D world families spanning six dynamic challenge categories. Under a $3 \times 3$ factorial design, WorldDraft orthogonally crosses draft granularity (abstract proxy to previs) and appearance alignment (neutral to conflicting) under identical dynamics. Extensive evaluations across $12$ representative models reveal that: (I) world preservation and visual realization remain in stark operational tension; (II) richer draft scaffolding anchors structure but paradoxically impairs appearance fidelity; and (III) conflicting source cues trigger severe source leakage (up to $64%$), exposing fragile cross-modal disentanglement. To mitigate these bottlenecks, we propose WorldDraft-Primer, a plug-and-play visual prompting strategy that consistently improves both objectives across frozen video renderers. Together, WorldDraft and WorldDraft-Primer lay a foundation for bridging programmatic 4D simulation with video generation, advancing physically grounded generative rendering.

Details

Motivation: 现有动态重建方法要么用静态场景假设抑制了有效运动，要么联合建模所有观测导致鬼影和不稳定几何；论文希望在随意拍摄视频中保留有效主体运动，同时去除瞬态遮挡、阴影和体积效应等干扰。

Result: 在合成与真实动态场景以及合成烟雾/雾场景上验证了方法在不同干扰情形下的有效性；摘要未给出具体定量指标或明确 SOTA 结论。

Insight: 创新点在于利用学习到的形变场构造 Jacobian 形变敏感度分数，作为重建歧义的经验代理来区分动态核心内容与干扰观测；该准则无需掩码、预训练分割器或特定介质监督，可统一处理空间遮挡、光度伪影及演化烟雾/雾。

Abstract: Dynamic reconstruction from casual video must preserve valid subject motion while suppressing transient occluders, shadows, and volumetric effects. Existing methods either suppress valid motion through static-scene assumptions or model all observations jointly, producing ghosting and unstable geometry. We introduce ReCan-GS, a 4D Gaussian Splatting framework that separates coherent scene content from inconsistent observations. It derives a Jacobian-based deformation-sensitivity score from the learned deformation field, using it as an empirical proxy for reconstruction ambiguity. The score guides separation into dynamic Core Gaussians and per-view Distractor Gaussians, or post-hoc pruning of sensitive Core Gaussians. The same criterion supports removal of spatial occluders, photometric artifacts, and evolving smoke or fog without masks, pretrained segmenters, or medium-specific supervision. Experiments on synthetic and real dynamic scenes and synthetic smoke/fog scenes demonstrate its usefulness across the evaluated distractor regimes.

Details

Motivation: 现有视频去雨方法多采用确定性恢复范式，假设可从雨天观测唯一恢复干净场景；但严重遮挡区域内容模糊，需要先验驱动推断，确定性方法易过平滑，而全局生成式恢复又可能产生不必要幻觉。

Abstract: Video deraining is challenging due to rain streaks, occlusions, and atmospheric effects. Existing methods mainly follow a deterministic restoration paradigm, assuming that clean scenes can be uniquely recovered from rainy observations. However, rain degradation is spatially heterogeneous. Reliable regions require faithful reconstruction, whereas severely occluded regions contain ambiguous content that require stronger prior-driven inference. Therefore, conventional deterministic restoration often produces over-smoothed results, while global generative restoration may introduce unnecessary hallucinations. We introduce a Selective Generative Restoration paradigm for video deraining, where generative priors are adaptively activated according to restoration uncertainty. Based on this principle, we propose Selective Generative Video Deraining (SGVD), an uncertainty-aware diffusion framework that enables adaptive fidelity preservation and generative recovery. Specifically, SGVD exploits stochastic diversity between dual one-step diffusion predictions to estimate region-level restoration uncertainty, which determines where observation fidelity should be preserved and where generative inference should be activated. Furthermore, an anchor-guided trajectory consistency mechanism is proposed to regularize stochastic diffusion evolution across frames, reducing temporal generation drift and improving video coherence. To achieve practical efficiency, SGVD adopts one-step diffusion inference while maintaining uncertainty-aware generative capability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SGVD achieves superior restoration performance, perceptual quality, and temporal consistency compared with state-of-the-art video deraining methods.

Details

Motivation: 现有标定多视角手部重建方法依赖真实采集数据，成本高、手部几何多样性有限且标注不完美；合成数据虽有精确监督，但存在显著的合成到真实外观差距。论文希望利用低成本可获取的二值掩码作为观测接口，减少外观域差异，从而有效利用合成几何数据。

Result: 在五个真实世界基准上，使用参考手渲染掩码时，SilHand 将相对重建精度从 49.2% 提升到 63.4%；随着合成训练集增大，真实重建精度持续提升；在掩码损坏情况下仍较鲁棒，重建误差最多增加 1.63 mm。

Insight: 创新点在于以二值掩码而非 RGB 作为多视角手部重建的核心观测，通过将三维假设投影到各视角并融合掩码射线证据来学习几何。客观来看，该设计降低了合成到真实的外观域差，使大规模合成手部几何数据能够直接服务真实场景，并提升了对掩码噪声的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Calibrated multi-view hand reconstruction has advanced rapidly, yet the effectiveness of existing methods depends strongly on real captured data, which is costly to acquire, limited in hand-geometry diversity, and often affected by imperfect annotations. Synthetic data offers a scalable alternative with accurate supervision, but a substantial synthetic-to-real appearance gap hinders its use. To address this, we introduce Hand in Silhouette (SilHand), a calibrated multi-view hand reconstruction framework that operates on binary hand masks, obtainable at low cost either automatically or manually, to reduce the synthetic-to-real observation gap. Rather than relying on RGB features for 3D lifting, SilHand projects metric 3D hypotheses into each view to query and fuse mask-ray evidence for hand reconstruction. This mask-centric formulation enables training on a large-scale synthetic dataset comprising 46.8k grasping poses and 60k free-hand samples, providing broad coverage of hand geometry while directly transferring to real captured hand geometry. Across five real-world benchmarks, SilHand improves relative reconstruction accuracy from 49.2% to 63.4% using masks rendered from reference hands. Moreover, reconstruction accuracy improves consistently as the synthetic training set grows, indicating that broader geometric coverage translates into better real-world performance. While achieving such diversity is difficult with real captured data, it becomes practical under our mask-centric formulation. Finally, SilHand remains robust under mask corruption, with reconstruction error increasing by at most 1.63 mm. Together, these results support binary masks as an effective interface for learning hand reconstruction from synthetic geometry.

Abstract: Accurate plant disease recognition is essential for intelligent agriculture, but few-shot settings provide limited labeled samples that may not capture diagnostic symptoms and intra-class variations. Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs), such as CLIP, offer transferable visual-semantic knowledge. However, existing CLIP-based methods often rely on coarse class semantics and global visual representations, overlooking disease-specific diagnostic information and fine-grained local cues. Excessive few-shot adaptation may also cause over-specialization and weaken the generalization ability of large-scale pre-training. We propose a description-guided, two-stage CLIP adaptation method to address these issues. Symptom and comparative descriptions enrich class semantics with typical visual symptoms and discriminative cues between similar diseases. The enhanced semantics guide the aggregation of class-relevant patch evidence to calibrate global image-text matching with fine-grained cues. For adaptation, we first update the vision-language encoders with lightweight parameters, then freeze them and train a linear classifier. At inference, the classifier is used for in-domain prediction, while class prototypes from the adapted text encoder support cross-domain prediction and retain vision-language transferability. Experiments with two backbones show that our method achieves the highest mean accuracy in most few-shot settings on PlantVillage, Cassava, and Pear, reaching 93.16%, 79.30%, and 96.56%, respectively, with ViT-L/14 under 16-shot. On class-aligned cross-domain evaluation across 21 shared PlantDoc classes, it achieves 56.96% accuracy, outperforming all compared methods. [101] RADIANT: Redundancy-Aware Information Allocation for Universal Multi-Modal Fusion cs.CV | cs.MMPDF

Shenghai Wu, Yue Lin

TL;DR: 论文提出任务通用的多模态融合框架RADIANT，认为通用融合的关键问题不是模态对齐不足，而是融合过程中的信息分配不佳。该方法围绕抑制冗余跨模态重叠、保留模态独有证据、组合任务条件协同三类角色组织融合，并用稳定矩阵指数投影和任务记忆硬正更新实现动态信息分配。在六个异构融合任务上取得一致提升，并在FLIR检测和跨数据集迁移上验证有效性。

Details

Motivation: 论文指出通用多模态融合失败往往源于信息分配不当：冗余证据被反复放大，模态独有证据在对齐时被过度平滑，任务相关协同证据激活不足。因此需要从冗余感知和动态分配角度重新组织融合过程。

Result: 在六个异构融合任务上相较对比方法取得一致改进；在FLIR下游检测上，mAP@0.5从GIF的91.2%提升到96.8%，提升5.6个百分点（相对6.1%）。方法还表现出较强跨数据集迁移能力，并可在冻结全部网络权重时通过推理时任务记忆适配进一步获益。

Insight: 创新点在于将通用多模态融合显式拆分为抑制冗余、保留独有、激活任务协同三类互补角色，并用稳定矩阵指数投影降低共享流与模态独有流的干扰。任务记忆与硬正更新用于选择当前融合机制下应激活的协同交互，客观上有助于实现冗余感知的动态信息分配和冻结权重下的任务适配。

Abstract: Universal multi-modal fusion often fails not because modalities are insufficiently aligned, but because the fusion process allocates information poorly: redundant evidence is repeatedly amplified, modality-unique evidence is over-smoothed during alignment, and task-dependent synergistic evidence is insufficiently activated. We present RADIANT (Redundancy-Aware Dynamic Information Allocation Network), a task-general framework that organizes fusion around three complementary roles: suppressing redundant cross-modal overlap, preserving modality-unique evidence, and composing task-conditioned synergy through cross-modal interaction. Specifically, stable matrix-exponential projections are used to reduce interference among shared and modality-unique streams, while task memory with hard positive updates selects which synergistic interactions should be activated for the current fusion regime. Extensive experiments across six heterogeneous fusion tasks show consistent improvements over the compared methods. On FLIR, RADIANT improves downstream detection mAP@0.5 from 91.2% to 96.8%, a gain of 5.6 percentage points (6.1% relative) over GIF. RADIANT also exhibits strong direct cross-dataset transfer, with further gains from inference-time task-memory adaptation while keeping all network weights frozen. Additional ablations and sensitivity studies support the robustness of the allocation mechanism.

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective post-training approach to improving fine-grained perception in multimodal large language models by amortizing costly inference-time zooming operations into a single-pass recipe. However, vanilla OPD is inherently susceptible to linguistic momentum: the teacher can follow the student’s erroneous prefixes, thus weakening corrective supervision. To address this, we propose Blink On-Policy Distillation (BlinkOPD), in which the teacher “blinks” at every token: it reads the same prefix once with its eyes open and once with its eyes closed. By extracting more effective supervision from each on-policy trajectory, BlinkOPD achieves state-of-the-art average performance across six fine-grained benchmarks using only 500 training images (12x less data) than existing OPD methods. Insight: 创新点在于分离视觉与推理验证并耦合策略更新：EoT-SFT通过描述、分析和有支撑结论教授细粒度观察与忠实推理；RTPO独立采样场景匹配且细粒度视觉差异的图像对，用Gravity和Flight双重视角评分形成共享成对优势。客观分析，利用成对比较和双视角验证有效提升细粒度推理与证据 grounding。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly capable, yet their reasoning in complex remote sensing scenes can remain coarse-grained and insufficiently grounded in task-relevant visual evidence. We propose Gravity and Flight (G&F), a framework for scene specialization with limited supervision that separates visual and reasoning verification while coupling policy updates. It combines Evidence-of-Thought supervised fine-tuning (EoT-SFT) and Relational Transfer Policy Optimization (RTPO). EoT-SFT teaches fine-grained observation and faithful reasoning through description, analysis, and supported conclusions. RTPO independently samples responses to scene-matched image pairs with fine-grained visual differences under a shared task. Its rubric-based verification combines Gravity, which checks visual fidelity and description coverage, and Flight, which takes descriptions as premises to assess evidence use and relations between paired judgments. Their scores and task correctness yield a shared pair-level advantage that couples policy updates from both responses. Using 1,008 demonstrations and 500 training pairs for harbor specialization, G&F achieves a mean score of 69.12 across six evaluation sets, exceeding the backbone and supervised initialization by 9.76 and 4.56 points. Matched controls show gains over single-image and joint-input RL, and removing either verification view reduces performance. Applying G&F to urban specialization also improves on the base model and supervised initialization across three evaluation subsets. Project details and updates are available at https://github.com/Z0ngL1ng/Gravity-and-Flight.

Abstract: Advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have improved remote sensing image analysis and supported geospatial decision-making. However, fine-grained understanding of remote sensing imagery remains challenging for VLMs. Large variations in object scale, complex geospatial relations among instances, and limited visual detail hinder precise instance localization, visible attribute identification, and spatial relation understanding, potentially leading to descriptions that lack visual grounding. Existing datasets and benchmarks insufficiently assess end-to-end instance localization and captioning without target-specific textual cues. To support this evaluation, we introduce RICapBench, a dataset and benchmark that unifies instance localization and fine-grained captioning in remote sensing. RICapBench contains 245,466 instance-level annotations and 498,005 spatial relation tuples spanning general remote sensing, UAV remote sensing, and dense small-object scenarios. To evaluate the accuracy and completeness of spatial relation captioning, we propose a remote sensing spatial relation parser and the RelCap-F1 metric. We combine RelCap-F1 with instance localization and attribute captioning metrics into a composite metric, RICScore. Abstract: Multimodal dataset distillation (MDD) aims to reduce the cost of vision-language pre-training by synthesizing compact image-text datasets. Although recent feature-matching methods have improved distillation efficiency, synthetic data may become specialized to the model representations encountered during optimization, limiting generalization to unseen model states. We propose Model Distribution-Aware Multimodal Dataset Distillation (MDA-DD), which leverages supervision from a dynamic staggered queue of models at different training stages. We further introduce asymmetric dual projection for complementary alignment in visual and linguistic spaces and an uncentered second-order moment objective that retains mean-related cross-modal information. Experiments on Flickr30K and MS-COCO demonstrate consistent retrieval improvements over existing methods across synthetic-data budgets. With only 100 synthetic pairs, MDA-DD improves IR@1 and TR@1 on Flickr30K by 12.9 and 18.2 percentage points, respectively, over the strongest baseline at the same budget, outperforming all compared methods using 500 pairs on both datasets across all six retrieval metrics.

Abstract: Physical AI aims to learn transferable physical knowledge from real-world experience to understand, predict, and act in the physical world. Abstract: The gap between controlled synthetic distortions and complex real-world degradations introduces a critical domain shift challenge for No-Reference Image Quality Assessment. Although Unsupervised Domain Adaptation (UDA) approaches attempt to address this issue, they remain constrained by substantial retraining overhead and cumulative error propagation inherent in iterative pseudo-labeling. Inspired by the dual-process theory of human cognition for remarkable generalization ability, we propose Hierarchical Intuitive and Fuzzy Consensus (HIFC), a completely train-free Cross-Domain IQA framework. HIFC employs asymmetric perceptual decomposition to disentangle semantic content from distortion-sensitive representations. The final quality score is fused through the synergy of two complementary branches: a memory consensus branch that retrieves a stable Fuzzy Quality Centroid from a synthetic reference gallery, and an intuitive branch that captures aesthetic polarization via zero-shot vision-language alignment. Extensive experiments show that HIFC achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, attaining the highest average SRCC and PLCC among competing UDA methods. Most notably, in the challenging synthetic-to-authentic cross-domain setting, HIFC achieves a substantial performance margin over competing methods, demonstrating remarkable robustness and generalization.

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) reason effectively about video content, but whether they perceive its event boundaries in a manner consistent with humans remains unclear. We first investigate this through Generic Event Boundary Detection (GEBD), which identifies event boundaries naturally perceived by humans in continuous video, without relying on predefined event categories. On Kinetics-GEBD dataset, LVLMs miss many boundaries when predicting directly from frames, but effectively refine candidates proposed by an unsupervised discovery method, achieving state-of-the-art unsupervised GEBD performance. We extend this discovery-refinement approach to hour-long egocentric video, where boundary discovery requires a broader semantic scale and whole-video LVLM refinement becomes impractical. We address these challenges through coarse-to-fine temporal discovery and localized LVLM accept/reject judgments. Human evaluation shows that the LVLM’s agreement with group consensus falls within the range observed among human annotators, with disagreements concentrated on low-consensus cases. Using this pipeline, we construct EgoBoundary, a benchmark for event boundary detection in hour-scale egocentric video. Representative supervised and unsupervised methods degrade substantially on EgoBoundary, highlighting the challenge of long-horizon event boundary detection.

Abstract: We present UniSpark-9B, a unified multimodal model that investigates hybrid attention for visual understanding and generation. Autoregressive text prediction and visual flow matching jointly support visual question answering, text-to-image generation, instruction-guided editing, and interleaved text-image prediction. In interleaved generation, noisy visual targets are isolated from subsequent predictions, while clean visual representations provide context for later outputs. Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) enable future prediction in latent space without pixel reconstruction. However, latent predictive consistency alone does not establish the relative driving quality of candidate plans. We present ActWorld-JEPA, a joint-embedding formulation for candidate-based driving. Where Plan-Conditioned JEPA (PC-JEPA) uses global and step-wise trajectory intentions to guide recurrent latent prediction, then combines the predicted future with the current scene through gated cross-attention; Relational Residual Reranking ($R^3$) models agreement and discrepancy between plan and future representations under planning supervision, yielding bounded, gated residual corrections to the base planning scores. This formulation connects latent prediction to candidate selection using only observed context and proposed trajectories at inference. Benchmark-specific implementations report 94.1 PDMS on NAVSIM v1 navtest, with the final selector calibrated using aggregate NAVTEST feedback, and 91.6 and 42.7 EPDMS on NAVSIM v2 navtest and navhard, respectively. The nuScenes evaluation reports a mean $L_2$ error of 1.20 m and a predicted collision rate of 0.76% over 1–6 s. This work provides an effective path from JEPA’s predictive representations to driving decisions informed by anticipated consequences.

Details

Motivation: 社会场景数据同时标注个人动作和群体意图，论文质疑并检验“同时训练两个层级会互相促进”的常见假设，关注细节是否帮助整体以及方向性。

Abstract: Social scene datasets label people at two levels: what each person is doing and what the group is there for. Training on both levels at once is often assumed to help each of them. We test this assumption for vision-language models (VLMs) on JRDB-Social, treating person actions as the details and group intent as the whole. We fine-tune Qwen3.5 4B under eight training arms with three seeds, repeat the core arms on Qwen3.5 9B and Qwen3-VL 4B, and judge every arm with a verdict rule fixed before testing. Writing each member’s actions before the intent raises intent macro-F1 from 30.1 to 31.0, and writing the intent first reaches 31.2, while action F1 stays within 0.2 points of an action-only model. Under the selected recipe, no arm trained with intent improves actions. The gain requires correct, image-grounded actions: empty action lists or actions borrowed from another group with the same intent lower intent F1. Forced-prefix tests, occlusion, member masking, linear probes and component grafting show that the intent follows the written actions, relies more on people and partly resides in the vision tower. Supplying actions as input instead of as targets hurts both levels.

Details

Motivation: 机器人平台上即使完成标定，受时间不同步、机械振动或点云重建误差影响，单图与点云间仍存在数像素重投影误差，并在中等距离造成分米级几何偏差，影响跨模态监督、纹理映射和语义标签迁移等下游任务。

Result: 论文在来自异构数据源、经手工标定的图像数据集上评估方法，并展示自动点云重缩放因子可使单一尺度不变公式在广泛场景中稳健细化；摘要未给出具体定量指标、benchmark 排名或 SOTA 对比。
