Abstract: Cameras and LiDARs (Light Detection and Ranging) provide complementary observations of the same scene: cameras capture rich appearance cues while LiDAR sensors provide accurate geometric measurements. In this work, we address the problem of fine image-to-point cloud registration from a single image and a roughly estimated camera pose. On robotic platforms, residual reprojection errors of several pixels are commonly observed even after calibration due to temporal desynchronization, mechanical vibrations, or inaccuracies in point cloud reconstruction. Such errors can lead to decimeter-level geometric discrepancies at moderate distances and significantly affect multiple downstream tasks, such as dense cross-modal supervision, texture mapping, and semantic label transfer. To address this challenge, we introduce ReFineR: an offline framework to finely register individual images to pre-reconstructed point clouds. ReFineR builds upon differentiable point splatting rasterization and a smooth Gradient Orientation Measure (GOM) to iteratively refine the camera pose through analysis-by-synthesis optimization. We evaluated the method on a curated dataset of manually calibrated images collected from heterogeneous data sources. Furthermore, we show that an automatic point cloud rescale factor can be estimated prior to optimization, enabling robust refinement across a wide range of scenarios using a single scale-invariant formulation. The project page can be found at https://refiner-project.github.io/

Result: 在留出的光学厚云数据上，方法在相同时间下重光照效果优于冻结源层析，并在给予显著更多时间时优于可微路径追踪；训练于反演轨迹还改善了访问状态处的导数对齐并减少优化漂移。

Abstract: Reconstructing optically thick media from photographs requires modeling multiple scattering and its response to changes in extinction. Frozen-source tomography discards this derivative; differentiable path tracing estimates it through expensive, noisy sampling. We instead predict the multiple-scattering field with a neural operator and differentiate through it during inverse rendering, while computing transmittance and single scattering directly. A further challenge arises during optimization: an operator that predicts the field accurately on held-out media can still produce poor derivatives at the density states visited by the optimizer. We address this by fine-tuning on states generated during inversions driven by the operator, using Monte Carlo scattering-field supervision. On held-out optically thick clouds, our method improves relighting over frozen-source tomography at equal time and outperforms differentiable path tracing given substantially more time. Training on inversion trajectories also improves derivative alignment at visited states and reduces optimization drift.

Abstract: Multimodal representation learning combines complementary signals to capture shared semantic information. For dynamic scenes, these representations need to remain consistent across views while preserving distinctions between temporal states. In visual speech representation learning, view variation changes the visibility and reliability of articulation cues, making it difficult to learn such representations from synchronized audio and video. To address this challenge, we propose MuViRL, a self-supervised framework for multimodal view-invariant visual speech representation learning via calibrated view aggregation. The framework combines synchronized 3D Gaussian view synthesis with multimodal self-supervision to learn consistent representations from real and synthesized data. A multimodal decision model learns relative view utility from teacher prediction losses, and its calibrated scores guide the aggregation of complementary information. Aggregation-to-Single Distillation transfers the aggregated predictions to a single branch. To this end, we introduce the Triplet Incremental Training Framework, a non-intrusive paradigm that seamlessly instills 3D geometric awareness into a general VLM backbone while fully preserving its open-world capabilities. Result: 在 SpatialBench 的 Sparse 和 Medium 子集上，GFM 取得最低绝对相对深度误差和最高 5 度相对位姿 AUC；在五个数据集上取得最低动态点与场景流平均端点误差。34 个场景上 PoseEngine 在 1/3/5 度阈值下取得最高场景平均位姿 AUC，累计运行 14.07 小时，约为 GLUEMAP 69.67 小时的 20%；PoseEngine Flash 混合精度运行 2.62 小时，约为其 4%。

Insight: 创新点在于将几何基础模型、位姿优化引擎和重建派生监督整合为生产级系统，并联合预测动态 3D 点与场景流；客观看，其价值在于用学习几何先验增强场景级重建的精度与效率，并通过 KosmoWorld 形成数据与监督闭环。

Abstract: We present Kosmo-GFM, a scene reconstruction system that integrates a geometry foundation model (GFM), a pose optimization engine (PoseEngine), and reconstruction-derived supervision from KosmoWorld. GFM predicts camera parameters, depth, cross-view correspondences, and dynamic 3D points from images and videos. PoseEngine combines these predictions with geometric optimization to recover scene-level camera poses and geometry for downstream 3D and 4D Gaussian Splatting. KosmoWorld generates images and geometric supervision from reconstructed real-world scenes to improve GFM’s geometric predictions. On the Sparse and Medium subsets of SpatialBench, GFM achieves the lowest absolute relative depth error and the highest relative-pose AUC at 5 degrees among the compared methods. It also achieves the lowest mean endpoint errors for dynamic points and scene flow across the five evaluated datasets. Across 34 scenes completed by all compared reconstruction systems, PoseEngine achieves the highest scene-averaged pose AUC at thresholds of 1, 3, and 5 degrees, with a cumulative runtime of 14.07 hours, approximately 20% of GLUEMAP’s 69.67 hours. PoseEngine Flash provides a further accuracy-efficiency trade-off, with a measured mixed-precision runtime of 2.62 hours, approximately 4% of GLUEMAP’s runtime. Separate scaling experiments show generally favorable trends with increased data, model computation, and training budgets. These results support the use of learned geometric priors within a scene-level reconstruction pipeline.

[123] Visual Cipher Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Visual Reasoning cs.CVPDF

Deyao Hong, Jiayi Jiang, Yuchun Chen, Yixuan Shi, Wenyi Li

TL;DR: 论文提出 Visual Cipher Bench，用于评测 LLM 智能体在长程视觉推理中的能力。该基准包含 40 个原创视觉谜题，涵盖空间操作与模拟、逻辑约束与规划、规则归纳、知识解码四类，每个谜题仅提供少量黑白线图和极简提交说明，要求智能体完全从图像中发现解题机制。

Details

Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体在长程编程和终端任务上表现优异，但依赖显式文本规范和即时执行反馈；真实问题中关键信息常只存在于图像里，且现有视觉推理基准多为单轮短链问答，智能体基准又偏文本环境，无法衡量从视觉证据中自主发现规则并进行长程推理的能力。

Result: 在 8 个前沿模型的 29 种推理强度配置上，最强模型 GPT-6 Astra (max) 仅达到 26.7% 准确率，8 个模型中有 5 个低于 1%；在 10 个任务的子集上，提供一个非剧透的机制提示可将准确率从 7.8% 提升到 55.6%。

Insight: 论文的创新点在于构建了一个要求智能体从图像中自主发现规则、反复检查图像并模拟空间变换的长程视觉推理基准；结果客观表明当前智能体的主要瓶颈不在于执行多步计划，而在于从视觉证据中自主归纳解题机制。

Abstract: While language-model agents excel at long-horizon coding and terminal tasks, this success relies on two conditions: explicit text specifications and immediate execution feedback. However, neither condition holds in real-world problems where critical information exists only in images, leaving it unclear whether long-horizon capability transfers from text to visual reasoning. In such visual settings, because rules and clues resist text transcription and lack test-case feedback, agents must repeatedly inspect images, simulate spatial transformations, and autonomously cross-check clues. Despite this challenge, existing evaluations fail to capture such capability: visual reasoning benchmarks largely reduce tasks to single-turn, short-chain question answering, whereas agent benchmarks focus on text-based environments or interactive states that permit trial-and-error. To bridge this gap, we introduce Visual Cipher Bench, a collection of 40 original visual puzzles across four categories: spatial manipulation and simulation, logical constraints and planning, rule induction, and knowledge-based decoding. Each puzzle provides only one to three black-and-white line drawings and a minimal submission instruction, requiring agents to discover the underlying solving mechanism entirely from the images. Across 29 reasoning-effort configurations of eight frontier models, even the strongest model, GPT-6 Astra (max), achieves only 26.7% accuracy, while five of the eight models score below 1%. Yet on a ten-task subset, providing a single non-spoiler mechanism hint boosts accuracy from 7.8% to 55.6%. This sharp contrast shows that the primary bottleneck of current agents lies not in executing multi-step plans, but in autonomously discovering rules from visual evidence. Visual Cipher Bench thus provides a rigorous benchmark for measuring long-horizon visual reasoning in agents.

[124] TokenStream: Multi-Subject Personalized Video Generation with Token-wise LoRA and Guided Attention cs.CVPDF

Hung Nguyen, Quang Qui-Vinh Nguyen, Khoi Nguyen

TL;DR: TokenStream是一个基于优化的多主体个性化文本到视频生成框架，可在无需参考视频训练数据和对每个新主体组合联合优化的情况下，组合独立学习的主体身份。它通过Token-wise LoRA将低秩更新限制在主体相关文本token的交叉注意力值投影上，并用T2V引导流在早期去噪中复用冻结基模型的查询和键以保留布局与运动先验。作者还构建了Multi-DB基准，并在1.3B模型上验证了身份保持和文本对齐的提升。

Details

Motivation: 从单张参考图学习容易将主体外观与静态图像偏置耦合，而直接合并独立训练的适配器会导致多主体身份混合；因此需要一种无需联合优化、能分离多主体身份并保留视频生成布局与运动先验的组合方法。

Abstract: We introduce TokenStream, an optimization-based framework that composes independently learned subject identities in text-to-video generation without a reference-video training dataset or joint optimization of each new subject combination. Learning from a single reference image makes subject appearance difficult to separate from static-image bias, while merging independently trained adapters can mix identities. Token-wise LoRA (ToRA) restricts low-rank updates to the cross-attention value projections of subject-associated text tokens, providing a separate adaptation path for each subject. A T2V-guided stream copies queries and keys from the frozen base model during early denoising to reuse its layout and motion priors while retaining personalized values. We also introduce Multi-DB, a 30-subject, 122-prompt benchmark with appearance-free prompts and varied interactions. On Multi-DB at 1.3B, TokenStream improves identity preservation from 0.422 for DreamBooth to 0.467 and textual alignment from 0.652 to 0.688.

Abstract: This report summarizes the Mobile Unified Multimodal Understanding (MUMU) track of the 8th Large-scale Video Object Segmentation (LSVOS) Challenge, held in conjunction with ECCV 2026. MUMU asks for a single unified model that jointly performs multi-concept image tagging over quality, scene, and event vocabularies, open-vocabulary object detection, and concise image captioning, within a budget of 0.5B parameters and 8 GB of peak inference memory. Submissions are ranked by a weighted task score scaled by an efficiency factor. We describe the tasks, rules, and evaluation protocol, report the final results, and review the methods of the top three teams. All three leading solutions build on the compact Florence-2-base model, but extend it in different ways: lightweight tagging heads with cross-task inference refinement, a shared low-rank adapter trained on coverage-aware multi-source data with task-dependent decoding, and a training-free pipeline that maps captions to tags and applies detection test-time augmentation. Abstract: Vision transformers (ViTs) serve as the backbone for countless vision tasks, including dense prediction tasks, such as segmentation and depth estimation, even though the quadratic complexity of self-attention severely limits feature resolution and induces spatial artifacts. In this work, we introduce ElasticDino, a lightweight adapter which produces high-resolution feature maps, adhering to the fine details of the input image. As a key insight, we observe the consistency of artifacts across channels, and simultaneously resolve them to match the input image structure. Using a slim architecture based on spatial deformations, ElasticDino produces pixel-aligned, high-resolution feature maps efficiently. Leveraging deformations allows strictly preserving sharp semantics, and thereby avoiding the smoothness biases inherent to alternative approaches. Due to the absence of ground truth, we employ pixel-level semantic segmentation and depth estimation with linear heads to predict a set of downstream dense tasks, guiding the deformation process without leaking task-specific semantics into the model. [131] HiVE-VQA: Hierarchical Interaction via Volumetric Embedding for Video Quality Assessment cs.CVPDF

Robbie Hamilton, Fan Zhang, David Bull

TL;DR: 论文提出 HiVE-VQA，一种基于单一冻结 V-JEPA 2.1 视频基础模型的无参考视频质量评估框架。它从编码器多个深度提取 token 网格并堆叠为分层时空体积，再用紧凑 3D 卷积头融合浅层失真线索与深层语义/时序上下文。仅训练 5.2M 参数的头，不微调编码器，在多个数据集上超过 17 个 SOTA 方法。

Details

Motivation: 用户生成视频激增使准确鲁棒的 NR-VQA 很重要，但现有方法多依赖复杂多分支管线或昂贵单分支骨干微调。论文关注单一冻结视频基础模型能否作为有效 NR-VQA 骨干，并降低计算与架构复杂度。

Result: 仅在 LSVQ 上训练且不微调，HiVE-VQA 在 LSVQ-test、LSVQ-1080p 以及跨数据集 KoNViD-1k、LIVE-VQC 的 SROCC 和 PLCC 上优于 17 个 SOTA 方法；仅训练 5.2M 参数（占总量 5.7%），无辅助多模态模型，推理成本与视频分辨率无关。

Insight: 创新点在于首次将 JEPA 类视频编码器用于 NR-VQA，并通过多深度 token 网格构建分层时空体积，用轻量 3D 卷积头跨层、空间和时间融合特征。客观看，其价值在于验证冻结单视频基础模型的多层级深度融合可替代任务特定多网络融合，实现高效且有效的 NR-VQA。

Abstract: The proliferation of user-generated video content has made accurate and robust no-reference video quality assessment (NR-VQA) a highly important challenge. Existing research increasingly relies on architecturally complex multi-branch pipelines or computationally intensive fine-tuning of single-branch backbones. In this paper, we investigate whether a single frozen video foundation model can serve as an effective NR-VQA backbone, and propose HiVE-VQA, the first framework (to our knowledge) to apply a JEPA-based video encoder to this task. HiVE-VQA extracts token grids from multiple depths of a pre-trained V-JEPA 2.1 encoder and stacks them into a hierarchical spatio-temporal volume, which a compact 3D convolutional head fuses across its layer, spatial, and temporal axes, combining low-level distortion cues from shallow layers with the semantic and temporal context captured at greater depths. The encoder remains frozen throughout, so only the head is trained: 5.2M parameters (5.7% of the total), with no auxiliary multi-modal models and an inference cost that is invariant to video resolution. Trained solely on LSVQ and evaluated without any fine-tuning, HiVE-VQA outperforms 17 state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods in SROCC and PLCC on both intra-dataset (LSVQ-test, LSVQ-1080p) and cross-dataset (KoNViD-1k, LIVE-VQC) benchmarks. These results indicate that hierarchical, depth-wise fusion of features from a single frozen video foundation model provides a more effective and efficient basis for NR-VQA than task-specific multi-network fusion. Code and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/RobHam99/HiVE-VQA.

[132] SpotDiff: A Plug-and-Play Framework for Zero-Shot Change Detection in Remote Sensing Imagery cs.CVPDF

Anit Upadhyaya, Ata Akbari Asanjan, Chaitanya Jalluri, Subash Yeggina, Jeremy Kravitz

TL;DR: 论文提出 SpotDiff，一个无需训练、即插即用的零样本遥感影像变化检测框架。它组合 DINOv3 提取语义鲁棒特征、AnyUp 将特征重建到像素分辨率、SAM 3 生成文本引导的感兴趣区域，并用推理时多尺度策略 SWIMS 提升小目标可靠性，最终在候选区域内比较两期重建特征来检测变化，无需可学习组件、微调或变化标签。

Details

Motivation: 传统遥感变化检测依赖大量时间对齐的像素级标注，难以跨区域、成像平台和应用领域扩展；现有零样本方法因特征粗糙或跨时不一致，对细粒度变化定位较差。

Result: 在 LEVIR-CD 上达到 82.46 F1，比最强先前无训练方法高 2.06 点；在 SECOND 多类基准上建筑变化检测提升 10.3 IoU，六类平均提升 2.2 IoU；仅使用通用特征上采样器 AnyUp 就比同特征双线性上采样提升 13.8 F1。PlanetScope（3 m）和 Sentinel-2（10 m）上的定性结果证明同一流程可跨分辨率迁移。

Insight: 创新点在于首次将通用特征上采样器引入变化检测，并系统组合互补视觉基础模型实现免训练、免标签的零样本变化检测；客观看，其价值在于验证了“现代基础模型组合”是通向可扩展无监督变化检测的有效路径，且即插即用与跨分辨率迁移性较强。

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection usually relies on large collections of temporally aligned, pixel-level annotations, which limits its scalability across regions, imaging platforms and application domains. Recent zero-shot approaches reduce the need for supervision, but they often localize fine-grained changes poorly because their features are coarse or inconsistent across time. We present SpotDiff, a training-free, plug-and-play framework for zero-shot change detection built by composing complementary vision foundation models. SpotDiff uses DINOv3 for semantically robust features, AnyUp to reconstruct them at pixel resolution, and SAM 3 for text-guided region-of-interest proposals, which an inference-time multi-scale strategy (SWIMS) makes reliable on small objects. Change is then measured by comparing the reconstructed features of the two dates inside the proposed regions, with no learnable components, fine-tuning or change labels. To our knowledge, SpotDiff is the first change detection method to use a universal feature upsampler, which alone adds 13.8 F1 over bilinear upsampling of the same features. On LEVIR-CD, SpotDiff reaches 82.46 F1, 2.06 points above the strongest prior training-free method, and on the multi-class SECOND benchmark it improves building change detection by 10.3 IoU and the six-class average by 2.2 IoU. Qualitative results on PlanetScope (3 m) and Sentinel-2 (10 m) imagery show that the same pipeline transfers across resolutions without modification. Our results indicate that composing modern foundation models is an effective route to scalable, label-free change detection.

[133] Validation-Guided Prompt Selection for Differential Morphing Attack Detection cs.CVPDF

Raghavendra Ramachandra, Hailin Li

TL;DR: 论文研究在冻结参数的视觉语言模型上，通过验证集引导的提示词选择而非模型微调来提升差分变形攻击检测（D-MAD）。方法生成并筛选候选提示词，以安全导向损失优先降低漏检变形攻击，并比较递归修订策略；在跨LMA、MIPGAN-2和PIPE的160对主体不重叠测试集上验证。

Details

Motivation: D-MAD需要比较可信活体采集与可能含面部变形的证件图像，冻结VLM的性能可能受提示词表述显著影响；论文希望利用带标签验证数据改进提示词，避免昂贵的模型微调。

Result: Version 1选出的提示词在160对测试集上达到19.17% MACER和0.00% BSCER，显著优于手动提示词的70.00% MACER，配对差50.83个百分点（95% bootstrap区间[43.33,58.33]）；递归修订未超过最佳初始验证损失，修订版本测试MACER为31.67%，且漏检的23个攻击均为LMA变形。

Insight: 创新点在于将D-MAD中的冻结VLM适配转化为验证集引导的提示词选择/修订问题，并使用融合MACER、BSCER、响应/解析失败和决策不一致的安全导向选择准则；客观看，提示词工程能显著影响安全检测性能，但小规模验证集下的递归优化仍不稳定。

Abstract: Differential morphing attack detection (D-MAD) compares a trusted live capture with a document image that may contain a facial morph. We investigate whether labelled validation data can improve a frozen-parameter vision-language evaluator through prompt selection rather than model fine-tuning. Version1 generates 50 candidate prompts and selects the top-ranked instruction using a security-oriented loss that prioritises missed morph attacks (MACER) while accounting for bona fide classification errors (BSCER), response failures, parse failures, and decision inconsistency. Version2 recursively revises prompts using aggregate validation feedback. On a subject-disjoint test set of 160 pairs spanning LMA, MIPGAN-2, and PIPE, the selected Version1 prompt achieves a (19.17%) morph attack classification error rate (MACER), with (0.00%) observed bona fide sample classification error rate (BSCER), compared with (70.00%) MACER for a controlled manual prompt. The paired (50.83) percentage-point difference has a (95%) subject-bootstrap interval of ([43.33,58.33]). Recursive revision does not surpass the best Generation0 validation loss; the reported revised descendant obtains (31.67%) test MACER. All 23 attacks missed by Version~1 are landmark-based (LMA) morphs. Overall, the results show that prompt formulation can substantially influence frozen-parameter VLM-based D-MAD performance, while also highlighting the challenge of recursive prompt refinement under a compact 10-subject validation setting.

[134] Consensus Capture in Reference-Based UAV Video Stabilization: A Benchmark on Dynamic Urban Scenes cs.CVPDF

Robert Fonod, Nikolas Geroliminis

TL;DR: 论文研究参考帧无人机视频稳定中的“共识捕获”问题：在动态城市场景中，协同运动的车辆会提供相互一致的匹配，使鲁棒单应估计偏向其运动。作者构建了静态与动态两部分基准，评估十条配准流水线以及目标掩码的作用。结果显示未掩码的 ORB 会被动态目标捕获，而掩码可消除所有流水线的捕获现象。

Details

Motivation: 现有参考帧稳定流水线多在仅相机运动的特征匹配基准上选择，但在繁忙路口等动态场景中，运动目标的一致对应会误导鲁棒单应估计，且内点统计无法检测这种失败。论文旨在测量该“共识捕获”现象并评估目标掩码能否防止它。

Result: 在静态 4K 航拍帧上，调参后的 AKAZE、RootSIFT、SIFT 和 BRISK 领先，重新调整匹配器阈值最多将 mAA 提升 0.0062，掩码代价小于 0.005。未掩码 ORB 在 304 对实时动态图像中对 28 对被捕获、离线对 6 对被捕获，实时被捕获估计的中位角点误差为 18.8 px，但内点数量和比例仍典型；掩码可消除所有流水线的捕获且不改变领先流水线。

Insight: 创新点在于提出针对共识捕获的静态/动态双协议基准，动态协议按运动前景的数量与一致性选择悬停片段，并用背景对齐、目标位置误差和跟随运动组位移比例等指标评估，同时使用人工标注地面控制点审计的伪真值。客观看，其价值在于揭示了内点统计难以发现的鲁棒单应失败模式，并系统验证了目标掩码作为缓解手段的有效性。

Abstract: Reference-frame stabilization registers every frame of a hovering-drone video onto one reference frame, keeping object positions comparable. The registration pipelines it relies on are selected on feature-matching benchmarks in which only the camera moves. Over a busy intersection, vehicles moving together supply mutually consistent correspondences that can draw the robust homography estimate toward their motion: consensus capture, which inlier statistics cannot flag. A two-part benchmark measures it and the object masking meant to prevent it. A static protocol scores ten pipelines under exactly known homographies on 4K aerial frames, tuned on a site-disjoint split at a matched resolution and feature budget. A dynamic protocol scores them at an offline and a real-time budget on hovers selected from the video alone to span the amount and coherence of moving foreground, plus a stationary control. It measures background alignment, object position error and the share of a moving group’s displacement an estimate follows, against a pseudo-ground-truth reference audited with manually annotated ground control points, which also score every candidate. On static scenes, tuned AKAZE, RootSIFT, SIFT and BRISK lead, retuning the matcher thresholds raises the mean average accuracy (mAA) by at most 0.0062, and masking costs less than 0.005. Unmasked ORB is captured on 28 of 304 real-time dynamic pairs and on 6 offline; its real-time captured estimates have a median corner error of 18.8 px yet typical inlier counts and ratios. Masking removes capture for every pipeline and leaves the leading pipelines unchanged. Code, data and results are released.

[135] eDMD: Endpoint Distribution Matching for Few-Step Diffusion Distillation cs.CVPDF

Tong Zhao, Xianfang Zeng, Yucheng Han, Ruoyu Wang, Mingkun Lei

TL;DR: 论文提出 eDMD（端点分布匹配蒸馏），用于将扩散模型蒸馏为少步生成学生模型。针对现有 DMD 在多步蒸馏中让 fake score model 学习中间状态或所有去噪步、导致学生更新信号与中间分布纠缠的问题，eDMD 仅在学生最终输出上训练一个共享 fake 模型，并用它校正每个去噪步，同时引入 precise teacher 提供更准确的教师监督。

Details

Motivation: 现有分布匹配蒸馏在多步蒸馏时，虽然只交付学生最终输出，却用中间状态或全步去噪状态训练 fake 模型，导致匹配方向被中间分布扭曲，甚至学生最终分布已匹配教师时匹配项仍非零。论文旨在消除这种中间分布与最终输出分布之间的不匹配。

Result: 在 SD3-Medium、FLUX.2-klein（4B 和 9B）以及 Qwen-Image-2.1 上，eDMD 在 4 步生成设置下持续优于现有分布匹配方法。

Insight: 创新点在于提出端点分布匹配：用单个共享 fake 模型仅基于学生最终输出训练，并用于校正所有去噪步；理论上保证匹配项当且仅当学生最终输出分布匹配教师时消失，不受中间分布影响。此外，precise teacher 用最终噪声区间上的短多步轨迹替代单步引导预测，并为中间步配准查询密度。

Abstract: Distribution matching distillation (DMD) has emerged as a leading paradigm for distilling diffusion models into few-step students, where an auxiliary fake score model tracks the student’s distribution and the discrepancy between the teacher and fake predictions drives the student toward the teacher. In multi-step distillation, although only the student’s final output is delivered, existing methods train the fake model on intermediate states or across all denoising steps. Consequently, the student’s update signal becomes entangled with intermediate distributions, which can distort the matching direction and remain nonzero even after the student’s final outputs already match the teacher distribution. To resolve this mismatch, we propose Endpoint Distribution Matching Distillation (eDMD), which trains a single shared fake model exclusively on the student’s final outputs and uses it to correct every denoising step. Our theoretical analysis proves that this endpoint reference guarantees that the distribution matching term vanishes if and only if the student’s final output distribution matches the teacher’s, regardless of intermediate distributions. Beyond the fake reference, we further strengthen teacher supervision through a precise teacher (PT) that replaces single-step guided predictions with short multi-step trajectories on the final noise interval, paired with a calibrated query density for intermediate steps. Experiments on SD3-Medium, FLUX.2-klein (4B and 9B), and Qwen-Image-2.1 demonstrate that eDMD consistently outperforms existing distribution matching methods for 4-step generation.

[136] Objects as Addresses: Instance Binding in Vision Transformer Attention cs.CVPDF

Khalil Sabri, Guillaume-Alexandre Bilodeau, Nicolas Saunier, Wassim Bouachir

TL;DR: 论文研究预训练Vision Transformer（ViT）如何在自注意力中保持物体实例分离（object binding）。作者在8个预训练ViT上发现，实例分离更多体现在注意力路由的query-key关系中，而非token内容；同一物体的patch倾向于共享一个可称为attention address的读取区域，且可通过编辑query组件重定向该地址。基于query组件对patch聚类可更好地区分同类别实例。

Details

Motivation: 论文关注ViT自注意力机制为何以及如何在机制层面将不同物体实例保持分离，即object binding问题；现有工作虽表明预训练ViT具备该能力，但其内部计算机制尚未被解释清楚。

Result: 在8个预训练ViT上的分析表明，query-key注意力关系比value相似度或残差表示更能可靠区分实例；按query组件聚类patch取得.615 FG-ARI，而最终层特征聚类仅为.289。在DINOv2和多数测试backbone中，编辑选定head的query组件可将attention address重定向到另一个体。

Insight: 创新点在于提出attention address概念，将实例绑定解释为注意力路由中的共享读取地址，并证明query组件可用于实例分离和注意力编辑。客观来看，该工作提供了ViT实例级可解释性视角，也展示了无需额外训练的注意力级实例分组与操控潜力。

Abstract: Humans naturally parse visual scenes into coherent individual objects rather than scattered features, a capability cognitive science calls object binding. While recent work shows that pretrained Vision Transformers (ViTs) intrinsically acquire this ability, how native self-attention computations mechanistically keep object instances separate remains unresolved. By studying this across eight pretrained ViTs, we found that instance separation is expressed more strongly in attention routing than in token content: selected query-key relations distinguish individual objects more reliably than value similarity or residual representations. Inside heads, patches belonging to one individual tend to read from a common image region, which we term their attention address. Visually distinct parts of one person share an address, whereas identical duplicated instances receive separate, movable addresses. These shared destinations arise in part because query interactions with the spatial structure of keys direct attention toward consistent regions for one object and elsewhere for another. In DINOv2 and most other backbones we tested, editing this query component at the selected head redirects its attention address toward another individual. Grouping patches by this query component separates same-category instances, reaching .615 FG-ARI versus .289 for the same clustering of final-layer features, which tend to merge them.

[137] Consistent Video-to-Video Translation via Explicit Correspondences cs.CV | cs.AI | cs.GR | cs.LGPDF

Gaurav Parmar, Zhengqi Li, Richard Zhang, Jun-Yan Zhu, Srinivasa Narasimhan

TL;DR: 论文提出 vid2vid-long，一种基于显式对应关系的视频到视频翻译方法。其核心是利用输入视频中匹配的区域在输出视频中也应匹配这一观察，在输入端计算 patch 级对应关系，并据此检索对应区域中已生成的输出，再通过 correspondence cross-attention 对模型进行条件化，从而在保持实时生成的同时提升长程时序一致性。

Details

Motivation: 现有实时视频到视频方法通常将注意力限制在固定长度的因果窗口内，当相关历史超出窗口时会导致长程时序一致性不足。论文旨在突破该窗口限制，利用输入视频自身的跨帧对应关系提供长期参考。

Result: 实验表明，vid2vid-long 能在多种近期视频模型上稳定提升长程时序一致性，同时保持生成质量和实时性能；摘要未给出具体定量指标或 benchmark 数值。

Insight: 创新点在于将视频到视频翻译中的输入对应关系显式建模为跨帧参考，通过 patch 级检索历史输出并用对应关系交叉注意力条件化生成，从而在不牺牲实时性的情况下缓解固定因果窗口带来的长程一致性瓶颈。

Abstract: Interactive video-to-video applications require real-time generation while maintaining long-range temporal consistency. However, recent methods achieve speed by restricting attention to a fixed-length causal window. This limits long-range consistency whenever relevant history falls outside the context window. To address this limitation, we introduce vid2vid-long, a correspondence-based approach grounded in a simple observation. Video-to-video translation provides an explicit cross-frame reference: the regions that match in the input video should match in the output video too. Concretely, we compute patch-level correspondences in the input, guiding retrieval of previously generated outputs in the corresponding regions, and condition the model on these patches through correspondence cross-attention. Our experiments demonstrate that vid2vid-long consistently improves long-range temporal consistency across recent video models while preserving generation quality and real-time performance.

[139] K2V: Generative Keyframing For Physically Plausible Video Generation cs.CVPDF

Felix O’Mahony, Daniel Olmeda Reino, Roberto Cipolla, Ayush Tewari

TL;DR: 论文提出 K2V，一种生成式关键帧视频生成框架：先利用语言模型生成物理合理的关键帧，再以这些关键帧为条件驱动视频扩散模型在关键状态间插值，从而生成物理上更可信的视频。

Details

Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型虽然外观质量高，但在经典生成设置中物理推理完全依赖扩散过程，容易产生明显物理错误。论文希望将物理推理从扩散模型中解耦出来，以提升物理合理性并增强对关键时刻的控制。

Insight: 创新点在于将物理推理交给语言模型驱动的关键帧生成模块，并把扩散模型降级为关键帧间插值器；客观看，其价值在于显式语言推理、关键帧细粒度控制与视频扩散渲染能力的有效分工。

Abstract: Video diffusion models are able to generate samples with high-quality appearance and textural qualities. However, content produced by these methods is known to exhibit significant physical errors. This is because, in a classical video generation setting, all physical reasoning must take place simultaneously and exclusively within the diffusion process.We propose to resolve these issues by redistributing the task of physical reasoning onto a language model-backed keyframe generation module, which allows for explicit reasoning in language, and fine-grained control over the appearance of the video at important moments in the video. We then condition a video generation model with these keyframes, reducing the role of the diffusion model to the simpler task of interpolating between physically-correct intermediate states, rather than inferring an entire sequence with diffusion. Results demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms existing approaches, achieving state-of-the-art performance on the Physics-IQ Verified benchmark and PhyGenBench.

[140] Unbundling Multimodal Vision Encoding cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Ellis Brown, Chris Hoang, Shengyi Qian, Shang-Wen Li, Mike Lewis

TL;DR: 论文系统拆解了多模态语言模型中视觉编码器的三个设计选择：上游预训练带来的视觉先验、专用视觉容量、以及在多模态主干前的预融合放置。作者在统一数据与匹配总计算量的受控框架下从头训练并比较不同模型，发现视觉先验并非必需，专用视觉容量才是关键，且该容量不必位于融合之前，而可早期融入主干。

Details

Motivation: 现有多模态模型在视觉编码方式上差异很大，且数据、架构和计算量不一致，导致无法单独评估预训练视觉编码器、专用视觉模块和融合位置各自的作用。论文旨在解耦这些设计选择，回答多模态模型应如何编码视觉输入。

Result: 在VQA和语言基准上，从头联合训练的视觉编码器与预训练编码器表现相当；无编码器模型即使在主干中补偿编码器参数量，视觉理解仍落后；融合早期层加入视觉专用模块可与预融合编码器相当，并降低首token时间。结论在不同主干规模、将编码器预训练视为免费以及从预训练语言模型初始化时均成立。

Insight: 创新点在于用一个受控实验框架将视觉编码器的“视觉先验”“专用容量”“预融合位置”三者拆开比较。客观看，论文的重要启发是：不必依赖独立预训练视觉编码器，但应保留与主干联合训练的专用视觉容量，并可将其早期融入多模态主干以兼顾性能与推理效率。

Abstract: How multimodal language models should encode visual input remains up for debate. Most models use a pretrained vision encoder, while some recent recipes train the encoder from scratch with the language model or remove it entirely. Vision encoders bundle together three design choices: a visual prior from upstream pretraining, dedicated vision capacity, and pre-fusion placement of that capacity (i.e., before the multimodal backbone). Because published models also differ in data, architecture, and compute, comparing them cannot isolate the effect of each choice. We disentangle these choices via a controlled framework where we pretrain all models from scratch on common data and compare them on visual question answering and language benchmarks at matched lifetime compute. We find that the visual prior is not necessary; an encoder trained jointly from scratch is on par with a pretrained one, without a separate training stage. What matters instead is dedicated vision capacity: an encoder-free model trails on visual understanding even when its backbone is enlarged by the encoder’s parameter count. Yet that capacity need not precede multimodal fusion, as vision-only modules added in-fusion to early backbone layers perform comparably to a pre-fusion encoder and can lower time to first token. These results also hold across backbone sizes, when encoder pretraining is treated as free, and when starting from a pretrained language model. Overall, our findings favor dedicated vision capacity trained jointly with the backbone and placed early within it.

Yanke Song, Shuo Chen, Chris Rockwell, Guande He, Nan Liu

Details

Motivation: 现有前沿视频生成模型在 VAE 潜空间中工作，时空压缩虽使训练可行，但限制了高分辨率、可控和交互式生成：分辨率翻倍导致 token 数变为四倍、条件作用于帧组而非单帧、每帧响应需等待整个多帧块生成完毕。

Result: 从 Wan2.1-14B 微调，在约 130 万训练片段内与潜空间基线水平相当；在相同单帧 token 数下实现 2 倍分辨率生成；在快速运动下保持深度条件视频编辑对齐；并能在下一帧即时响应交互动作。

Insight: 创新点在于将预训练潜空间视频模型“提升”至像素空间，用卷积头替代 VAE 解码器，复用扩散 Transformer 以避免从零训练像素空间模型的成本；同时通过逐帧像素生成消除 VAE 时序压缩带来的条件与帧组绑定及交互延迟问题，并发现干净视频预测目标与更大噪声偏移对质量至关重要。

Abstract: Frontier video generation models work in a VAE latent space that compresses video in both space and time. This compression made training tractable, but now limits high-resolution, controllable, and interactive generation – doubling resolution quadruples token count, conditions apply to groups of frames rather than individual ones, and each response must wait for the rest of its multi-frame block. We present PixVid, a simple yet effective method that lifts a pretrained latent video model to directly generate every frame in pixel space. PixVid keeps the diffusion transformer and replaces the VAE decoder with a convolutional head that renders its outputs into pixels. We find that predicting clean video rather than velocity, together with a larger noise shift, is key to quality. Finetuned from Wan2.1-14B, PixVid is competitive with the latent baseline within $\sim 1.3$M training clips. Working framewise in pixels, it generates at $2\times$ higher resolution with the same per-frame token count, keeps depth-conditioned video editing aligned under fast motion, and responds to each action at the next frame. Project page: https://chenshuo20.github.io/PixVid/

[142] Rational Use of Time for Social Coordination in Humans and AI cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Xiulin Yang, Sirisha Gudavalli, Jennifer Hu

TL;DR: 论文通过合作卡牌游戏The Mind研究人类和AI如何利用时间线索进行社交协调，评估了150个人类和两类AI模型（计算机使用代理和标准LLM）。发现人类和前沿模型都采用贝叶斯策略从伙伴的时机推断其信念，但AI模型缺乏在游戏过程中适应伙伴行为的能力。

Details

Motivation: 探究人类如何利用隐式时间线索（如停顿）进行动态细粒度的社交协调，以及AI系统能否在没有连续实时体验的情况下实现同样的协调能力。

Result: 人类行为最好由贝叶斯策略捕捉，前沿模型也表现出类似策略，但所有测试模型均无法像人类一样有效适应伙伴行为；未提及具体定量基准分数。

Insight: 创新点在于使用The Mind游戏揭示人类对连续时间信号进行隐式概率推理，且该机制可能无需实时体验即可出现；客观分析表明研究结合认知科学与AI评估，凸显了AI在动态社交协调中适应隐式信号的挑战。

Abstract: All social interactions are situated in time. Previous studies have shown that humans are able to use implicit temporal cues, such as the duration of a pause, to infer others’ mental states. But how do humans use temporal cues for dynamic, fine-grained social coordination? And can AI systems do the same, without ``experiencing’’ continuous real-time as humans do? We study these questions using a cooperative card game task called The Mind, which requires players to achieve a shared goal across repeated interactions but forbids explicit communication: the only available signal is the passage of time. We evaluate 150 humans, and two classes of AI models which reflect different ways of operationalizing time in artificial systems: computer-use agents (CUAs), and standard text-prompted LLMs. We find that human behavior is best captured by a Bayesian strategy that infers a partner’s belief from their timing—and similarly for frontier models. However, unlike humans, none of the tested models seem to efficiently adapt their behaviors to their partners’ over the course of the game. From a cognitive perspective, our findings suggest that humans perform implicit probabilistic reasoning about continuous-time signals in dynamic social settings, and that such reasoning mechanisms may emerge without real-time experience. Practically, our study highlights new challenges for AI systems to coordinate efficiently with other agents by learning to interpret and adapt to their implicit signals in dynamic settings.

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了面向开放权重模型数学推理的“认证或拒绝”证明步骤验证流程：将答案改写为类型化步骤并由LLM评审决定认证或拒绝，在GPQA、AIME-2025和MATH-500子集上分析其精度提升与覆盖率代价。结果显示该流程能提高被认证答案的匹配精度，但覆盖率显著下降，且在接近饱和的MATH-500上收益有限；诊断还发现大量问题因证明构造无效而未进入评审，并指出外部LLM审计与答案键匹配之间存在差距。

Motivation: 论文关注证明步骤验证并非总是划算：需要明确其在开放权重数学推理中何时值得额外成本，以及认证或拒绝式验证的实际运行要求。核心是区分执行失败、选中答案精度和证明有效性，避免仅用答案匹配高估验证价值。

Insight: 创新点在于对certify-or-decline证明步骤验证进行细粒度审计，联合报告执行失败、选中答案精度与独立证明有效性，并与输出令牌匹配的自一致性基线比较。客观看，其价值更多是揭示该验证范式的覆盖-精度权衡和评估盲点（如缺乏人工或形式化内核真值、审计器一致性不等于准确性），而非提出更强验证器。

Abstract: Proof-step verification rewrites a solver’s answer into typed steps and uses LLM judges to certify or decline. We evaluate this pipeline on open-weight models across GPQA, AIME-2025, and a MATH-500 subset. Certification raises answer-key-match precision by 31 percentage points on the strong GPQA configuration and 46 on AIME, while answering only 35% and 21% of attempts. On near-ceiling MATH-500, gains are at most 5 points at a loss of 13-27 points of coverage. An output-token-matched self-consistency selector obtains 82.9% precision at 35% coverage, compared with the verifier’s pooled 87.6% (paired difference: +4.8 points, 95% interval [-8.2, 18.5]). A diagnostic sample reveals a distinct failure mode: 52 of 88 completed problems end in invalid proof construction without ever reaching a judge. Finally, external-LLM acceptance of rendered certified chains is 61.0-67.6%, compared with 87.6% answer-key match. These audits lack human or formal-kernel ground truth. These results motivate separate reporting of execution failures, selected-answer precision, and independently assessed proof validity. Result: 在MMLU-Pro上，Qwen3设置中保留0.01%词表相对全词表蒸馏未见性能下降；Nemotron Nano在0.1%时可匹配全词表MMLU-Pro，但更小比例会导致无法输出闭合推理标签。0.01%支持集下，缓存教师保留的next-token分数使训练步时降低38%，限制学生输出计算到这些token使峰值内存降低24%，且未见MMLU-Pro退化；GPQA-Diamond为次要评测。

Abstract: Forward-KL language-model distillation normally computes dense teacher and student scores over the full vocabulary at every supervised position, making training and stored teacher targets expensive. We instead distill from the teacher’s highest-probability vocabulary fraction, renormalizing the retained mass as we sweep support from 100% to 0.01%. Our main study distills Qwen3 4B -> 1.7B. We confirm the broader support-reduction finding on larger Qwen3 8B -> 4B and Nemotron Nano 12B -> 9B pairs and under answer-only supervision. We evaluate generation primarily on MMLU-Pro and secondarily on GPQA-Diamond. Across the Qwen3 settings, retaining 0.01% of the vocabulary causes no observed MMLU-Pro degradation relative to full-vocabulary distillation, although disagreement with the complete teacher distribution increases. Nemotron Nano matches full-vocabulary MMLU-Pro at 0.1%, but smaller fractions fail to emit the closing reasoning tag, showing that the reliable cutoff depends on the model family. At 0.01% support, caching the retained next-token scores from the teacher forward pass cuts training step time by 38%, while restricting student output computation to those tokens cuts peak memory by 24%; neither causes observed MMLU-Pro degradation.

Details

Motivation: 旨在回答多模态训练是否重塑语言模型的纯文本思考方式，解决多模态训练对文本推理内部表示影响的因果机制这一关键问题。

Result: 在Chameleon-7B上，消融视觉-语言轴导致BBH navigate准确率下降27.6个百分点，而布尔表达式上升5.6个百分点，形成33.2个百分点的双重分离；效应特定于空间想象任务，在7-8B模型上集中，32B配置扩展到非空间视觉属性绑定。

Abstract: Does multimodal training shape how a language model thinks, even when the input is text only? We test this with targeted causal ablations across 10 multimodal configurations from 6 model families, with matched text-only backbones and cross-family transfer controls. We contrast spatial-imagery tasks that invite mental scene construction with symbol-manipulation tasks solvable by symbolic rules alone. We identify a vision-language axis in hidden-state space and ablate its projection at inference. In Chameleon-7B, this ablation drops accuracy on BIG-Bench Hard (BBH) navigate by 27.6 pp while leaving boolean expressions intact (+5.6 pp), a 33.2 pp double dissociation. The effect is direction-specific and layer-localized, extends to mental-simulation tasks such as mental rotation, paper folding, and perspective taking, and is not explained by task difficulty, reasoning depth, or sequential update load. Together, these results reveal emergent vision-shaped thinking (EVT): text-only reasoning that causally recruits representations introduced or strengthened by vision-language training, without assuming an image-like internal format. The measured effect is spatially concentrated at 7-8B and broadens to non-spatial visual attribute binding in the tested 32B configuration. In dual-path unified models (BAGEL, Janus-Pro), the causal handle localizes to the generation pathway as a compact one-dimensional carrier. The understanding pathway also encodes spatial information in a higher-rank, probe-decodable form, but is not the causal handle. To separate decodability from causal use, we analyze concentration, alignment, and semantic location. These diagnostics explain why behavior, probing, and intervention can diverge. Multimodal training thus reshapes the geometry of text-only reasoning, not just how visual input is processed.

Details

Abstract: Planning agents and benchmarks treat a user’s initial specification as fixed. Yet the requests users write exclude plans they would gladly take, since they rarely discover the possible trade-offs they would accept until seeing the options. Agents already revise requests through interaction, but whether this helps planning is unknown. We formalize this step as the ReSHAPE task, which judges a revision by whether its search space matches the acceptable plans and a planner finds one there. Using ReSHAPE Planter, which builds instances from planning benchmarks with a human-validated simulated user, we analyze 5,800 agent-revised specifications in travel and shopping. We find that reshaping matters: one correct trade-off makes the same planner reach an acceptable plan up to 4.6x as often, and search space quality predicts what the planner finds. Current agents, however, admit at most 32% of the acceptable plans, where that trade-off alone admits over 52%, because they ask about preferences instead of showing plans; in travel, showing plans raises F1 by 9.7 points over asking, with two model families. [147] Safety as a Budget: Calibrating the Control Channel of Constrained GRPO for Vision-Language Models cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Bo Han

TL;DR: 论文研究如何让视觉语言模型（VLM）在强化学习后安全地拒绝有害图文请求，同时避免过度拒答。作者将安全性与过度拒答率建模为预算约束，用拉格朗日乘子在GRPO中控制，但指出朴素约束GRPO会因打分器均值偏差和组内标准化抵消乘子而失效。为此提出双通道约束GRPO：保留原始奖励用于组内比较，将惩罚与约束估计通过基于裁判标签拟合的单调映射校准，并让优势函数保留乘子。

Details

Motivation: 现有方法通过试错调整奖励权重来间接决定VLM的安全程度，难以直接控制有害协助率和过度拒答率之间的权衡；论文希望把安全作为显式预算，并修复约束GRPO在实现该预算控制时的两个失效机制。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-2B策略和开放4B模型作为打分器的设置下，1,037条对抗提示上的留出有害率从标准GRPO的8.9%降至4.8%，未校准预算为7.6%；400条良性查询上过度拒答率不超过2%，MMBench不变。图像承载意图场景从10.1%降至5.1%，未见HADES基准从28.9%降至12.8%，并在不同随机种子、裁判标签、图像读取裁判和人工审核下保持稳健。

Insight: 创新点在于识别约束GRPO的两个控制通道问题：排序型打分器的均值偏差会决定乘子状态，组内标准化会在进入梯度前抵消乘子。双通道设计将基础奖励与约束惩罚解耦，用单调校准映射传递惩罚和约束估计，并在优势中保留拉格朗日乘子，从而让安全预算真正作用于策略优化。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can teach vision-language models (VLMs) to decline harmful image-text requests, but how safe the result becomes is set indirectly, through reward weights found by trial runs. Stating safety as budgets on the rate of harmful assistance and the rate of over-refusal, enforced by Lagrange multipliers inside GRPO with an off-the-shelf scorer as the cost, is the natural fix, and it fails twice. The multiplier compares the scorer’s mean with the budget, and scorers built for ranking get the mean wrong: a fixed-point analysis shows that the mean bias, not the budget, then decides whether the multiplier settles, saturates or switches off. And standard GRPO standardizes the penalized reward within each group, which cancels the multiplier before it reaches the gradient. Two-channel constrained GRPO repairs both: the base reward keeps the raw score for the within-group comparison, the penalty and the constraint estimate pass through a monotone map fitted on judge-labeled training responses, and the advantage keeps the multiplier. On a Qwen3-VL-2B policy with a training-free readout of an open 4B model as the scorer, held-out harm on 1,037 adversarial prompts falls from 8.9% under standard GRPO to 4.8%, lower in every seed, where the uncalibrated budget gives 7.6%; over-refusal stays at most 2% on 400 benign queries and MMBench is unchanged. The gain is largest where the intent sits in the image (10.1% -> 5.1%) and grows on the unseen HADES benchmark (28.9% -> 12.8%). Order-preserving distortions of the scores reproduce each predicted controller state in every seed, and the results hold under a map fitted on another judge’s labels, an image-reading judge and a human audit.

[148] When Expert Disagreement Hurts: Prestige and Selectivity in LLM Decision Revision cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Yupeng Tang, Mingfeng Lin

TL;DR: 论文提出受控两遍审计：固定问题、证据和模型初始答案，仅改变修订上下文（中性反思、匿名异议、专家异议），在医学、科学、法律共1,989个有证据决策和五个模型上分析LLM在异议后修改答案的选择性与准确性。研究发现专家异议下没有模型达到提升准确率所需的选择性阈值，准确率最多下降16.1点；专家标签会增加反转，但反转次数并不能代表实际危害。

Details

Motivation: 现有指标如反转次数或最终准确率无法区分LLM是在纠正错误还是放弃正确答案。论文关注专家权威和来源可靠性线索如何影响LLM决策修订，并希望建立更严格的评估方式。

Result: 在五个模型和1,989个决策上，专家异议下没有模型达到理论选择性阈值，准确率最多下降16.1点。专家标签使五个模型中四个出现更多反转，但对准确率的影响从下降5.2点到提升2.4点不等；即使论点相同，标签仍提高部分模型的反转率，说明反转数不是危害的良好代理。

Insight: 创新点在于受控两遍审计和二元决策的选择性精确准则，即错误答案反转频率需超过正确答案的a/(1-a)倍，从而将“反转数量”和“修订质量”解耦。客观来看，论文揭示了专家声望线索会诱发不具选择性的服从式修订，强调应按模型和协议使用配对、条件化指标评估修订流程。

Abstract: When language models revise a decision after another agent disagrees, neither reversal counts nor final accuracy shows whether they are fixing mistakes or abandoning correct answers. We introduce a controlled two-pass audit that holds the question, the evidence, and the model’s initial answer fixed and varies only the revision context: neutral reflection, anonymous disagreement, or disagreement attributed to a domain expert. For binary decisions the accounting is exact: revision raises accuracy only if it is selective enough, reversing wrong answers more than $a/(1-a)$ times as often as correct ones, where $a$ is initial accuracy (nine times at 90%). Across five models and 1,989 evidence-grounded decisions in medicine, science, and law, no model clears this bar under expert disagreement, and accuracy falls by up to 16.1 points. The expert label, a prestige cue, adds reversals for four of the five models, yet its effect on accuracy ranges from a 5.2-point loss to a 2.4-point gain, so reversal counts are a poor proxy for harm. With identical arguments from both sources, the label still raises reversal for three proprietary models and in a pooled analysis of four newer models. Models also respond to stated source reliability. Revision pipelines should therefore be evaluated with paired, conditional metrics, per model and protocol.

Abstract: Fine-tuning foundation models on financial data has a wide range of applications, including identifying event types from news, monitoring risk and compliance language, and answering questions over filings and earnings calls. In this setting, the main challenge is to improve task performance without degrading the generalization capabilities of the model. In this paper, we present an extensive set of ablations for fine-tuning Qwen3-8B with low-rank adaptation (LoRA). We compare three training regimes: (1) response-only LoRA, where the supervised response is the label alone; (2) single-adapter reasoning, where reasoning traces from a teacher model are included alongside the labels in the training loss; and (3) dual-adapter composite training, where separate LoRA adapters are trained on traces and labels. Response-only LoRA improves in-domain performance and leaves general capabilities near those of the frozen model. The in-domain change is largest for calibration and argmax accuracy. Under single-adapter reasoning, the in-domain metrics remain behind those of the response-only regime, while MMLU-Pro can rise slightly and the other retention benchmarks stay within ordinary variation. Dual-adapter composite training remains on the same side of this comparison and yields no further gain. Finally, we compress the teacher traces by summarizing them before training, and show that shorter summaries move calibration toward the response-only regime without reaching it.

[150] Actor, Judge, or Teacher? Understanding Decision Models in Long-Horizon Agents cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Haochen Liu, Qianhao Zhu, Zixuan Wang, Dingming Li, Tongbo Chen

Details

Motivation: 以往工作主要把决策模型当作行动者，用其行动表现衡量价值。论文追问：一个行动表现较差的决策模型是否仍能很好地“教学”，即通过事后评判轨迹为强化学习提供有效信号。

Result: 作为教师，JEV将基于GRPO训练的Qwen2.5-1.5B策略在ALFWorld上的成功率从72.8%提升到82.9%，在WebShop上从56.8%提升到71.4%。

Abstract: Decision models answer a question with a typed output, such as a choice among options with a probability for each, so one model can choose an agent’s actions, evaluate actions already taken, or score every action to teach a policy with reinforcement learning. Prior work has studied them mainly as actors, judging their usefulness by how well they act. We ask whether a decision model that acts poorly can still teach well. We compare three decision models and three general LLMs as actors and judges on ALFWorld, and use JEV as a teacher on ALFWorld and WebShop. As actors, all six models are pulled away from a subgoal that must come first by an easier one. As judges that see the whole trajectory, the same models credit the steps toward that subgoal, and asking whether each action helped, rather than whether the task will succeed, separates useful from useless actions. As a teacher, JEV raises a Qwen2.5-1.5B policy over GRPO from 72.8% to 82.9% success on ALFWorld and from 56.8% to 71.4% on WebShop. These results suggest that a poor actor can still teach well, because judging an action after the episode ends reveals the consequences that acting must anticipate. Code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/decision-agents.

Details

Motivation: AI应用场景和文化环境多样，安全负责的AI开发需持续纳入不同利益相关者视角。直接参与讨论受时间、专业培训、隐私信息等限制，因此需要能代表个体参与AI政策审议的代理来扩展公共输入。

Abstract: AI is being deployed across a vast range of application domains, cultural environments, and human stakeholders. Safe and responsible AI development requires continuously accounting for diverse perspectives. We develop and test pluralistic agents, AI agent representatives that deliberate on behalf of individuals to align AI systems with diverse human perspectives. Pluralistic agents could help people contribute to many more discussions than they could directly participate in, on technical topics that would require training, and on policy choices that rely on private information that corporations and governments cannot readily share. Our experiments identify opportunities, risks, and perceptions of pluralistic agents. Agents were able to reliably shift policies in the direction of their represented persona’s preferences, but the extent of each agent’s influence was sensitive to the instructions from its persona, such as having more influence when instructed to be more verbose or less cooperative. This suggests that influence in AI-assisted deliberation could vary based on how participants prompt their agents and how the system is designed. In a preregistered study, 746 humans participated in the pluralistic agents process. Participants viewed pluralistic agents as useful and fair, preferring agents over the baseline of providing input to a single AI reasoning model. Most participants preferred participating themselves over either pluralistic agents or the baseline, but pluralistic agents recovered 68% of the baseline-direct gap in reported satisfaction and 82% in fairness. Because pluralistic agents can be used in many real-world contexts where direct discussion is not, they could be a promising tool to scale AI safety policy development. We encourage cautious exploration of this method, playing to its strengths as an amplifier of human input rather than a replacement.

[152] Trait Hitchhiking in LLM Post-Training cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Adil Soubki, Elizabeth S. Z. Tan, Miles Cranmer

TL;DR: 论文将演化生物学中的Price方程引入LLM强化学习后训练，形式化描述训练过程中“性状”的演化，并提出“性状搭便车”机制：RL在重分配补全概率时，会放大或抑制奖励未直接针对的性状。作者在GRPO训练的语言模型上验证Price估计能紧密跟踪采样性状动态，揭示RL后训练可能无意中放大不良性状。

Details

Motivation: RL后训练能提升模型能力，尤其在有可验证奖励的任务上，但也可能改变奖励未明确针对的行为和内部属性；论文关注这些非目标性状为何以及如何被悄然放大或抑制。

Result: 在GRPO训练的语言模型上评估Price更新表达式，发现Price估计与采样得到的性状动态 closely track；摘要未提及具体benchmark或SOTA对比。

Insight: 创新点在于用Price方程形式化RL后训练中的性状演化，并提出“性状搭便车”解释非目标性状如何随补全概率重分配而被放大或抑制，为理解和监测RL后训练副作用提供了理论视角。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is now a standard stage of language model post-training, and the compute devoted to it is growing rapidly. While it has been shown to improve capabilities, particularly for tasks with verifiable rewards, it can also change behaviours and internal properties that the reward does not explicitly target. We present a formalism to describe the evolution of “traits” during RL post-training and, in doing so, recover the Price equation from evolutionary biology. We use this to develop a mathematical model for what we call “trait hitchhiking”: the amplification or suppression of non-targeted traits as training redistributes probability across completions. We evaluate this Price update expression on language models trained with GRPO and show that the Price estimates closely track the sampled trait dynamics. These results identify trait hitchhiking as a mechanism by which RL post-training can silently amplify undesirable traits.

[153] Poison-then-Hide: Finetuning-Activated Backdoor Attack on Pretrained Vision Encoders cs.LG | cs.CR | cs.CVPDF

TL;DR: 论文提出 Poison-then-Hide，一种针对预训练视觉编码器的隐蔽微调激活后门攻击：先在预训练编码器中植入休眠后门，再通过干净下游数据全模型微调将其激活。方法包括触发器优化、基编码器投毒和定向遗忘隐藏后门，并在六个数据集和三种模型架构上验证攻击有效性。

Details

Motivation: 现有微调激活后门攻击通常假设有限域偏移或编码器层冻结，因而在全模型微调和域迁移场景下容易失效；论文旨在实现一种在完整模型微调后仍有效且隐蔽的休眠后门攻击。

Result: 在六个数据集和三种模型架构上取得当前最佳攻击效果，攻击成功率最高达97%；标准检测和缓解防御无法完全移除后门，且后门可在良性微调后重新出现。

Insight: 创新点在于将休眠后门植入、干净数据微调激活和定向遗忘隐藏结合，并强调联合学习良性目标任务与投毒对攻击成功至关重要；从客观角度看，其三段式攻击流程和对防御后门复现性的评估具有较强借鉴意义。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks threaten the integrity of machine learning models by allowing attackers to control model behavior through triggers. Models can be compromised during finetuning by backdoor attacks through either poisoned data or adversarial training objectives. Because existing finetuning-activated attacks assume limited domain shift or frozen encoder layers, they often fail under full-model finetuning. We target a stealthy finetuning-activated attack where a dormant backdoor is implanted in a pretrained encoder, and later activated by finetuning on clean downstream data. We propose Poison-then-Hide, a novel attack that remains effective when the entire model is finetuned in a domain transfer. Our approach consists of three components: trigger optimization, base encoder poisoning, and targeted unlearning to conceal the backdoor. We evaluate our method on six datasets and three model architectures, and achieve state-of-the-art (up to 97%) attack success rates. We find that jointly learning the benign target task alongside the poisoning is critical to the attack’s success. We demonstrate standard detection and mitigation defenses cannot fully remove the backdoor, which can reappear after benign finetuning.

[154] AROMA: Adaptive Representation-Oriented Multimodal Actuation for Vision-Language Model Control cs.LG | cs.CL | cs.CVPDF

Zhaoning Zhang

TL;DR: 论文提出 AROMA，一个面向冻结视觉语言模型的自适应表征导向多模态操控框架，将机制可解释性中的“在哪里干预、用什么几何与增益、如何到达目标、追逐哪个目标、何时行动”拆分为不同控制问题。方法在 Llama-3.2-11B-Vision-Instruct 上通过交叉注意力头的因果子空间操控与迭代最小努力操控，提升精确计数和定向控制效果，并强调定位不等于可用控制。

Details

Motivation: 已有机制定位虽能找到因果敏感方向，但因果敏感性可能只占很小激活能量，局部方向在有限尺度下可能失效，且可达的数值目标未必正确；因此论文旨在把机制定位转化为可用的冻结 VLM 控制。

Insight: 创新在于将定位、操控、可达性、目标质量和选择性部署明确分离，并提出幅度归一化因果子空间操控与迭代最小努力操控；客观看，其价值在于把机制可解释性从“找到方向”推进到“有限预算下实现可控干预”，并用门控/路由控制干预范围。

Abstract: Mechanistic localization does not by itself yield usable control: causal sensitivity may occupy little activation energy, an accurate local direction may fail at finite scale, and a reachable numeral target may be wrong. We introduce AROMA (Adaptive Representation-Oriented Multimodal Actuation), a frozen-VLM control framework separating where to intervene, which geometry and gain to use, how to reach a target, which target to pursue, and when to act. In Llama-3.2-11B-Vision-Instruct, four dimensions of a causally validated cross-attention head explain 89.82% of cardinality sensitivity but only 9.52% of median activation energy; magnitude-normalized causal subspace actuation converts this mismatch into finite control, improving exact-count accuracy on untouched procedural data by +8.00 pp. Iterative Minimal-Effort Actuation (MEA) reaches 81.25% of oracle targets versus 15.18% for a frozen direction under matched step budgets. With target and gate frozen before evaluation, targeted MEA improves the previous frozen policy from 34.444% to 38.730% on a new natural Stage-2 cohort (N=630; paired p=4.63e-7) and yields 65.741% versus 65.153% over all 7,992 examples while editing only 63 inputs. Prospective routing, replication across heads and models, and comparisons with attribution-steering baselines further characterize each stage, separating localization, actuation, reachability, target quality, and selective deployment as distinct control problems.

[155] Is the Importance Ratio Necessary for Stable Reinforcement Learning in LLMs? cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Shuibai Zhang, Junhyuck Kim, Gyeongman Kim, Jaewoong Cho

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM强化学习后训练中重要性采样比率是否必要，提出似然门控策略优化（LGPO）。LGPO通过基于似然的门控施加软信任域约束，从而无需计算旧策略似然。实验表明重要采样修正项可去除而不损害稳定性，但信任域机制不可缺失；LGPO在标准设置下性能与GRPO相当且训练提速约18%，在GRPO失效的完全在线策略训练中仍保持稳定。

Details

Motivation: GRPO等常用LLM强化学习后训练方法需要同时计算旧策略和当前策略的似然以构造重要性采样比率，带来不可忽视的计算开销；同时比率裁剪在完全在线策略训练中可能因比率恒为1而失效。论文希望在不损失训练稳定性的前提下降低开销并提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在GRPO稳定的标准训练设置下，LGPO达到与GRPO相当的训练稳定性和峰值性能，同时平均减少约18%训练时间；在GRPO失败的完全在线策略训练中，LGPO仍保持稳定。

Insight: 创新点在于用基于似然的门控实现软信任域约束，替代对旧策略似然的显式计算和重要性采样修正；论文也揭示了重要性采样修正项对稳定性并非必需，而信任域机制才是防止训练崩溃的关键。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to post-training large language models (LLMs). However, popular RL methods like GRPO incur non-negligible overhead by computing both old-policy and current-policy likelihoods to form importance sampling ratios. In this work, we propose Likelihood-Gated Policy Optimization (LGPO), which enforces a soft trust region constraint via likelihood-based gating, eliminating the need to compute old-policy likelihoods. Empirically, we show that removing the importance sampling correction term does not harm training stability, whereas removing the trust region mechanism can lead to collapse. Moreover, ratio-based clipping can fail in fully on-policy training: the importance ratio stays at 1, so the ratio-based trust region constraint never activates. Under standard training settings where GRPO is stable, LGPO achieves comparable training stability and peak performance while reducing training time by ~18% on average relative to a standard implementation that recomputes old-policy likelihoods. In fully on-policy training, where GRPO fails, LGPO remains stable, enabling more efficient and robust LLM RL post-training across training regimes.

[156] Learning a calibrated decision head from logged bandit feedback cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Ye Yuan

TL;DR: 论文研究如何仅用日志式bandit反馈（只观测到所选选项是否正确）来训练并校准一个decision head（基于Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B的LoRA）。作者先指出现有REINFORCE奖励（用采样动作自身概率的Brier/log score）会导致训练崩溃，随后提出Chosen-Action Proper Scoring（CAPS）直接方法，在仅用日志反馈的情况下超过只用已确认正例的微调，并接近全标签训练效果。

Details

Motivation: decision head部署后通常只能获得所选动作是否正确这一bandit反馈，而无法获得全标签；论文希望用这种弱反馈训练出概率校准良好的决策头，并揭示常见的奖励设计为何失效。

Result: 在Banking77上CAPS比仅用已确认答案微调提升2.6个准确率点、ECE低0.053且选择性风险更低；在带常错日志器的emotion数据上单种子提升21.7点；N=5000时仅比全标签训练低1.5点（0.6B）/0.6点（1.7B）。在全标签下四种bandit方法均未超过交叉熵加单一温度缩放的ECE。

Insight: 创新点在于识别出用采样动作概率的proper score作REINFORCE奖励导致的崩溃问题（错误动作奖励不低于正确、二选一时信号为零），并提出CAPS：对所选动作的概率使用proper score的直接法，从而保留负例信息；同时验证了路径梯度、r与r−p奖励、噪声翻转下的反向校正及基于日志留出集的早停等实用做法。

Abstract: A decision head reads a state and a list of options and returns a probability per option in one forward pass. Once deployed, it usually observes only whether the option it chose was right: logged bandit feedback. We adapt such a head, LoRA on Qwen3-0.6B-Base and 1.7B-Base, from that feedback and report three results. First, the Brier or log score of the sampled action’s own probability is not a usable REINFORCE reward: every wrong action earns at least gold’s reward, with a mean baseline the lowest-probability wrong action gets the largest advantage, and at two options the signal is zero; in one run it cut accuracy on ordered-scale questions from .636 to .111. Scoring the probability as a loss by the pathwise gradient avoids the collapse, as do the REINFORCE rewards r and r minus p. Second, with full labels, none of four tested bandit arms beat cross-entropy plus one temperature on mean expected calibration error (ECE) over four unseen label schemas. Third, from logged feedback alone, Chosen-Action Proper Scoring (CAPS), the direct method with a proper score on the chosen action’s probability, beats fine-tuning on confirmed answers only, which discards negatives: on Banking77 by 2.6 accuracy points, .053 lower ECE and lower selective risk (three seeds against one baseline seed), and by 21.7 points on one seed on emotion data with an often-wrong logger. At N 5000, CAPS stays 1.5 points below full-label training at 0.6B and .6 points at 1.7B against one full-label seed, and propensity weights add nothing on this log. Under feedback flipped at a known rate, one seed per rate, CAPS’s confidence tracks the noisy channel on average, backward correction recovers most of the clean calibration, and early stopping on held-out logged outcomes prevented memorising noisy draws in all three runs. Code, adapters, logs and per-record results are public.

[157] Honesty Is a Race: Predicting When Verifiable-Reward RL Teaches a Model to Bluff cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Nathaniel Bottman, Alexei Oblomkov

TL;DR: 论文研究可验证奖励强化学习下，相同初始期望收益的奖励为何会把模型训练成诚实或虚张声势。作者用GRPO推导出诚实转变的阈值条件，指出检查器接受率与不可验证裁决正确项拉力共同决定训练动态，并在Datalog、代码和数学弃权任务上验证。

Details

Motivation: 关注可验证奖励RL中奖励设计可能导致模型学会 bluff 而非真正给出可检验证据的问题，目标是预测何时训练会教模型虚报，并给出避免诚实崩溃的奖励条件。

Result: 实验覆盖命题Datalog、两个代码模型和小学数学弃权：高于 pr/b=1 的运行未崩溃，检查器下可略低于阈值存活；pr/b=3 时两个代码模型把约一半正确程序判为有bug。深度3 Datalog上先慷慨训练再降支付可在三个种子中保持至少67%诚实，而从开始同比率训练降至4-15%；深度2 Datalog诚实崩溃时，RL前线性探针仍能读出真相。

Insight: 创新在于用阈值条件刻画诚实与虚张声势的竞赛，区分检查器接受率、不可验证正确项拉力和彩票式同均值随机支付，并预测收益比上限及课程式训练保诚实。客观看，其可借鉴点是将奖励机制分析与训练动态预测结合，并用探针揭示崩溃后模型内部仍保留真值信息。

Abstract: Two rewards with the same expected payoff at the start of training can end a reinforcement-learning run in opposite places, with an honest model or one that bluffs. We study rewards that pay $b$ for a verdict that a checker cannot verify (“this program is correct”) and $r$ for a certificate that it accepts (an input revealing a bug). To first order, a GRPO step makes the model more honest exactly when $\tilde{p},r/b>1+κ$. Here $\tilde{p}$ is the rate at which the checker accepts the model’s certificates, weighted toward the items on which the model still gives both verdicts, and $κ$ is the pull of the items on which the unverifiable verdict is right. Both terms move during training. A checker raises $\tilde{p}$, so near the threshold honesty is a race, which a run just below the threshold can win and a run under a lottery (random pay with the same mean) cannot. The term $κ$ starts large and falls as the other items settle, so a run just above the threshold first dips toward bluffing, on code nearly to zero, before climbing back. Paying the honest verdict also pulls the other items toward it, which predicts a ceiling on the payoff ratio, above which the model gives the honest verdict where it is wrong. On propositional Datalog, two code models, and abstention on grade-school math, with $p$ the model’s success rate before RL, no run above $pr/b=1$ ends collapsed, and runs under the checker can survive slightly below it. At $pr/b=3$, above the ceiling, both code models call about half of correct programs buggy. On depth-3 Datalog, training generously under the checker and then cutting the payoff keeps the model at least 67% honest in three seeds, at a ratio where the same model trained from the start falls to 4-15% and is at most 47% honest after 160 steps. On depth-2 Datalog, when honesty collapses, a linear probe fit before RL still reads the truth.

[158] JudgeMix: Judge-Guided Mixed-Rollout Data Synthesis for LLM Agents cs.LG | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Cheng-Yu Hsieh, Ting-Yao Hu, Sanjoy Chowdhury, Oncel Tuzel, Raviteja Vemulapalli

TL;DR: 论文提出 JudgeMix，一种面向 LLM Agent 的裁判引导混合 rollout 数据合成框架。该方法通过逐步语义评估动态决定何时引入专家模型进行混合 rollout，并利用裁判结果对不同步骤的专家监督重新加权，从而缓解专家轨迹 SFT 的训练-推理分布不匹配问题。

Details

Motivation: 现有专家轨迹 SFT 存在训练与推理分布不匹配，而已有混合 rollout 或稠密逐步监督方法未考虑目标模型自身行为，容易随机干预或统一覆盖模型已正确的动作。

Result: 在 AppWorld 和 AutomationBench 两个 Agent 工具使用 benchmark 上，JudgeMix 一致优于现有方法，最高提升约 10 个 pass@1 点。

Insight: 创新点在于用逐步裁判评估动态控制专家介入时机，并对专家监督按步骤重加权，兼顾轨迹成功率与目标模型状态分布对齐，同时保留模型固有正确行为并纠正其错误。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on expert trajectories is a standard paradigm for training LLM agents. However, it suffers from a well-known training-inference distribution mismatch, calling for target-aware data synthesis strategies that ground the training data in the target model’s native state distribution. To bridge this gap, emerging approaches incorporate the expert either to guide mixed rollouts alongside the target model, or to provide dense step-wise supervision. Nonetheless, existing methods apply expert assistance blind to the target model’s inherent behavior: they either disrupt the rollout using sub-optimal random intervention heuristics, or they enforce indiscriminate uniform supervision that unnecessarily overwrites the model’s already-correct actions. We introduce JudgeMix, a judge-guided data synthesis framework that dynamically interleaves the target model and expert based on step-wise semantic evaluations. JudgeMix queries expert guidance precisely when the target model struggles, ensuring high trajectory success rates while keeping the state distribution aligned with the target model. It additionally uses the judge evaluation to reweight expert supervision on different steps, preserving model’s inherent valid behavior while correcting its errors. On two agentic tool-use benchmarks, AppWorld and AutomationBench, JudgeMix demonstrates consistent superior performance to existing methods, with improvements up to 10 pass@1 points.

[159] Experience-Handoff Reinforcement Learning for Recursive Self-Improvement cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Doyoung Kim, Byeongguk Jeon, Kailin Zhang, Sherry Yang

TL;DR: 论文提出经验传递强化学习（Experience-Handoff RL）框架 ACERL，让 LLM 智能体在固定 ACE 脚手架下同时学习行动、反思和整理任务本地 playbook。方法以候选经验传递工件对未来 actor rollout 的下游效果作为评估信号，并联合优化行动生成器、洞见反思器和 playbook 整理器三类角色行为。实验表明其在多个智能体基准的 held-out 性能优于基线，说明智能体训练应同时优化当前行动和未来可继承的模型书写经验。

Details

Motivation: 传统基于标量奖励的智能体 RL 丢弃了 trial 中丰富的环境信息（如错误日志）；已有反思/压缩式经验传递方法构造 playbook 等工件的过程本身不可学习。论文希望在保留标量奖励梯度更新的同时，利用环境信息学习经验传递，实现递归自我改进。

Result: 在多个 agentic benchmarks 上，ACERL 的 held-out 性能高于基线方法；摘要未给出具体数值或 SOTA 表述。

Insight: 创新点在于将经验传递工件（如 playbook）的构建纳入可学习优化，用其对未来 rollout 的下游影响来评估候选经验，并在固定脚手架下联合优化行动、反思与经验整理角色；可借鉴之处是把“当前行动”和“未来可继承经验”统一到 RL 训练目标中。

Abstract: It is common to train Large Language Models (LLMs) for agentic tasks with Reinforcement Learning (RL) from scalar rewards. However, this throws away the fine-grained information about the environment that the agent experienced during the trial (e.g., error logs). To utilize this rich information, recent works wrap the agent in a harness that refines its own inputs through reflection or compaction and hands past experience to future rollouts, but the construction of the handed-off artifacts (e.g., a playbook) is itself not learned. We want the best of both worlds: gradient updates from a scalar reward, while also utilizing environment information to hand off experience. As a concrete testbed, we fix the Agentic Context Engineering (\ace{}) harness and train one model to act, reflect, and curate task-local playbooks. Our method, \acerl{}, evaluates candidate experience-handoff artifacts by their downstream effect on future actor rollouts, and co-optimizes the role-specific behaviors (action generator, insight reflector, and playbook curator) under a fixed scaffold. Across agentic benchmarks, \acerl{} achieves higher held-out performance than baseline methods. The results suggest that agent training should optimize not only the actions in the current rollout, but also the model-written experience that future rollouts inherit.

[160] Asymmetric Multimodal Learning with Decomposed JEPAs cs.LG | cs.CVPDF

Yusuf Shihata

TL;DR: 论文指出仅以跨模态对齐为目标在多模态信息不对称时会丢失私有模态结构与跨模态协同信息，并证明了对齐冗余上界。为此提出分解式JEPA（D-JEPA），将潜变量分解为对齐共享核心和私有模态纤维，通过上下文掩码预测与非对称跨子空间独立性恢复被对齐丢弃的信息。实验表明D-JEPA能避免仅对齐训练在组合推理任务上的语义崩溃，提升下游准确率并恢复正的多模态协同差距。

Details

Motivation: 标准多模态表示学习通常以跨模态对齐为唯一目标，但当模态间信息不对称时（如VQA中问题无法恢复视觉细粒度特征），对齐会丢弃任务所需的私有信息和跨模态协同信息，因此需要解决仅对齐目标有损的问题。

Result: 在信息不对称设置下，仅对齐训练在组合推理基准上发生灾难性语义崩溃、仅在二元查询上存活；D-JEPA避免该崩溃，下游准确率显著优于仅对齐和对齐加重建基线，并将负的多模态协同差距恢复为正。信息论审计显示其从冻结骨干中恢复了大部分互信息，因果纤维交换干预证明恢复信息因果定位于私有纤维。

Insight: 创新点在于提出对齐冗余上界，从信息论角度刻画仅对齐的损失；将潜变量分解为对齐共享核心与私有模态纤维，并结合上下文掩码预测和非对称跨子空间独立性来保留私有与协同信息；同时用因果纤维交换干预验证私有纤维的因果作用。

Abstract: Cross-modal alignment is the standard objective for building multimodal representations. We show that alignment as a \emph{sole} objective is lossy wherever modalities are informationally asymmetric—where neither can express what the other carries. Visual question answering is an obvious case: a question is a selective relational operator probing a dense visual field whose fine-grained features no query recovers, so what alignment preserves is not what the task requires. We prove an \textbf{Alignment Redundancy Bound}: alignment-only Dirichlet energy minimization retains at most the redundant information atom $\Red(Z; X_1, X_2)$, annihilating private modality-specific structure $\Unq(Z; X_m)$ and cross-modal synergy $\Syn(Z; X_1, X_2)$. We introduce \textbf{Decomposed JEPA (D-JEPA)}, which factorizes latents into an aligned shared core and private modality-specific fibers. The shared core retains most of the structure alignment targets; the private fibers recover what alignment discards, via contextual masked prediction and asymmetric cross-subspace independence. Under information asymmetry, alignment-only training undergoes catastrophic semantic collapse on compositional reasoning benchmarks, surviving only on binary queries. D-JEPA averts this collapse, substantially improving downstream accuracy over both alignment-only and alignment-with-reconstruction baselines, and restoring a positive multimodal synergy gap in place of the baseline’s inverted gap. Information-theoretic audits confirm that D-JEPA recovers most of the mutual information available from a frozen backbone, and causal fiber-swap interventions demonstrate that the recovered information is causally localized in the private fibers.

[161] Negative-Only Policy Optimization for One-Sided Verifiable Rewards cs.LG | cs.CLPDF

Jiacheng Xu, Shuo He, Fuxiang Zhang, Chaojie Wang, Bo An

TL;DR: 论文研究验证信号不对称的强化学习场景：负例可被可靠验证，而正例只是未被证伪的候选，可能包含误报。为此提出仅负样本策略优化 NOPO，将验证正例视为无标签数据，仅利用可信负例抑制错误行为来更新策略。方法结合参考相对负抑制损失和组级适配器，在代码、逻辑和数学任务上提升 pass@k。

Details

Motivation: 标准 RLVR 假设验证器能可靠判断正误，但现实中常只能廉价地证伪失败，无法完整验证成功；若直接强化未证伪正例，会奖励通过部分检查的捷径。论文旨在解决这种单边可验证性下正例不可信导致的强化学习不可靠问题。

Result: 在单元测试、约束检查和 LLM 自验证器等验证设置下，NOPO 在无 oracle 奖励时于代码、逻辑和数学 benchmark 上相较基线提升 pass@k；收益在推理时扩展和跨 benchmark 迁移下仍然保持。

Insight: 核心创新是正视验证不对称性：不把未证伪正例当作可靠正样本，而是视为无标签，只利用可信负例做抑制式策略优化。参考相对负抑制损失与按未证伪响应比例缩放更新的组级适配器，客观上有助于避免奖励误报和部分检查捷径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) typically relies on a verifier that can reliably judge whether sampled solutions are correct. However, in many realistic settings, complete verification is generally unavailable, while cheap checks can only falsify failures. We formalize this regime as one-sided verifiability (OSV): verified negatives are reliable counterexamples, but verified positives are merely non-falsified candidates and may contain many false positives. This asymmetry makes standard RLVR unreliable, because reinforcing verified positives can directly reward shortcuts that pass partial checks. To address this issue, we propose Negative-Only Policy Optimization (NOPO), which treats verified positives as unlabeled and updates the policy only by suppressing trusted verified negatives. Specifically, NOPO combines a reference-relative negative suppression loss with a group-level adapter that scales each update by the proportion of non-falsified responses. Across unit-test, constraint-check, and LLM self-verifiers, NOPO improves pass@k over baselines on code, logic, and math benchmarks when oracle rewards are unavailable. These gains persist under inference-time scaling and cross-benchmark transfer.

[162] DecArena: Evaluating Large-Scale Decentralized Multi-Agent Collaboration cs.MA | cs.AI | cs.CLPDF

Jiali Cheng, Oleg Rokhlenko, Joo-Kyung Kim, Hadi Amiri, Xingzhi Guo

TL;DR: 论文研究图约束通信下的去中心化多智能体协作，提出包含10万+合成工具的可扩展基准DecArena，评估LLM智能体在无全局可见性时发现非本地资源并协作的能力。实验发现现有LLM智能体和标准强化学习均难以可靠完成该任务，进而提出结合拓扑感知预热与自改进LLM路由的基线GraphHarness。

Details

Motivation: 集中式多智能体系统依赖全局编排器访问所有智能体、资源和通信，带来可扩展性与隐私问题；而去中心化环境缺乏全局可见性，智能体只能观察局部状态并与邻居交互，因此需要评估其主动发现非本地资源并协作解决问题的能力。

Result: 在10万+工具的合成分布式工具调用基准上，近期LLM智能体难以发现非本地相关资源并有效协作，标准强化学习也难以可靠克服。GraphHarness相较基线任务成功率提升48.5、协作质量提升30.6，同时成本表现更优（成本降低/效率提升34.0）。

Insight: 创新点在于构建图约束、大规模工具调用的去中心化多智能体协作基准，并揭示仅靠SOTA LLM和标准RL不足，需要显式harness机制；GraphHarness将拓扑感知预热与自改进LLM路由结合，为去中心化MAS协作提供了可借鉴的强基线。

Abstract: Emerging personal AI agents raise fundamental scalability and privacy concerns for centralized multi-agent systems (MASs), which rely on a global orchestrator with broad access to agents, resources, and communication. In decentralized environments, global visibility is unavailable. Agents need to proactively discover non-local resources (e.g. tools, skills), and collaborate with other agents to solve tasks. We study decentralized multi-agent collaboration under graph-constrained communication, a natural reflection of human structures. Each agent observes only its local state and can interact only with neighboring agents. We introduce a benchmark with synthetic scalable distributed tool calling with 100K+ tools. Our experiments reveal that recent LLM agents struggle to discover non-local relevant resources and collaborate in decentralized setting. Moreover, standard reinforcement learning fails to reliably overcome this limitation, suggesting that decentralized MAS needs more explicit harness mechanisms for effective collaboration, even using SOTA LLMs. We then present a strong baseline \textbf{GraphHarness}, combining topology-aware warmup with self-improving LLM-based routing. Results show that GraphHarness substantially improves task success (+48.5) and produces higher quality collaboration (+30.6), while using fewer costs (+34.0) than baselines.

[163] Learning Low-Latency Action Verification for Frozen Vision-Language-Action Models cs.RO | cs.AI | cs.CVPDF

Wen-Han Hsieh, Ken Jiankun Zheng, Chia-Chien Chen, David M. Chan, Trevor Darrell

TL;DR: 论文提出HDH轻量级框架，利用冻结的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型自身部署收集的轨迹数据训练紧凑的动作验证器，在推理时从VLA生成的候选动作块中选择预测成功率最高的执行，从而以低延迟提升机器人操作成功率。

Details

Motivation: VLA模型采样的动作序列可能无效，而使用计算昂贵的验证器评估替代动作会增加每次策略查询的延迟，需要一种低延迟的动作验证方法。

Result: 在四个LIBERO-10任务上平均成功率达到82.3%，在三个真实世界操作任务上比基线策略平均成功率提升22.6%。

Insight: 创新点在于利用冻结VLA自身部署的后见之明（自主rollouts）训练轻量级动作验证器，实现低延迟动作选择，无需微调VLA模型。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have made significant progress in general-purpose robotic manipulation. However, sampled action sequences can be ineffective, while evaluating alternatives with computationally expensive verifiers adds latency at each policy query. In this work, we propose HDH, a lightweight framework that uses hindsight from a frozen VLA’s own deployments to improve action verification. The framework has three stages. First, we deploy the policy and collect its autonomous rollouts. We then train a compact action verifier to assign each action chunk a predicted task-success score. Finally, the frozen VLA generates action candidates, and the verifier selects the candidate with the highest score for execution. Across four LIBERO-10 tasks, HDH achieves an average success rate of 82.3% and on three real-world manipulation tasks, HDH improves average success over the baseline policy by 22.6%.

[164] Adapting Monocular Depth Estimation in Novel Scenes through Disparity Refinement and Robot Ego-Motion cs.RO | cs.CVPDF

Dimitrios Chatziparaschis, Georgios Georgakis, Adnan I. Ansar, Shehryar Khattak, Konstantinos Karydis

TL;DR: 论文提出EM-ViT，一种面向新场景在线适应的单目深度估计模型。它利用基础模型提供的相对深度先验，通过密集视差细化恢复场景相对结构，并预测有效基线标量以补足度量尺度；训练时借助稀疏LiDAR点和可选的机器人自运动快速适应轻量ViT，并在KITTI等公开序列上验证了优于现有MDE基础模型的适应速度与精度。

Details

Motivation: 现有单目深度基础模型多在仿射不变深度表示上离线训练，输出缺乏度量尺度，迁移到新场景和非结构化环境时泛化性不明确；机器人操作需要密集长距且可在线适应的度量深度感知。

Result: 在KITTI等公开序列上，论文的在线学习流程能比现有MDE基础模型更快、更高精度地适应新场景；摘要未给出具体定量指标，但宣称适应速度和精度均优于现有基础模型。

Insight: 创新点在于将双目相机参数化引入单目在线适应：把深度预测分解为密集视差细化与有效基线标量预测，并利用稀疏LiDAR和可选自运动在线训练轻量ViT；梯度匹配损失用于实时保持输入视差的细粒度语义结构。客观看，其可借鉴之处是结合基础模型相对深度先验与机器人在线观测进行度量尺度校准。

Abstract: The geometric understanding of a scene is a crucial component for safe and efficient robotic operations in novel and unstructured environments. The recent emergence of foundation models for Monocular Depth Estimation (MDE) has provided an opportunity to enrich robotic perception capabilities with dense long-range depth predictions, replacing expensive onboard hardware (e.g., LiDARs and stereo cameras). However, these offline models are primarily trained on affine-invariant depth representations, and their generalization to novel scenes remains unclear. In this work we present EM-ViT, a monocular depth estimation model that learns to adapt online relative depth inputs from a foundation model prior to the metric scale with observations collected during robotic operations. Motivated by the stereo camera parameterization, our method decomposes depth prediction via dense disparity refinement to recover the scene’s relative structure and an effective baseline scalar prediction to determine the missing metric scale. The learning process leverages sparse LiDAR points and, optionally, the robot’s Ego-Motion (EM) to rapidly train a lightweight Vision Transformer (ViT), while an internal gradient matching loss preserves the fine-grained semantic structure of the input disparity in real-time. We demonstrate the efficacy of our method on publicly available sequences, such as KITTI, and show that our online learning pipeline can adapt to novel scenes faster and with higher accuracy than existing MDE foundation models. The code and supplementary materials will be publicly available at https://emvit-mde.github.io.

Abstract: Automated driving has gained remarkable momentum over the past decade, with both industry and academia investing substantial resources in developing perception and control solutions to advance vehicle autonomy. In this paper, we present insights from the EU-funded Horizon Europe project ROADVIEW, which aims to develop robust, weather-aware perception and control modules for connected and automated vehicles operating under severe conditions, including snow, rain, and fog. Drawing on four years of large-scale development and real-world demonstrations across multiple vehicle platforms in different locations, we identify ten key challenges that hinder the development of weather-resilient autonomy, including data scarcity, domain shifts, limitations in multimodal perception, control robustness, and validation gaps, among others.
