[166] What Makes a VLA Reactive? A Study of Execution Strategies for Dynamic Manipulation cs.RO | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Zhuoyue Li, Zhendong Han, Pankhuri Vanjani, Wenting Li, Rudolf Lioutikov

TL;DR: 论文研究预训练VLA模型在动态操作中为何具有反应性，基于π0.5在兩個动态仿真基准和三个真实机器人任务上系统比较预测/执行时域、同步/异步推理、动作块连续性与策略自适应等策略。结论是最佳预测时域约为1秒，最佳执行时域依赖检查点；短时域更利于异步执行，保持轨迹连续比单纯降低延迟更有效。作者强调VLA的反应性同时取决于执行流水线和策略本身，应联合评估。

Details

Motivation: VLA模型通常一次预测未来动作块，但当物体移动时，动作块在执行前会过时，导致动态操作反应滞后。论文旨在厘清预训练VLA的反应性由哪些预测与执行策略决定，并解决动态场景下动作块失效的问题。

Result: 在兩個动态仿真基准和三个真实机器人任务上开展实证研究：最佳预测时域约为1秒，最佳执行时域随检查点变化；短时域在异步执行下收益更大，保持动作块连续性的方法优于仅降低延迟的方法；策略自适应可修复特定失败，但在传送带任务上调整执行时域的收益与之相当。

Insight: 创新点在于系统拆解并联合评估VLA的预测时域、执行时域、同步/异步推理、动作块连续性和策略自适应等执行策略，而非只改进策略网络。客观看，其价值在于揭示动态操作中“执行流水线”与“策略”同等重要，为VLA反应性设计提供了可操作的实证规律。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions, and when objects move, those chunks go stale before they are executed. We study what makes a pretrained VLA reactive, using \pihalf{} on two dynamic simulation benchmarks and three real-robot tasks. We vary the prediction and execution horizons, compare synchronous and asynchronous inference, test methods that deliver actions sooner or keep successive chunks continuous, and adapt the policy with motion cues and latent-space reinforcement learning. The best prediction horizon is about one second, and the best execution horizon depends on the checkpoint. Short horizons help more with asynchronous execution, and methods that keep the trajectory continuous outperform those that only reduce latency. Adapting the policy repairs targeted failures, but on the conveyor task changing the execution horizon gained as much. Reactivity depends on the execution pipeline as much as on the policy, so both should be evaluated together.

Details

Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要依赖2D视觉观测和语言指令，场景几何与障碍关系需要隐式推断，在杂乱环境中容易因小空间误差导致碰撞或意外接触。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong performance in robotic manipulation, yet most rely primarily on 2D visual observations and language instructions, leaving scene geometry and obstacle relationships to be inferred implicitly. This limitation is particularly problematic in cluttered environments, where small spatial errors can lead to unintended contacts or collisions. We propose MOSAIC, a modular spatial- and obstacle-aware framework that can be integrated with pretrained VLA policies to improve object-level scene understanding and action generation. Our method augments the VLA backbone with an auxiliary semantic grounding task that predicts role-specific masks for the target object, destination region, and surrounding obstacles. We further combine the target and destination masks with depth estimated from 3D scene reconstruction to construct role-specific geometric tokens that are injected directly into the action expert. In addition, we propose obstacle-aware attention supervision that emphasizes obstacles near the robot body during task execution. The resulting attention features are subsequently injected into later action-expert layers to further guide action generation. Together, these components provide explicit semantic, geometric, and obstacle-aware cues while preserving the underlying pretrained VLA architecture. We evaluate our framework on pretrained VLA model using the RoboPRO benchmark, including visual perturbation tests, and further validate it on real-world manipulation tasks. MOSAIC improves the success rate by 5.1% in cluttered scenes, and by 9.7% under visual perturbations, with gains of up to 25.0% in the most challenging environment. We will release our code and dataset upon acceptance at the following Github page: https://mmahdavian.github.io/MOSAIC/

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have become a powerful foundation for robotic scene understanding, task planning, and language-conditioned control. A natural question follows: can these models also decode what a human operator intends while teleoperating a robot, without any explicit instruction? We formulate this as hierarchical intent prediction, jointly inferring the ongoing atomic action and the broader mission it serves from a short visual teleoperation history. To study this, we introduce VI AMO (Vision Intention Action and Mission Ongoing), a dataset of 6,600 episodes from eleven robot sources with 13,653 temporally aligned action annotations and 9,702 mission annotations. Large proprietary models struggle with this problem: on held-out episodes, GPT-4o reaches Sentence-BERT similarities of only 0.563 and 0.470 for action and mission, and GPT-5.6 Luna achieves only 0.581 and 0.554. Fine-tuning a VLM on VI AMO yields our Vision-Intention Model (VIM), which raises these scores to 0.825 and 0.821 and still reaches 0.726 and 0.717 on the 35.4% of test actions with both labels absent from training. Finally, as a downstream application, VIM’s predicted mission enables a vision-language-action (VLA) policy to take over from the operator mid-task in a real-world environment unseen during VIM fine-tuning, reducing human teleoperation time by 34.6% relative to GPT-4o and 39.8% relative to GPT-5.6 Luna, while cutting intent-prediction latency by roughly 70% on average.

Abstract: Language-guided UAV navigation has largely focused on long-horizon route planning, leaving short-range, object-relative flight maneuvers underexplored. Flow encompasses both primitive motion commands and complex object-interactive behaviors, assessing motion intent comprehension and spatial context grounding. To enable policy learning and closed-loop evaluation, we introduce UAV-Flow, a comprehensive dataset comprising 30,692 expert-piloted real-world trajectories across eight distinct motion categories, alongside 10,109 simulation trajectories in the UAV-Flow-Sim environment equipped with task-specific completion criteria. Furthermore, we develop FlowVLA, which undergoes imitation pretraining on mixed real-sim demonstrations and online post-training via Tail-Aware GRPO (TGRPO). Details

Motivation: 无人机视觉语言导航中，三维运动预测的小误差会导致导航失败或碰撞；现有方法难以预判执行结果，难以从语言指令准确预测导航waypoint，因此需要增强视觉上下文与空间动作输出之间的对齐。

Abstract: Vision language navigation (VLN) is very challenging for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) since small errors in 3D motion prediction can lead to navigation errors and collisions. Unable to fathom execution outcomes in 3D environments, existing methods often struggle to predict accurate navigation waypoints from language instructions. To solve this, we introduce the Prototypical Visual Imagination framework, shortened as ProtoVis-Nav, which forces the model to imagine an outcome-associated local visual representation for the intended next UAV movement. Specifically, we predict feature prototypes and use their linear combination to estimate the visual feature of the target region. In this manner, the VLN model first imagines the local visual representation of the next intended location, and then uses predicted prototypes as context for generating the final action, promoting alignment between visual context and waypoint outputs. [172] Aggregate Accuracy Hides Diagnosis-Specific Failure in Medical Vision-Language Models eess.IV | cs.CL | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

TL;DR: 论文系统审计了五个开源医疗视觉语言模型（MedGemma-4B、Lingshu-7B、HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B、Qwen2.5-VL-7B、MedVLM-R1）在按性别、年龄、病灶类型和标签平衡的 6,048 道 ChestX-ray14 题目上的表现，发现“平均准确率”会掩盖模型在单一诊断上的彻底失败：HuatuoGPT-Vision 与 Qwen2.5-VL 在 432 例气胸阳性 X 光片上检出为零（平衡准确率 0.5，等同随机），却仍能流利地描述气胸征象再予以否认。作者进一步通过提示格式、分辨率、模型规模、独立数据集与内部表征探针等控制实验，证明这并非数据或视觉能力缺失，而是内部已编码、输出端被抑制的系统性失败。

Details

Motivation: 当前医疗视觉语言模型主要以跨病灶平均的准确率进行比较，这种聚合指标可能完全掩盖模型在某一具体诊断上的零召回，从而高估模型的临床可用性，论文希望揭示并量化这一评测盲区。

Result: 在平衡后的 ChestX-ray14 上，同一批气胸图像上 MedGemma 与 Lingshu 分别检出 38% 和 67%，而 HuatuoGPT-Vision 与 Qwen2.5-VL 对全部 864 例气胸问题一律回答“无”（平衡准确率 0.500）；该现象在四种提示格式、原生分辨率、单标签样本、模型放大至 72B（仅 4% 检出）后依然存在，并在带放射科医生勾画掩膜的独立数据集 SIIM-ACR 上复现（432 例确诊气胸检出 0，其中最大的 144 例也全部漏检）。

Insight: 创新点在于提出“逐病灶召回/特异度＋平衡样本”的评测范式，并给出“模型能描述征象却输出否认、不弃权”这一失败模式；客观来看，其最有价值的发现是借助 logit 与隐状态分析证明失败模型对气胸其实高度自信且线性探针可从隐藏表示中恢复该诊断，即信息在内部存在但在输出层被抑制，这提示问题根源更可能在解码/对齐阶段而非视觉编码器。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) are compared mainly by accuracy averaged across findings, a summary that can hide complete failure on a single diagnosis. We audit five open medical VLMs (MedGemma-4B, Lingshu-7B, HuatuoGPT-Vision-7B, Qwen2.5-VL-7B, and MedVLM-R1) on 6,048 ChestX-ray14 questions balanced by sex, age, finding, and label. On the 432 pneumothorax-positive X-rays, HuatuoGPT-Vision and Qwen2.5-VL detect zero cases while MedGemma and Lingshu detect 38% and 67% of the same images. The two failing models answer “no” to all 864 pneumothorax cases (balanced accuracy 0.500, chance), yet produce fluent, anatomically specific explanations that name the signs of a collapsed lung and then deny them, with no hedge or abstention. This failure is not an artifact of prompting, image resolution, label ambiguity, or model scale. It persists across four prompt formats, at native resolution, and on single-label cases. A within-model control shows the same forced prompt gives HuatuoGPT 79% recall on nodule but only 6% on pneumothorax. It survives a tenfold scale increase (Qwen2.5-VL-72B detects 4%) and replicates on SIIM-ACR, an independent dataset with radiologist-drawn masks, where the failing models detect 0 of 432 confirmed pneumothoraces, including 0 of the 144 largest lesions. Reading the models’ logits and internal states shows this is not blindness. The two failing models are highly confident yet no better than chance and a linear probe recovers pneumothorax from their hidden representations even where recall is zero, so the finding is represented internally but suppressed at the output. Aggregate accuracy therefore cannot establish that a medical VLM can see a given finding, because a model that misses one can still describe it convincingly. Reporting per-finding recall and specificity on balanced cases is needed to tell the difference.

[173] Supervision-Aware Evaluation of MedGemma for Reference-Assisted Radiotherapy Contour Ranking eess.IV | cs.CV | cs.LGPDF

Jaden Moon, Caiwen Jiang, Songlin Zhao, Wei Liu

TL;DR: 论文对冻结的MedGemma 1.5在放疗轮廓排序任务中进行监督感知评测，比较最终提示状态、yes/no输出概率、投影视觉token、结构化面板特征和解剖条件读出等不同表征读出方式在HaN-Seg留出集、交叉拟合及SegRap2023迁移上的表现，强调医学影像VLM评估需控制监督信号与表征访问差异。

Details

Motivation: VLM在放疗轮廓排序质量保证中的性能增益可能来自监督方式、评分构造、可用输入或表征访问，而非模型本身能力，因此需要更公平、可解释的评测。

Result: 在12例HaN-Seg留出集上，最终提示状态的监督读出比yes/no输出概率读出高+0.3047 pooled AP（95%区间[+0.2822,+0.3269]），且优于参数量匹配的非线性对照；投影视觉token读出在交叉拟合和SegRap迁移中比最终状态读出高+0.0706 AP（[+0.0607,+0.0804]），结构化面板特征具竞争力，解剖条件读出未显优势；结果为计算/合成指标，非临床可接受性。

Insight: 创新点在于提出监督感知的医学VLM评测框架，系统比较多种读出与对照，揭示表征访问和监督方式会显著影响结论；可借鉴视觉token与结构化特征控制、参数匹配对照及跨数据集迁移验证。

Abstract: Radiotherapy contour ranking can support quality assurance, but vision-language model (VLM) gains may reflect supervision, score construction, available inputs, or representation access. We evaluated frozen MedGemma 1.5 in a single-institution study, a 12-patient held-out HaN-Seg test, five-fold cross-fitting on 30 non-test HaN-Seg patients, and transfer without refitting to 120 SegRap2023 patients. On held-out HaN-Seg, a supervised readout of the final prompt state exceeded a supervised readout of the model’s yes/no output probabilities by +0.3047 pooled average precision (AP; 95% patient-bootstrap interval [+0.2822, +0.3269]); the advantage persisted against a parameter-count-matched nonlinear control. A readout of projected visual tokens exceeded the final-state readout in cross-fitting and transfer (+0.0706 AP averaged equally across patients on SegRap; 95% interval [+0.0607, +0.0804]), while a structured panel-feature readout remained competitive with visual tokens. The anatomy-conditioned readout did not demonstrate an advantage over the native-feature comparator. These results motivate visual-token and structured-feature controls in medical-imaging VLM evaluations; outcomes were computational or synthetic, not clinical acceptability.
